1) NEW FILE: server/realtime/liveUpdates.ts (FULL FILE)
Create folder server/realtime/ and add:

import type { Server as HttpServer, IncomingMessage } from "http";
import type { RequestHandler } from "express";
import { WebSocketServer, type WebSocket } from "ws";
import Database from "better-sqlite3";

/**
 * Live Updates (WebSocket + Event Log)
 *
 * Baseline guarantees:
 * - One WS endpoint (/ws) used by web today and native later.
 * - Server can broadcast "facts" (events) derived from the authoritative engine/DB.
 * - Clients may miss WS events (mobile backgrounding, flaky networks), so we persist
 *   low-volume events into sqlite (realtime_events) and expose cursor-based catch-up.
 *
 * Design choices:
 * - We DO NOT persist high-frequency tick data (quotes) in realtime_events.
 * - We DO persist user-impacting deltas (trade lifecycle, account snapshots, order stages).
 */

export type LiveUpdateEvent = {
  type: string;
  cursor?: number;
  [k: string]: any;
};

export type LiveUpdatesInitOptions = {
  httpServer: HttpServer;
  sessionMiddleware: RequestHandler;
  /**
   * Optional token authenticator for WS messages.
   * Should resolve to a userId or null.
   */
  authenticateToken?: (token: string) => Promise<number | null>;
  /**
   * Optional hook to validate that a sessionId is still active (revocation check).
   * If provided, it will be used on connect and on explicit auth.
   */
  isSessionActive?: (sessionId: string) => Promise<boolean>;
};

type ClientMeta = {
  userId: number | null;
  authedVia: "session" | "token" | null;
  sessionId: string | null;
  scopes: Set<string>;
  connectedAtMs: number;
};

const DEFAULT_SCOPES_PUBLIC = ["quotes"];
const DEFAULT_SCOPES_AUTHED = ["quotes", "trades", "account", "audit"];

function safeJsonParse(input: any): any | null {
  try {
    const s = Buffer.isBuffer(input) ? input.toString("utf8") : String(input);
    return JSON.parse(s);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function wsReady(ws: WebSocket): boolean {
  return ws.readyState === ws.OPEN;
}

function inferChannelFromType(type: string): string {
  if (!type) return "misc";
  if (type.startsWith("quotes:") || type.startsWith("quote:")) return "quotes";
  if (type.startsWith("trades:") || type.startsWith("trade:")) return "trades";
  if (type.startsWith("order:") || type.startsWith("orders:")) return "trades";
  if (type.startsWith("account:")) return "account";
  if (type.startsWith("audit:") || type.startsWith("admin:")) return "audit";
  return "misc";
}

function ensureRealtimeEventsTable(db: Database.Database) {
  db.exec(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS realtime_events (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      user_id INTEGER,
      scope TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USER',
      event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
      payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
      created_at_ms INTEGER NOT NULL
    );
  `);
  db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_realtime_events_user_id_id ON realtime_events(user_id, id);`);
  db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_realtime_events_scope_id ON realtime_events(scope, id);`);
  db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_realtime_events_event_type ON realtime_events(event_type);`);
}

function openEventDb(): Database.Database {
  const db = new Database("./trading_app.db");
  try {
    db.pragma("journal_mode = WAL");
    db.pragma("synchronous = NORMAL");
  } catch {
    // Safe to ignore if pragmas fail
  }
  ensureRealtimeEventsTable(db);
  return db;
}

export type LiveUpdates = {
  publishGlobal: (event: LiveUpdateEvent, opts?: { persist?: boolean; eventType?: string }) => number | null;
  publishToUser: (userId: number, event: LiveUpdateEvent, opts?: { persist?: boolean; eventType?: string }) => number | null;

  // Compatibility shims (keep current web app behavior)
  publishQuotesUpdated: () => void;
  publishTradesUpdated: (userId: number) => void;
  publishAccountUpdated: (userId: number) => void;

  // Cursor catch-up
  getLatestCursorForUser: (userId: number, includeGlobal?: boolean) => number;
  fetchEventsSince: (
    userId: number,
    cursor: number,
    limit: number,
    includeGlobal?: boolean
  ) => { nextCursor: number; events: LiveUpdateEvent[] };
};

export function initLiveUpdates(opts: LiveUpdatesInitOptions): LiveUpdates {
  const { httpServer, sessionMiddleware, authenticateToken, isSessionActive } = opts;

  const db = openEventDb();
  const wss = new WebSocketServer({ noServer: true });
  const clients = new Map<WebSocket, ClientMeta>();

  const insertStmt = db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO realtime_events (user_id, scope, event_type, payload_json, created_at_ms) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
  );

  function maybeCleanup() {
    const MAX_EVENTS = 100_000;
    const MAX_AGE_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

    const maxRow = db.prepare(`SELECT MAX(id) AS maxId FROM realtime_events`).get() as any;
    const maxId = Number(maxRow?.maxId || 0);

    if (maxId > MAX_EVENTS) {
      const cutoff = maxId - MAX_EVENTS;
      db.prepare(`DELETE FROM realtime_events WHERE id <= ?`).run(cutoff);
    }

    const cutoffMs = Date.now() - MAX_AGE_MS;
    db.prepare(`DELETE FROM realtime_events WHERE created_at_ms < ?`).run(cutoffMs);
  }

  function persistEvent(params: {
    userId: number | null;
    scope: "USER" | "GLOBAL";
    eventType: string;
    payload: LiveUpdateEvent;
  }): number {
    const { userId, scope, eventType, payload } = params;
    const payloadJson = JSON.stringify(payload);
    const createdAtMs = Date.now();
    const info = insertStmt.run(userId ?? null, scope, eventType, payloadJson, createdAtMs);

    if (typeof info.lastInsertRowid === "number" && info.lastInsertRowid % 2000 === 0) {
      try {
        maybeCleanup();
      } catch {
        // never fail publish due to cleanup
      }
    }

    return Number(info.lastInsertRowid);
  }

  function sendToMatchingClients(event: LiveUpdateEvent, match: (meta: ClientMeta) => boolean) {
    const payload = JSON.stringify(event);
    for (const [ws, meta] of clients.entries()) {
      if (!wsReady(ws)) continue;
      if (!match(meta)) continue;
      try {
        ws.send(payload);
      } catch {
        // ignore per-client failures
      }
    }
  }

  function publishGlobal(event: LiveUpdateEvent, o?: { persist?: boolean; eventType?: string }): number | null {
    const channel = inferChannelFromType(event.type);
    let cursor: number | null = null;

    if (o?.persist) {
      cursor = persistEvent({
        userId: null,
        scope: "GLOBAL",
        eventType: o.eventType || event.type,
        payload: event,
      });
      event = { ...event, cursor };
    }

    sendToMatchingClients(event, (meta) => meta.scopes.has(channel));
    return cursor;
  }

  function publishToUser(userId: number, event: LiveUpdateEvent, o?: { persist?: boolean; eventType?: string }): number | null {
    const channel = inferChannelFromType(event.type);
    let cursor: number | null = null;

    if (o?.persist) {
      cursor = persistEvent({
        userId,
        scope: "USER",
        eventType: o.eventType || event.type,
        payload: event,
      });
      event = { ...event, cursor };
    }

    sendToMatchingClients(event, (meta) => meta.userId === userId && meta.scopes.has(channel));
    return cursor;
  }

  async function setAuthedUser(ws: WebSocket, meta: ClientMeta, userId: number, via: "session" | "token") {
    meta.userId = userId;
    meta.authedVia = via;
    for (const s of DEFAULT_SCOPES_AUTHED) meta.scopes.add(s);

    if (isSessionActive && meta.sessionId) {
      const ok = await isSessionActive(meta.sessionId);
      if (!ok) {
        try {
          ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "auth:revoked" }));
        } catch {}
        try {
          ws.close(4401, "Session revoked");
        } catch {}
      }
    }
  }

  // Manual upgrade handler so we can run session middleware
  httpServer.on("upgrade", (req, socket, head) => {
    const url = (req.url || "").split("?")[0];
    if (url !== "/ws") return;

    try {
      sessionMiddleware(req as any, {} as any, () => {
        wss.handleUpgrade(req, socket as any, head, (ws) => {
          wss.emit("connection", ws, req);
        });
      });
    } catch {
      try {
        socket.destroy();
      } catch {}
    }
  });

  wss.on("connection", (ws: WebSocket, req: IncomingMessage) => {
    const anyReq = req as any;
    const session = anyReq?.session as any | undefined;

    const sessionUserId = typeof session?.userId === "number" ? session.userId : null;
    const sessionId = typeof anyReq?.sessionID === "string" ? anyReq.sessionID : null;

    const meta: ClientMeta = {
      userId: sessionUserId,
      authedVia: sessionUserId ? "session" : null,
      sessionId,
      scopes: new Set(sessionUserId ? DEFAULT_SCOPES_AUTHED : DEFAULT_SCOPES_PUBLIC),
      connectedAtMs: Date.now(),
    };

    clients.set(ws, meta);

    try {
      ws.send(
        JSON.stringify({
          type: "hello",
          userId: meta.userId,
          authedVia: meta.authedVia,
          serverTimeMs: Date.now(),
        })
      );
    } catch {}

    ws.on("message", async (raw) => {
      const msg = safeJsonParse(raw);
      if (!msg || typeof msg !== "object") return;

      if (msg.type === "auth") {
        if (typeof msg.token === "string" && msg.token.length > 10 && authenticateToken) {
          try {
            const uid = await authenticateToken(msg.token);
            if (uid) {
              await setAuthedUser(ws, meta, uid, "token");
              ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "auth:ok", userId: uid }));
            } else {
              ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "auth:fail" }));
            }
          } catch {
            try {
              ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "auth:fail" }));
            } catch {}
          }
          return;
        }

        // Never trust a client-supplied userId; session-derived user is authoritative for web.
        if (meta.userId) {
          try {
            ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "auth:ok", userId: meta.userId }));
          } catch {}
        } else {
          try {
            ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "auth:required" }));
          } catch {}
        }
        return;
      }

      if (msg.type === "subscribe" && Array.isArray(msg.channels)) {
        const next = new Set<string>();
        for (const ch of msg.channels) {
          if (typeof ch !== "string") continue;
          if (ch === "quotes") next.add("quotes");
          if (ch === "trades" && meta.userId) next.add("trades");
          if (ch === "account" && meta.userId) next.add("account");
          if (ch === "audit" && meta.userId) next.add("audit");
        }
        next.add("quotes");
        meta.scopes = next;
        try {
          ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "subscribe:ok", channels: Array.from(meta.scopes) }));
        } catch {}
        return;
      }

      if (msg.type === "ping") {
        try {
          ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "pong", serverTimeMs: Date.now() }));
        } catch {}
        return;
      }
    });

    ws.on("close", () => clients.delete(ws));
    ws.on("error", () => clients.delete(ws));
  });

  function publishQuotesUpdated() {
    publishGlobal({ type: "quotes:updated" });
  }

  function publishTradesUpdated(userId: number) {
    publishToUser(userId, { type: "trades:updated", userId }, { persist: true, eventType: "TRADES_UPDATED" });
  }

  function publishAccountUpdated(userId: number) {
    publishToUser(userId, { type: "account:updated", userId }, { persist: true, eventType: "ACCOUNT_UPDATED" });
  }

  function getLatestCursorForUser(userId: number, includeGlobal = true): number {
    if (!userId) return 0;
    const row = db
      .prepare(
        includeGlobal
          ? `SELECT MAX(id) AS maxId FROM realtime_events WHERE (user_id = ?) OR (user_id IS NULL AND scope = 'GLOBAL')`
          : `SELECT MAX(id) AS maxId FROM realtime_events WHERE user_id = ?`
      )
      .get(userId) as any;
    return Number(row?.maxId || 0);
  }

  function fetchEventsSince(
    userId: number,
    cursor: number,
    limit: number,
    includeGlobal = true
  ): { nextCursor: number; events: LiveUpdateEvent[] } {
    const safeCursor = Number.isFinite(cursor) && cursor > 0 ? Math.floor(cursor) : 0;
    const safeLimit = Number.isFinite(limit) ? Math.max(1, Math.min(2000, Math.floor(limit))) : 500;

    const rows = db
      .prepare(
        includeGlobal
          ? `SELECT id, payload_json FROM realtime_events WHERE id > ? AND ((user_id = ?) OR (user_id IS NULL AND scope = 'GLOBAL')) ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT ?`
          : `SELECT id, payload_json FROM realtime_events WHERE id > ? AND user_id = ? ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT ?`
      )
      .all(safeCursor, userId, safeLimit) as Array<{ id: number; payload_json: string }>;

    const events: LiveUpdateEvent[] = [];
    let nextCursor = safeCursor;

    for (const r of rows) {
      const parsed = safeJsonParse(r.payload_json);
      if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") {
        events.push({ cursor: r.id, ...(parsed as any) });
      }
      nextCursor = r.id;
    }

    return { nextCursor, events };
  }

  return {
    publishGlobal,
    publishToUser,
    publishQuotesUpdated,
    publishTradesUpdated,
    publishAccountUpdated,
    getLatestCursorForUser,
    fetchEventsSince,
  };
}

// Realtime event log for cursor-based catch-up (WS reconnect/mobile backgrounding)
export function ensureRealtimeEventsTable() {
  const db = getDb();
  try {
    db.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS realtime_events (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        user_id INTEGER,
        scope TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USER',
        event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
        payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at_ms INTEGER NOT NULL
      );
    `);
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_realtime_events_user_id_id ON realtime_events(user_id, id);`);
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_realtime_events_scope_id ON realtime_events(scope, id);`);
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_realtime_events_event_type ON realtime_events(event_type);`);
    console.log("Realtime events table ensured");
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

// --- API-first sync endpoints (native reliability baseline) ---
// GET /api/v1/bootstrap: returns wsPath + server time + initial cursor
app.get("/api/v1/bootstrap", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  let userId: number | null = typeof (req.session as any)?.userId === "number" ? (req.session as any).userId : null;
  if (!userId && authenticateTokenFromRequest) {
    try {
      const tokenUser = await authenticateTokenFromRequest(req);
      if (tokenUser) userId = tokenUser;
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  const cursor = userId ? liveUpdates.getLatestCursorForUser(userId, true) : 0;
  return res.json({
    serverTimeMs: Date.now(),
    userId,
    cursor,
    wsPath: "/ws",
  });
});

// GET /api/v1/sync?cursor=123&limit=500: catch up on persisted events since cursor
// Auth: session cookie (web) OR bearer token (native)
app.get("/api/v1/sync", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  let userId: number | null = typeof (req.session as any)?.userId === "number" ? (req.session as any).userId : null;

  if (!userId && authenticateTokenFromRequest) {
    try {
      const tokenUser = await authenticateTokenFromRequest(req);
      if (tokenUser) userId = tokenUser;
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }

  if (!userId) {
    return res.status(401).json({ message: "Not authenticated" });
  }

  const cursor = Number(req.query.cursor ?? 0) || 0;
  const limit = Number(req.query.limit ?? 500) || 500;
  const out = liveUpdates.fetchEventsSince(userId, cursor, limit, true);
  return res.json(out);
});

export function ensureOrderIntentAuditTable() {
  const db = getDb();
  try {
    db.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS order_intent_audit (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL,

        -- idempotency + outcome linkage
        client_request_id TEXT,
        result_trade_id INTEGER,

        event_at INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
        event_at_ms INTEGER,
        event_code TEXT NOT NULL,
        decision TEXT,
        reject_check TEXT,
        reject_reason TEXT,
        actor_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USER',
        user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
        session_id TEXT,
        ip TEXT,
        user_agent TEXT,
        symbol TEXT,
        side TEXT,
        order_type TEXT,

      );
    `);

    // If table existed before, ensure new columns exist (safe ALTERs)
    const table = "order_intent_audit";
    const adds: Array<{ col: string; ddl: string }> = [
      { col: "client_request_id", ddl: "ALTER TABLE order_intent_audit ADD COLUMN client_request_id TEXT" },
      { col: "result_trade_id", ddl: "ALTER TABLE order_intent_audit ADD COLUMN result_trade_id INTEGER" },
    ];
    for (const a of adds) {
      if (!hasColumn(db, table, a.col)) {
        db.exec(a.ddl);
        console.log(`Added column ${a.col} to ${table}`);
      }
    }

    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_correlation ON order_intent_audit(correlation_id);`);
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_user ON order_intent_audit(user_id);`);
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_event ON order_intent_audit(event_code);`);
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_decision ON order_intent_audit(decision);`);

    // Idempotency + outcome indexes
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_client_req ON order_intent_audit(user_id, client_request_id);`);
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_result_trade ON order_intent_audit(result_trade_id);`);

    // Prevent duplicate ORDER_RECEIVED for same (user, clientRequestId)
    db.exec(`
      CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_idempotency
      ON order_intent_audit(user_id, client_request_id)
      WHERE client_request_id IS NOT NULL AND event_code = 'ORDER_RECEIVED';
    `);

  } finally {
    db.close();
  }

4) NEW FILE: server/security/apiTokens.ts
Create this file:

import type { Request } from "express";
import crypto from "crypto";
import { and, eq, isNull, or, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "@db";
import { apiTokens, users } from "@shared/schema";
import { getClientIp, getUserAgent } from "./sessionTrail";

export type ApiTokenAuthContext = {
  userId: number;
  tokenId: number;
  tokenPrefix: string;
  expiresAt: Date | null;
};

const DEFAULT_TTL_DAYS = 90;

function sha256Hex(input: string): string {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(input).digest("hex");
}

function generateToken(): string {
  const raw = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
  return `tc_${raw}`;
}

function extractBearerToken(req: Request): string | null {
  const h = req.headers["authorization"];
  if (typeof h === "string") {
    const m = h.match(/^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i);
    if (m?.[1]) return m[1].trim();
  }

  // Optional fallback header if ever needed
  const x = req.headers["x-api-token"];
  if (typeof x === "string" && x.trim()) return x.trim();

  return null;
}

export async function issueApiTokenForUser(args: {
  userId: number;
  deviceName?: string | null;
  ttlDays?: number;
  ip?: string | null;
  userAgent?: string | null;
}): Promise<{ token: string; tokenId: number; tokenPrefix: string; expiresAt: Date }> {
  const ttlDays = Number.isFinite(args.ttlDays) ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(args.ttlDays!)) : DEFAULT_TTL_DAYS;

  const token = generateToken();
  const tokenHash = sha256Hex(token);
  const tokenPrefix = token.slice(0, 12);

  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + ttlDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

  const [row] = await db
    .insert(apiTokens)
    .values({
      userId: args.userId,
      tokenHash,
      tokenPrefix,
      deviceName: args.deviceName ?? null,
      expiresAt,
      lastUsedAt: new Date(),
      lastUsedIp: args.ip ?? null,
      lastUsedUserAgent: args.userAgent ?? null,
    })
    .returning({ id: apiTokens.id });

  return { token, tokenId: row.id, tokenPrefix, expiresAt };
}

async function loadValidTokenByHash(tokenHash: string): Promise<(typeof apiTokens.$inferSelect) | null> {
  const row = await db.query.apiTokens.findFirst({
    where: and(
      eq(apiTokens.tokenHash, tokenHash),
      isNull(apiTokens.revokedAt),
      or(
        isNull(apiTokens.expiresAt),
        // expires_at stored as epoch seconds; compare against sqlite now
        sql`${apiTokens.expiresAt} > (strftime('%s','now'))`
      )
    ),
  });

  return row ?? null;
}

export async function authenticateApiTokenFromRawToken(token: string): Promise<ApiTokenAuthContext | null> {
  if (!token || typeof token !== "string") return null;

  const tokenHash = sha256Hex(token);
  const row = await loadValidTokenByHash(tokenHash);
  if (!row) return null;

  // Best-effort last-used update
  try {
    await db
      .update(apiTokens)
      .set({
        lastUsedAt: new Date(),
      })
      .where(eq(apiTokens.id, row.id));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }

  return {
    userId: row.userId,
    tokenId: row.id,
    tokenPrefix: row.tokenPrefix,
    expiresAt: row.expiresAt ?? null,
  };
}

export async function authenticateApiTokenFromRequest(req: Request): Promise<ApiTokenAuthContext | null> {
  const token = extractBearerToken(req);
  if (!token) return null;

  const ctx = await authenticateApiTokenFromRawToken(token);
  if (!ctx) return null;

  // attach richer telemetry on usage (best-effort)
  try {
    await db
      .update(apiTokens)
      .set({
        lastUsedAt: new Date(),
        lastUsedIp: getClientIp(req),
        lastUsedUserAgent: getUserAgent(req),
      })
      .where(eq(apiTokens.id, ctx.tokenId));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }

  return ctx;
}

export async function revokeApiToken(args: {
  tokenId: number;
  actingUserId?: number | null;
  reason?: string | null;
}): Promise<boolean> {
  const tokenId = Number(args.tokenId);
  if (!Number.isFinite(tokenId) || tokenId <= 0) return false;

  const res = await db
    .update(apiTokens)
    .set({
      revokedAt: new Date(),
      revokedByUserId: args.actingUserId ?? null,
      revokeReason: args.reason ?? "revoked",
    })
    .where(eq(apiTokens.id, tokenId));

  // drizzle sqlite returns object, but we can conservatively return true
  return !!res;
}

export async function listApiTokensForUser(userId: number): Promise<Array<{
  id: number;
  tokenPrefix: string;
  deviceName: string | null;
  createdAt: Date;
  expiresAt: Date | null;
  revokedAt: Date | null;
  lastUsedAt: Date | null;
}>> {
  const rows = await db.query.apiTokens.findMany({
    where: eq(apiTokens.userId, userId),
    orderBy: (t, { desc }) => [desc(t.createdAt)],
  });

  return rows.map(r => ({
    id: r.id,
    tokenPrefix: r.tokenPrefix,
    deviceName: r.deviceName ?? null,
    createdAt: r.createdAt,
    expiresAt: r.expiresAt ?? null,
    revokedAt: r.revokedAt ?? null,
    lastUsedAt: r.lastUsedAt ?? null,
  }));
}

export async function isUserAdmin(userId: number): Promise<boolean> {
  const u = await db.query.users.findFirst({
    where: eq(users.id, userId),
    columns: { isAdmin: true },
  });
  return !!u?.isAdmin;
}
5) server/middleware/auth.ts — make requireAuth accept session OR bearer token
Full file replacement (drop-in):

import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";
import { storage } from "../storage";
import { authenticateApiTokenFromRequest, isUserAdmin } from "../security/apiTokens";

declare module "express-session" {
  interface SessionData {
    userId?: number;
    isAdmin?: boolean;
    email?: string;
    username?: string;
    isImpersonating?: boolean;
    impersonatedUserId?: number;

    // token-auth hints (not persisted as a “real” session of record)
    isTokenAuth?: boolean;
    tokenId?: number;
    tokenPrefix?: string;
  }
}

/**
 * Session OR Token auth.
 * - Web keeps working via session cookie.
 * - Mobile/native can use Authorization: Bearer <token>.
 */
export async function requireAuth(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  // 1) Session path
  if (req.session?.userId) {
    return next();
  }

  // 2) Token path
  const tokenCtx = await authenticateApiTokenFromRequest(req);
  if (!tokenCtx) {
    return res.status(401).json({ message: "Not authenticated" });
  }

  const user = await storage.getUser(tokenCtx.userId);
  if (!user) {
    return res.status(401).json({ message: "Invalid token user" });
  }

  // Disallow view-as on token auth (keeps admin impersonation web-only)
  if (req.session?.isImpersonating) {
    return res.status(403).json({ message: "Impersonation not allowed on token auth" });
  }

  // Lightweight shim into session-shaped fields (so existing routes keep working)
  req.session.userId = user.id;
  req.session.email = user.email;
  req.session.username = user.username;
  req.session.isAdmin = user.isAdmin;

  req.session.isTokenAuth = true;
  req.session.tokenId = tokenCtx.tokenId;
  req.session.tokenPrefix = tokenCtx.tokenPrefix;

  return next();
}

export async function requireAdmin(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  // First ensure authed (session or token)
  await requireAuth(req, res, async () => {
    // Prefer cached
    if (req.session?.isAdmin) return next();

    // If token authed, re-check admin flag defensively
    const userId = req.session?.userId;
    if (!userId) return res.status(401).json({ message: "Not authenticated" });

    const admin = await isUserAdmin(userId);
    if (!admin) return res.status(403).json({ message: "Admin access required" });

    req.session.isAdmin = true;
    return next();
  });
}

/**
 * If admin is impersonating, force downstream to operate on impersonated userId.
 * NOTE: token auth cannot impersonate (enforced in requireAuth).
 */
export function impersonationGuard(req: Request, _res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  if (req.session?.isImpersonating && req.session.impersonatedUserId) {
    req.session.userId = req.session.impersonatedUserId;
  }
  next();
}

1) client/src/lib/queryClient.ts — automatically attach Authorization header if a token exists
Full file replacement:

import { QueryClient, QueryFunction } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { getDeviceInstallId, getDeviceFingerprint, getClientTimezone, getClientLanguage } from "./identity";

let cachedFingerprint: string | null = null;

function getApiToken(): string | null {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem("tc_api_token");
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function getIdentityHeaders(): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  if (!cachedFingerprint) {
    cachedFingerprint = await getDeviceFingerprint();
  }

  const token = getApiToken();

  return {
    "x-device-fp": cachedFingerprint,
    "x-device-install-id": getDeviceInstallId(),
    "x-client-tz": getClientTimezone(),
    "x-client-lang": getClientLanguage(),
    ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
  };
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;

  constructor(message: string, status: number) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.name = "ApiError";
  }
}

async function throwIfResNotOk(res: Response) {
  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    let message = text || res.statusText;
    try {
      const errorData = JSON.parse(text);
      message = errorData.message || text || res.statusText;
    } catch {
      // non-JSON
    }
    throw new ApiError(message, res.status);
  }
}

export async function apiRequest(
  method: string,
  url: string,
  data?: unknown | undefined,
): Promise<Response> {
  const identityHeaders = await getIdentityHeaders();

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    headers: {
      ...(data ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : {}),
      ...identityHeaders,
    },
    body: data ? JSON.stringify(data) : undefined,
    credentials: "include", // keep sessions working for web
  });

  await throwIfResNotOk(res);
  return res;
}

type UnauthorizedBehavior = "returnNull" | "throw";
export const getQueryFn: <T>(options: { on401: UnauthorizedBehavior }) => QueryFunction<T> =
  ({ on401: unauthorizedBehavior }) =>
  async ({ queryKey }) => {
    const identityHeaders = await getIdentityHeaders();

    const res = await fetch(queryKey[0] as string, {
      credentials: "include",
      headers: identityHeaders,
    });

    if (unauthorizedBehavior === "returnNull" && res.status === 401) {
      return null;
    }

    await throwIfResNotOk(res);
    return await res.json();
  };

export const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      queryFn: getQueryFn({ on401: "throw" }),
      refetchInterval: false,
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
      staleTime: Infinity,
      retry: false,
    },
    mutations: {
      retry: false,
    },
  },
});

A2) Replace the current WS block with a secure WS (token/session handshake + per-user)
In server/routes.ts, find and replace the entire existing WS section:

From:

  server: httpServer,

  ...

Replace that whole section with this:

// --- Live WebSocket server for live updates (quotes + per-user events) ---
// Auth is determined server-side using (a) session cookie OR (b) ?token= query param.
// The client MUST NOT send userId over WS.
type LiveScope = "quotes" | "trades" | "account" | "audit" | "admin" | "global";
type LiveClientMeta = {
  userId: number | null;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  authedBy: "session" | "token" | "none";
  scopes: Set<LiveScope>;
};
type LiveClient = WebSocket & { meta?: LiveClientMeta };

const wss = new WebSocketServer({ noServer: true });

function createFakeResForSession() {
  // Minimal response stub for express-session middleware during WS upgrade
  return {
    getHeader() { return undefined; },
    setHeader() {},
    removeHeader() {},
    writeHead() {},
    end() {},
  } as any;
}

function inferScopeFromEvent(evt: any): LiveScope {
  const t = String(evt?.type ?? "");
  if (t.startsWith("quotes:")) return "quotes";
  if (t.startsWith("trades:")) return "trades";
  if (t.startsWith("account:")) return "account";
  if (t.startsWith("audit:")) return "audit";
  return "global";
}

function safeSend(ws: WebSocket, payload: any) {
  if (ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;
  try {
    ws.send(JSON.stringify(payload));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

async function authenticateWsToken(rawToken: string): Promise<{ userId: number; isAdmin: boolean } | null> {
  const token = String(rawToken || "").trim();
  if (!token) return null;

  // Basic abuse bounds (avoid giant query strings)
  if (token.length < 16 || token.length > 4096) return null;

  try {
    // Prefer your Part-4 token module if present
    const mod: any = await import("./security/apiTokens");
    if (typeof mod.authenticateApiTokenFromRawToken !== "function") return null;

    const result = await mod.authenticateApiTokenFromRawToken(token);
    if (!result || typeof result.userId !== "number") return null;

    return { userId: result.userId, isAdmin: !!result.isAdmin };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function buildScopes(userId: number | null, isAdmin: boolean): Set<LiveScope> {
  const scopes = new Set<LiveScope>(["quotes"]);
  if (userId) {
    scopes.add("trades");
    scopes.add("account");
    scopes.add("audit");
    if (isAdmin) scopes.add("admin");
  }
  return scopes;
}

// Secure broadcaster: can broadcast globally or to a single user, and respects scopes.
function broadcast(event: any, opts?: { userId?: number | null; scope?: LiveScope }) {
  const payload = JSON.stringify(event);
  const scope = opts?.scope ?? inferScopeFromEvent(event);
  const targetUserId = opts?.userId ?? null;

  for (const ws of wss.clients as Set<LiveClient>) {
    if (ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) continue;

    const meta = ws.meta;
    if (!meta) continue;

    // Scope gating
    if (scope && !meta.scopes.has(scope)) continue;

    // Per-user gating (if provided)
    if (targetUserId !== null && meta.userId !== targetUserId) continue;

    ws.send(payload);
  }
}

// WS upgrade handler (auth via session or token query param)
httpServer.on("upgrade", (req, socket, head) => {
  let url: URL;
  try {
    url = new URL(req.url || "", "http://localhost");
  } catch {
    socket.destroy();
    return;
  }

  if (url.pathname !== "/ws") {
    // Not our WS path
    return;
  }

  const tokenParam = url.searchParams.get("token");

  // Run the session middleware so req.session is available here.
  sessionMiddleware(req as any, createFakeResForSession(), () => {
    void (async () => {
      const sess: any = (req as any).session;

      // Support View-As: treat impersonatedUserId as the effective userId (read-only WS)
      const sessionUserId =
        sess?.isImpersonating && typeof sess?.impersonatedUserId === "number"
          ? sess.impersonatedUserId
          : (typeof sess?.userId === "number" ? sess.userId : null);

      const sessionIsAdmin = !!sess?.isAdmin;

      let userId: number | null = sessionUserId;
      let isAdmin = sessionIsAdmin;
      let authedBy: LiveClientMeta["authedBy"] = sessionUserId ? "session" : "none";

      // If no session auth, allow token query param auth
      if (!userId && tokenParam) {
        const tokenIdentity = await authenticateWsToken(tokenParam);
        if (!tokenIdentity) {
          // Token was provided but invalid: reject upgrade (mobile should re-auth)
          try {
            socket.write("HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\r\n\r\n");
          } catch {}
          socket.destroy();
          return;
        }
        userId = tokenIdentity.userId;
        isAdmin = tokenIdentity.isAdmin;
        authedBy = "token";
      }

      // If BOTH exist, enforce consistency (optional hardening)
      if (sessionUserId && tokenParam) {
        const tokenIdentity = await authenticateWsToken(tokenParam);
        if (tokenIdentity && tokenIdentity.userId !== sessionUserId) {
          try {
            socket.write("HTTP/1.1 403 Forbidden\r\n\r\n");
          } catch {}
          socket.destroy();
          return;
        }
      }

      // Stash identity for connection handler
      (req as any)._wsIdentity = { userId, isAdmin, authedBy };

      wss.handleUpgrade(req, socket, head, (ws) => {
        wss.emit("connection", ws, req);
      });
    })().catch(() => {
      socket.destroy();
    });
  });
});

wss.on("connection", (ws, req) => {
  const client = ws as LiveClient;

  const identity = ((req as any)._wsIdentity || {}) as {
    userId?: number | null;
    isAdmin?: boolean;
    authedBy?: LiveClientMeta["authedBy"];
  };

  const userId = typeof identity.userId === "number" ? identity.userId : null;
  const isAdmin = !!identity.isAdmin;
  const authedBy = identity.authedBy || (userId ? "session" : "none");

  client.meta = {
    userId,
    isAdmin,
    authedBy,
    scopes: buildScopes(userId, isAdmin),
  };

  // Server-side handshake (client can observe authed state + scopes)
  safeSend(client, {
    type: "ws:hello",
    authed: !!userId,
    authedBy,
    scopes: Array.from(client.meta.scopes),
    ts: Date.now(),
  });

  // IMPORTANT: ignore/deny any client attempt to set userId
  client.on("message", (raw) => {
    try {
      const msg = JSON.parse(raw.toString());

      // Legacy client behavior: { type: "auth", userId: ... } — reject silently or warn.
      if (msg?.type === "auth" && (msg.userId !== undefined || msg.token !== undefined)) {
        safeSend(client, {
          type: "ws:error",
          code: "WS_AUTH_SERVER_SIDE",
          message: "WS auth is server-side (session cookie or ?token=). Client auth messages are not accepted.",
          ts: Date.now(),
        });
        return;
      }

      // Optional future: subscribe/unsubscribe messages could go here.
    } catch {
      // ignore invalid messages
    }
  });
});

B1) Replace client/src/hooks/use-trades.tsx بالكامل
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { apiRequest } from "@/lib/queryClient";
import { useAuth } from "./use-auth";
import { useToast } from "./use-toast";
import { useWebSocket } from "./use-websocket";

function getAuthTokenForWs(): string | null {
  // Prefer the reserved contract key
  const a = localStorage.getItem("auth.token") || sessionStorage.getItem("auth.token");
  if (a) return a;

  // Back-compat (if you stored it under a different name earlier)
  const b = localStorage.getItem("tc_api_token") || sessionStorage.getItem("tc_api_token");
  return b || null;
}

function buildWsUrl(): string {
  const base =
    (window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss://" : "ws://") +
    window.location.host +
    "/ws";

  const token = getAuthTokenForWs();
  if (!token) return base;

  // Token query param auth (server validates). NOTE: do not log this URL.
  return `${base}?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`;
}

export function useTrades() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const { user } = useAuth();

  // Connect to WebSocket for live trade updates (server does per-user filtering)
  const { isConnected: isTradeWsConnected } = useWebSocket(buildWsUrl(), {
    onMessage: (message) => {
      if (!message || typeof message !== "object") return;

      if (message.type === "trades:updated") {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades"] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades/open"] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/auth/current-user"] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/account/summary"] });
      }
    },
  });

  // Get all trades
  const {
    data: trades = [],
    isLoading: isLoadingTrades,
    error: tradesError,
    refetch: refetchTrades,
  } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["/api/trades"],
    enabled: !!user,
  });

  // Get open trades
  const {
    data: openTrades = [],
    isLoading: isLoadingOpenTrades,
    error: openTradesError,
    refetch: refetchOpenTrades,
  } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["/api/trades/open"],
    enabled: !!user,
  });

  // Create a new trade
  const createTrade = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (data: {
      symbolId: number;
      type: "BUY" | "SELL";
      size: number;
      openPrice: number;
      takeProfit?: number;
      stopLoss?: number;
    }) => {
      return apiRequest("POST", "/api/trades", data);
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      toast({
        title: "Trade Created",
        description: "Your trade was successfully placed",
      });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades/open"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/auth/current-user"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/account/summary"] });
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      toast({
        title: "Error",
        description: error.message || "Failed to place trade",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    },
  });

  // Close a trade
  const closeTrade = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id }: { id: number }) => {
      return apiRequest("POST", `/api/trades/${id}/close`, {});
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      toast({
        title: "Trade Closed",
        description: "Your trade was successfully closed",
      });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades/open"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/auth/current-user"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/account/summary"] });
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      toast({
        title: "Error",
        description: error.message || "Failed to close trade",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    },
  });

  return {
    trades,
    openTrades,
    isLoadingTrades,
    isLoadingOpenTrades,
    tradesError,
    openTradesError,
    refetchTrades,
    refetchOpenTrades,
    createTrade,
    closeTrade,
    isTradeWsConnected,
  };
}
B2) Replace client/src/hooks/use-websocket.tsx to redact tokens in logs
Your current hook logs the full URL (which will include ?token=). Replace the file with:

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";

interface UseWebSocketOptions {
  onMessage?: (data: any) => void;
  onOpen?: () => void;
  onClose?: () => void;
  onError?: (error: Event) => void;
  reconnectInterval?: number;
  reconnectAttempts?: number;
}

function redactWsUrl(url: string): string {
  try {
    const u = new URL(url, window.location.href);
    if (u.searchParams.has("token")) u.searchParams.set("token", "[REDACTED]");
    return u.toString();
  } catch {
    return url.replace(/token=([^&]+)/g, "token=[REDACTED]");
  }
}

export function useWebSocket(url: string, options: UseWebSocketOptions = {}) {
  const [socket, setSocket] = useState<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const [isConnected, setIsConnected] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<Event | null>(null);
  const [reconnectCount, setReconnectCount] = useState(0);

  const {
    onMessage,
    onOpen,
    onClose,
    onError,
    reconnectInterval = 3000,
    reconnectAttempts = 10,
  } = options;

  const connect = useCallback(() => {
    // Never log raw token-bearing URLs
    console.log(`Connecting to WebSocket at: "${redactWsUrl(url)}"`);

    const ws = new WebSocket(url);

    ws.onopen = () => {
      console.log("WebSocket connected successfully");
      setIsConnected(true);
      setError(null);
      setReconnectCount(0);
      onOpen?.();
    };

    ws.onmessage = (event) => {
      try {
        const data = JSON.parse(event.data);
        onMessage?.(data);
      } catch {
        // ignore malformed messages
      }
    };

    ws.onclose = (event) => {
      console.log(`WebSocket disconnected:`, event.code, event.reason);
      setIsConnected(false);
      onClose?.();

      if (reconnectCount < reconnectAttempts) {
        const nextAttemptDelay = reconnectInterval;
        console.log(`Will attempt to reconnect in ${nextAttemptDelay}ms`);

        setTimeout(() => {
          setReconnectCount((prev) => prev + 1);
          connect();
        }, nextAttemptDelay);
      }
    };

    ws.onerror = (event) => {
      console.error("WebSocket error:", event);
      setError(event);
      onError?.(event);
    };

    setSocket(ws);

    return () => {
      if (ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CONNECTING) {
        ws.close();
      }
    };
  }, [url, onMessage, onOpen, onClose, onError, reconnectInterval, reconnectAttempts, reconnectCount]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const cleanup = connect();
    return cleanup;
  }, [connect]);

  const sendMessage = useCallback(
    (data: any) => {
      if (socket && socket.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        socket.send(JSON.stringify(data));
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    },
    [socket]
  );

  return { isConnected, sendMessage, error };
}
What this Part 5 achieves
Web clients: WS auth comes from session cookie (server resolves userId).

Mobile/native clients: WS auth comes from ?token= query param, validated server-side.

The WS no longer trusts any userId supplied by client messages.

Server sends a ws:hello handshake so the client can observe auth state/scopes if needed.

Trades updates are delivered only to the correct user’s sockets (multi-device safe).

6C) Audit writer: persist idempotency fields (FULL FILE REPLACEMENT)

// server/lib/auditWriter.ts
import crypto from "crypto";
import { db } from "@db";
import { tradeAudit, orderIntentAudit } from "@shared/schema";
import { eq, desc } from "drizzle-orm";

export type ActorType = "USER" | "ADMIN" | "SYSTEM";

export type AuditContext = {
  actorType: ActorType;
  userId?: number | null;
  sessionId?: string | null;
  ip?: string | null;
  userAgent?: string | null;
  deviceId?: string | null;
  correlationId?: string | null;
};

function canonicalize(value: any): any {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;
  if (typeof value !== "object") return value;
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(canonicalize);
  const keys = Object.keys(value).sort();
  const out: any = {};
  for (const k of keys) out[k] = canonicalize(value[k]);
  return out;
}

function canonicalJson(value: any): string {
  return JSON.stringify(canonicalize(value));
}

function sha256Hex(input: string): string {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(input).digest("hex");
}

export function generateCorrelationId(): string {
  return crypto.randomUUID();
}

// ===== Trade Audit (existing) =====

export type TradeAuditParams = {
  tradeId: number;
  eventType: string;
  eventCategory: string;
  ctx: AuditContext;

  correlationId?: string | null;
  orderId?: string | null;
  executionId?: string | null;
  positionId?: string | null;

  symbol?: string | null;
  side?: string | null;
  orderType?: string | null;
  timeInForce?: string | null;
  qtyLots?: number | null;

  requestedPrice?: number | null;
  triggerPrice?: number | null;
  fillPrice?: number | null;
  avgFillPrice?: number | null;

  quoteBid?: number | null;
  quoteAsk?: number | null;
  quoteMid?: number | null;
  quoteSpread?: number | null;
  spreadPips?: number | null;

  slippage?: number | null;
  slippagePips?: number | null;

  latencyMs?: number | null;

  riskCheckName?: string | null;
  riskLimitValue?: number | null;
  riskObservedValue?: number | null;
  riskResult?: string | null;
  reasonCode?: string | null;

  note?: string | null;
  payload?: any | null;
};

async function getLastTradeAuditHash(tradeId: number): Promise<string> {
  const last = await db.query.tradeAudit.findFirst({
    where: eq(tradeAudit.tradeId, tradeId),
    orderBy: desc(tradeAudit.id),
  });
  return (last as any)?.eventHash ?? "GENESIS";
}

export async function writeTradeAudit(params: TradeAuditParams): Promise<{ eventHash: string; prevHash: string }> {
  const now = new Date();
  const eventAtMs = Date.now();

  const prevHash = await getLastTradeAuditHash(params.tradeId);

  const envelope = {
    tradeId: params.tradeId,
    eventAtMs,
    eventType: params.eventType,
    eventCategory: params.eventCategory,

    correlationId: params.correlationId ?? params.ctx.correlationId ?? null,
    orderId: params.orderId ?? null,
    executionId: params.executionId ?? null,
    positionId: params.positionId ?? null,

    actorType: params.ctx.actorType,
    actorUserId: params.ctx.userId ?? null,
    sessionId: params.ctx.sessionId ?? null,
    ip: params.ctx.ip ?? null,
    userAgent: params.ctx.userAgent ?? null,
    deviceId: params.ctx.deviceId ?? null,

    symbol: params.symbol ?? null,
    side: params.side ?? null,
    orderType: params.orderType ?? null,
    timeInForce: params.timeInForce ?? null,
    qtyLots: params.qtyLots ?? null,

    requestedPrice: params.requestedPrice ?? null,
    triggerPrice: params.triggerPrice ?? null,
    fillPrice: params.fillPrice ?? null,
    avgFillPrice: params.avgFillPrice ?? null,

    quoteBid: params.quoteBid ?? null,
    quoteAsk: params.quoteAsk ?? null,
    quoteMid: params.quoteMid ?? null,
    quoteSpread: params.quoteSpread ?? null,
    spreadPips: params.spreadPips ?? null,

    slippage: params.slippage ?? null,
    slippagePips: params.slippagePips ?? null,
    slippageReference: params.slippageReference ?? null,
    latencyMs: params.latencyMs ?? null,

    riskCheckName: params.riskCheckName ?? null,
    riskLimitValue: params.riskLimitValue ?? null,
    riskObservedValue: params.riskObservedValue ?? null,
    riskResult: params.riskResult ?? null,
    reasonCode: params.reasonCode ?? null,

    note: params.note ?? null,
    payload: params.payload ?? null,
  };

  const payloadJson = canonicalJson(envelope);
  const eventHash = sha256Hex(prevHash + "\n" + payloadJson);

  await db.insert(tradeAudit).values({
    tradeId: params.tradeId,
    eventType: params.eventType,
    eventCategory: params.eventCategory,
    eventAt: now,
    eventAtMs,

    correlationId: params.correlationId ?? params.ctx.correlationId ?? null,
    orderId: params.orderId ?? null,
    executionId: params.executionId ?? null,
    positionId: params.positionId ?? null,

    actorType: params.ctx.actorType,
    actorUserId: params.ctx.userId ?? null,
    sessionId: params.ctx.sessionId ?? null,
    ip: params.ctx.ip ?? null,
    userAgent: params.ctx.userAgent ?? null,

    symbol: params.symbol ?? null,
    side: params.side ?? null,
    orderType: params.orderType ?? null,
    timeInForce: params.timeInForce ?? null,
    qtyLots: params.qtyLots ?? null,

    requestedPrice: params.requestedPrice ?? null,
    triggerPrice: params.triggerPrice ?? null,
    fillPrice: params.fillPrice ?? null,
    avgFillPrice: params.avgFillPrice ?? null,

    quoteBid: params.quoteBid ?? null,
    quoteAsk: params.quoteAsk ?? null,
    quoteMid: params.quoteMid ?? null,
    quoteSpread: params.quoteSpread ?? null,
    spreadPips: params.spreadPips ?? null,

    slippage: params.slippage ?? null,
    slippagePips: params.slippagePips ?? null,
    slippageReference: params.slippageReference ?? null,
    latencyMs: params.latencyMs ?? null,

    riskCheckName: params.riskCheckName ?? null,
    riskLimitValue: params.riskLimitValue ?? null,
    riskObservedValue: params.riskObservedValue ?? null,
    riskResult: params.riskResult ?? null,
    reasonCode: params.reasonCode ?? null,

    note: params.note ?? null,
    payloadJson,
    prevHash,
    eventHash,
  });

  return { eventHash, prevHash };
}

// ===== Order Intent Audit (idempotency added) =====

export type OrderIntentParams = {
  correlationId: string;
  eventCode: "ORDER_RECEIVED" | "DECISION";
  ctx: AuditContext;
  userId: number;

  // Idempotency / native reliability
  clientRequestId?: string | null;
  requestHash?: string | null;
  resultTradeId?: number | null;

  decision?: "PASS" | "REJECT" | null;
  rejectCheck?: string | null;
  rejectReason?: string | null;

  symbol?: string | null;
  side?: string | null;
  orderType?: string | null;
  timeInForce?: string | null;
  qtyLots?: number | null;

  requestedPrice?: number | null;
  limitPrice?: number | null;
  stopPrice?: number | null;
  takeProfit?: number | null;
  stopLoss?: number | null;

  quoteAsk?: number | null;
  quoteMid?: number | null;
  quoteTs?: Date | null;
  quoteIsStale?: boolean | null;

  riskLimit?: any | null;
  riskObserved?: any | null;
  riskSnapshot?: any | null;

  payload?: any | null;
};

export async function writeOrderIntentAudit(params: OrderIntentParams): Promise<{ eventHash: string; prevHash: string }> {
  const now = new Date();
  const eventAtMs = Date.now();

  const lastEvent = await db.query.orderIntentAudit.findFirst({
    where: eq(orderIntentAudit.correlationId, params.correlationId),
    orderBy: desc(orderIntentAudit.id),
  });

  const prevHash = (lastEvent as any)?.eventHash ?? "GENESIS";

  const envelope = {
    correlationId: params.correlationId,
    eventAtMs,
    eventCode: params.eventCode,

    // idempotency fields
    clientRequestId: params.clientRequestId ?? null,
    requestHash: params.requestHash ?? null,
    resultTradeId: params.resultTradeId ?? null,

    decision: params.decision ?? null,
    rejectCheck: params.rejectCheck ?? null,
    rejectReason: params.rejectReason ?? null,

    actorType: params.ctx.actorType,
    userId: params.userId,
    sessionId: params.ctx.sessionId ?? null,
    ip: params.ctx.ip ?? null,
    userAgent: params.ctx.userAgent ?? null,

    symbol: params.symbol ?? null,
    side: params.side ?? null,
    orderType: params.orderType ?? null,
    timeInForce: params.timeInForce ?? null,
    qtyLots: params.qtyLots ?? null,

    requestedPrice: params.requestedPrice ?? null,
    limitPrice: params.limitPrice ?? null,
    stopPrice: params.stopPrice ?? null,
    takeProfit: params.takeProfit ?? null,
    stopLoss: params.stopLoss ?? null,

    quoteBid: params.quoteBid ?? null,
    quoteAsk: params.quoteAsk ?? null,
    quoteMid: params.quoteMid ?? null,
    quoteIsStale: params.quoteIsStale ?? null,

    riskLimit: params.riskLimit ?? null,
    riskObserved: params.riskObserved ?? null,
    riskSnapshot: params.riskSnapshot ?? null,

    payload: params.payload ?? null,
  };

  const payloadJson = canonicalJson(envelope);
  const eventHash = sha256Hex(prevHash + "\n" + payloadJson);

  await db.insert(orderIntentAudit).values({
    correlationId: params.correlationId,
    eventAt: now,
    eventAtMs,
    eventCode: params.eventCode,

    // idempotency fields
    clientRequestId: params.clientRequestId ?? null,
    requestHash: params.requestHash ?? null,
    resultTradeId: params.resultTradeId ?? null,

    decision: params.decision ?? null,
    rejectCheck: params.rejectCheck ?? null,
    rejectReason: params.rejectReason ?? null,

    actorType: params.ctx.actorType,
    userId: params.userId,
    sessionId: params.ctx.sessionId ?? null,
    ip: params.ctx.ip ?? null,
    userAgent: params.ctx.userAgent ?? null,

    symbol: params.symbol ?? null,
    side: params.side ?? null,
    orderType: params.orderType ?? null,
    timeInForce: params.timeInForce ?? null,
    qtyLots: params.qtyLots ?? null,

    requestedPrice: params.requestedPrice ?? null,
    limitPrice: params.limitPrice ?? null,
    stopPrice: params.stopPrice ?? null,
    takeProfit: params.takeProfit ?? null,
    stopLoss: params.stopLoss ?? null,

    quoteBid: params.quoteBid ?? null,
    quoteAsk: params.quoteAsk ?? null,
    quoteMid: params.quoteMid ?? null,
    quoteTs: params.quoteTs ?? null,
    quoteIsStale: params.quoteIsStale ?? false,

    riskLimitJson: params.riskLimit ? JSON.stringify(params.riskLimit) : null,
    riskObservedJson: params.riskObserved ? JSON.stringify(params.riskObserved) : null,
    riskSnapshotJson: params.riskSnapshot ? JSON.stringify(params.riskSnapshot) : null,

    payloadJson,
    prevHash,
    eventHash,
  });

  return { eventHash, prevHash };
}

// Calculate slippage in pips
export function calculateSlippagePips(symbol: string, slippagePoints: number): number {
  const isJpy = symbol.toUpperCase().includes("JPY");
  const pipValue = isJpy ? 0.01 : 0.0001;
  return slippagePoints / pipValue;
}

// Calculate spread in pips
export function calculateSpreadPips(symbol: string, spreadPoints: number): number {
  const isJpy = symbol.toUpperCase().includes("JPY");
  const pipValue = isJpy ? 0.01 : 0.0001;
  return spreadPoints / pipValue;
}

2) Add helper functions (place anywhere above app.post("/api/trades"...)
function stableStringify(value: any): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "null";
  if (typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(stableStringify).join(",")}]`;
  const keys = Object.keys(value).sort();
  return `{${keys.map(k => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${stableStringify(value[k])}`).join(",")}}`;
}

function sha256Hex(input: string): string {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(input).digest("hex");
}

/**
 * Canonical request hash for idempotency conflict detection.
 * Excludes server-computed/openPrice noise and any volatile UI fields.
 */
function computeTradeRequestHash(body: any): string {
  const c: any = {
    symbolId: body?.symbolId ?? null,
    type: body?.type ?? null,
    orderType: body?.orderType ?? null,
    lots: body?.lots ?? body?.size ?? null,
    limitPrice: body?.limitPrice ?? null,
    stopPrice: body?.stopPrice ?? null,
    takeProfit: body?.takeProfit ?? null,
    stopLoss: body?.stopLoss ?? null,
  };
  return sha256Hex(stableStringify(c));
}

      // Capture request receipt time for latency measurement
      const receivedAtMs = Date.now();

      const auditCtx = buildAuditContext(req);
      const userId = req.session.userId;

      // Idempotency key (native/mobile retry safety)
      const clientRequestIdRaw =
        (req.header("Idempotency-Key") ||
          req.header("X-Idempotency-Key") ||
          (req.body?.clientRequestId as string | undefined) ||
          "") + "";

      const clientRequestId = clientRequestIdRaw.trim() ? clientRequestIdRaw.trim() : null;
      const requestHash = clientRequestId ? computeTradeRequestHash(req.body) : null;

      let correlationId = generateCorrelationId();
      let receivedRowExists = false;

      // If client provides idempotency key, attempt replay resolution BEFORE doing heavy work
      if (clientRequestId) {
        const existingReceived = await db.query.orderIntentAudit.findFirst({
          where: (t, ops) =>
            and(
              eq(t.userId, userId),
              eq(t.clientRequestId, clientRequestId),
              eq(t.eventCode, "ORDER_RECEIVED")
            ),
          orderBy: (t, ops) => desc(t.id),
        });

        if (existingReceived) {
          receivedRowExists = true;

          // Conflict: same idempotency key used with different payload
          if (existingReceived.requestHash && requestHash && existingReceived.requestHash !== requestHash) {
            return res.status(409).json({
              message: "Idempotency-Key reuse with different payload",
              code: "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
            });
          }

          correlationId = existingReceived.correlationId;
          auditCtx.correlationId = correlationId;

          // If the previous attempt already produced a trade, return it
          if (existingReceived.resultTradeId) {
            const t = await storage.getTradeById(existingReceived.resultTradeId);
            if (t) {
              res.setHeader("X-Idempotent-Replay", "1");
              return res.status(200).json(t);
            }
          }

          // Best-effort: if trade exists by correlationId, return it and backfill resultTradeId
          const tByCorr = await storage.getTradeByCorrelationId(correlationId);
          if (tByCorr) {
            try {
              await db
                .update(orderIntentAudit)
                .set({ resultTradeId: tByCorr.id })
                .where(eq(orderIntentAudit.id, existingReceived.id));
            } catch (e) {
              console.warn("Failed to backfill resultTradeId for idempotent replay:", e);
            }
            res.setHeader("X-Idempotent-Replay", "1");
            return res.status(200).json(tByCorr);
          }

          // Otherwise: continue with same correlationId (do NOT write another ORDER_RECEIVED row)
        }
      }

      // Ensure correlationId is on the ctx
      auditCtx.correlationId = correlationId;

6F) Client: automatically send Idempotency-Key on POST /api/trades
File: client/src/lib/queryClient.ts (FULL FILE REPLACEMENT)

import { QueryClient, QueryFunction } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { getDeviceInstallId, getDeviceFingerprint, getClientTimezone, getClientLanguage } from "./identity";

let cachedFingerprint: string | null = null;

function generateUuid(): string {
  // Browser-safe UUID
  // Prefer crypto.randomUUID; fallback to RFC4122-ish generator.
  const c: any = globalThis.crypto;
  if (c?.randomUUID) return c.randomUUID();

  const bytes = new Uint8Array(16);
  c.getRandomValues(bytes);
  // Set version (4) and variant (10)
  bytes[6] = (bytes[6] & 0x0f) | 0x40;
  bytes[8] = (bytes[8] & 0x3f) | 0x80;

  const hex = Array.from(bytes).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
  return `${hex.slice(0, 8)}-${hex.slice(8, 12)}-${hex.slice(12, 16)}-${hex.slice(16, 20)}-${hex.slice(20)}`;
}

async function getIdentityHeaders(): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  if (!cachedFingerprint) {
    cachedFingerprint = await getDeviceFingerprint();
  }

  return {
    "x-device-fp": cachedFingerprint,
    "x-device-install-id": getDeviceInstallId(),
    "x-client-tz": getClientTimezone(),
    "x-client-lang": getClientLanguage(),
  };
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;

  constructor(message: string, status: number) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.name = "ApiError";
  }
}

async function throwIfResNotOk(res: Response) {
  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    let message = text || res.statusText;
    try {
      const errorData = JSON.parse(text);
      message = errorData.message || text || res.statusText;
    } catch {
      // non-JSON
    }
    throw new ApiError(message, res.status);
  }
}

type ApiRequestOptions = {
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  idempotencyKey?: string;
};

export async function apiRequest(
  method: string,
  url: string,
  data?: unknown | undefined,
  options?: ApiRequestOptions
): Promise<Response> {
  const identityHeaders = await getIdentityHeaders();

  const extraHeaders = options?.headers ?? {};
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    ...(data ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : {}),
    ...identityHeaders,
    ...extraHeaders,
  };

  // Automatically attach Idempotency-Key to order placement
  // Native apps should REUSE the same key across retries; web gets per-request keys by default.
  if (method.toUpperCase() === "POST" && url === "/api/trades") {
    headers["Idempotency-Key"] = options?.idempotencyKey ?? `trade_${generateUuid()}`;
  }

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    headers,
    body: data ? JSON.stringify(data) : undefined,
    credentials: "include",
  });

  await throwIfResNotOk(res);
  return res;
}

type UnauthorizedBehavior = "returnNull" | "throw";
export const getQueryFn: <T>(options: { on401: UnauthorizedBehavior }) => QueryFunction<T> =
  ({ on401: unauthorizedBehavior }) =>
  async ({ queryKey }) => {
    const identityHeaders = await getIdentityHeaders();

    const res = await fetch(queryKey[0] as string, {
      credentials: "include",
      headers: identityHeaders,
    });

    if (unauthorizedBehavior === "returnNull" && res.status === 401) {
      return null;
    }

    await throwIfResNotOk(res);
    return await res.json();
  };

export const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      queryFn: getQueryFn({ on401: "throw" }),
      refetchInterval: false,
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
      staleTime: Infinity,
      retry: false,
    },
    mutations: {
      retry: false,
    },
  },
});

FILE: server/security/apiTokens.ts

import crypto from "crypto";
import Database from "better-sqlite3";

export type VerifiedApiToken = {
  tokenId: number;
  userId: number;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  scopes: string[];
};

function getDb() {
  return new Database("./trading_app.db");
}

function sha256Hex(input: string): string {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(input, "utf8").digest("hex");
}

export function generateRawToken(): string {
  // 64 hex chars
  return crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
}

export function extractBearerToken(req: any): string | null {
  const header = String(req?.headers?.authorization ?? "");
  const m = header.match(/^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i);
  if (m?.[1]) return m[1].trim();
  return null;
}

export function createApiToken(params: {
  userId: number;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  name?: string | null;
  scopes?: string[];
  expiresAtMs?: number | null;
}): { token: string; tokenId: number } {
  const token = generateRawToken();
  const tokenHash = sha256Hex(token);
  const now = Date.now();
  const scopesJson = JSON.stringify(params.scopes?.length ? params.scopes : ["*"]);

  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const stmt = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO api_tokens (user_id, is_admin, name, token_hash, scopes_json, created_at_ms, last_used_at_ms, expires_at_ms)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `);
    const info = stmt.run(
      params.userId,
      params.isAdmin ? 1 : 0,
      params.name ?? null,
      tokenHash,
      scopesJson,
      now,
      now,
      params.expiresAtMs ?? null
    );
    return { token, tokenId: Number(info.lastInsertRowid) };
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

export function revokeApiToken(tokenId: number, revokedByUserId?: number, reason?: string) {
  const db = getDb();
  try {
    db.prepare(`
      UPDATE api_tokens
      SET revoked_at_ms = ?, revoked_by_user_id = ?, revoke_reason = ?
      WHERE id = ?
    `).run(Date.now(), revokedByUserId ?? null, reason ?? null, tokenId);
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

export function verifyApiToken(token: string): VerifiedApiToken | null {
  if (!token || token.length < 32) return null;

  const tokenHash = sha256Hex(token);
  const now = Date.now();
  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const row = db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT id, user_id, is_admin, scopes_json, revoked_at_ms, expires_at_ms
        FROM api_tokens
        WHERE token_hash = ?
        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .get(tokenHash) as any;

    if (!row) return null;
    if (row.revoked_at_ms) return null;
    if (row.expires_at_ms && Number(row.expires_at_ms) <= now) return null;

    // Touch last_used_at_ms (best-effort)
    try {
      db.prepare(`UPDATE api_tokens SET last_used_at_ms = ? WHERE id = ?`).run(now, row.id);
    } catch {}

    let scopes: string[] = ["*"];
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(row.scopes_json || "[]");
      if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length) scopes = parsed.map(String);
    } catch {}

    return {
      tokenId: Number(row.id),
      userId: Number(row.user_id),
      isAdmin: Boolean(row.is_admin),
      scopes,
    };
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}
2) server/realtime/liveEventLog.ts
Creates the durable seq cursor event log used by /api/sync.

FILE: server/realtime/liveEventLog.ts

import Database from "better-sqlite3";

export type LiveSyncResponse = {
  nextCursor: number;
  events: Array<any & { seq: number; topic: string; userId?: number | null; createdAtMs?: number }>;
};

function getDb() {
  return new Database("./trading_app.db");
}

function safeParseJson(s: string): any {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(s);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function appendLiveEvent(params: {
  userId: number | null;
  topic: string;
  payload: any;
  createdAtMs?: number;
}): number {
  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const now = typeof params.createdAtMs === "number" ? params.createdAtMs : Date.now();
    const payloadJson = JSON.stringify(params.payload ?? {});

    const info = db
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO live_event_log (user_id, topic, payload_json, created_at_ms) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)`
      )
      .run(params.userId, params.topic, payloadJson, now);

    return Number(info.lastInsertRowid);
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

export function fetchLiveEventsSince(params: {
  userId: number;
  cursor: number;
  includeGlobal: boolean;
  topics?: string[];
  limit?: number;
}): LiveSyncResponse {
  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const cursor = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(params.cursor || 0)));
    const limit = Math.min(2000, Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(params.limit ?? 500))));

    let sql =
      "SELECT seq, user_id, topic, payload_json, created_at_ms FROM live_event_log " +
      "WHERE seq > ? AND (user_id = ?";

    const binds: any[] = [cursor, params.userId];

    if (params.includeGlobal) {
      sql += " OR user_id IS NULL";
    }
    sql += ")";

    const topics = (params.topics || []).map(s => String(s).trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (topics.length) {
      sql += ` AND topic IN (${topics.map(() => "?").join(",")})`;
      binds.push(...topics);
    }

    sql += " ORDER BY seq ASC LIMIT ?";
    binds.push(limit);

    const rows = db.prepare(sql).all(...binds) as any[];

    const events = rows.map((r) => {
      const payload = safeParseJson(String(r.payload_json || "{}")) || {};
      return {
        seq: Number(r.seq),
        topic: String(r.topic),
        userId: r.user_id === null || r.user_id === undefined ? null : Number(r.user_id),
        createdAtMs: Number(r.created_at_ms),
        ...payload,
      };
    });

    const nextCursor = rows.length ? Number(rows[rows.length - 1].seq) : cursor;
    return { nextCursor, events };
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}
3) server/realtime/wsServer.ts
This is the replacement WebSocket engine:

Auth sources:

session cookie (browser)

?token= query param (mobile)

No client WS “auth userId” required

Client “auth” message is ignored (backwards compatibility)

Tracks per-user WS clients, supports topic subscriptions

Emits events and persists USER events into live_event_log

FILE: server/realtime/wsServer.ts

import type { Server as HttpServer, IncomingMessage } from "http";
import { WebSocketServer } from "ws";
import { verifyApiToken } from "../security/apiTokens";
import { appendLiveEvent } from "./liveEventLog";

type SessionMiddleware = (req: any, res: any, next: (err?: any) => void) => void;

type WsClient = {
  ws: any;
  userId: number;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  subs: Set<string>; // topics
};

function safeJsonParse(s: string) {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(s);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function makeFakeRes() {
  return {
    getHeader: () => undefined,
    setHeader: () => {},
    end: () => {},
  } as any;
}

function urlFor(req: IncomingMessage): URL {
  const raw = req.url || "/";
  return new URL(raw, "http://localhost");
}

export type WsEmitOptions = {
  topic?: string;
  persist?: boolean;
};

export type LiveWsServer = {
  emitGlobal: (payload: any, opts?: WsEmitOptions) => void;
  emitToUser: (userId: number, payload: any, opts?: WsEmitOptions) => void;
};

export function attachWsServer(params: {
  httpServer: HttpServer;
  sessionMiddleware: SessionMiddleware;
  path?: string;
}): LiveWsServer {
  const wsPath = params.path || "/ws";
  const wss = new WebSocketServer({ noServer: true });
  const clients = new Set<WsClient>();

  function send(ws: any, obj: any) {
    if (!ws || ws.readyState !== 1) return;
    ws.send(JSON.stringify(obj));
  }

  function emitPersisted(userId: number | null, topic: string, payload: any, persist: boolean) {
    if (!persist) return { seq: undefined };
    const seq = appendLiveEvent({ userId, topic, payload });
    return { seq };
  }

  function clientWants(client: WsClient, topic: string): boolean {
    if (client.subs.has("*")) return true;
    return client.subs.has(topic);
  }

  function emitGlobal(payload: any, opts?: WsEmitOptions) {
    const topic = String(opts?.topic || payload?.topic || payload?.type || payload?.t || "global");
    const persist = opts?.persist === true; // default false for globals
    const { seq } = emitPersisted(null, topic, payload, persist);
    const out = seq ? { ...payload, seq, topic } : { ...payload, topic };

    for (const c of clients) {
      if (clientWants(c, topic) || topic === "global") {
        send(c.ws, out);
      }
    }
  }

  function emitToUser(userId: number, payload: any, opts?: WsEmitOptions) {
    const topic = String(opts?.topic || payload?.topic || payload?.type || payload?.t || "user");
    const persist = opts?.persist !== false; // default true for user-scoped events
    const { seq } = emitPersisted(userId, topic, payload, persist);
    const out = seq ? { ...payload, seq, topic } : { ...payload, topic };

    for (const c of clients) {
      if (c.userId !== userId) continue;
      if (!clientWants(c, topic)) continue;
      send(c.ws, out);
    }
  }

  params.httpServer.on("upgrade", (req: any, socket: any, head: any) => {
    try {
      const u = urlFor(req);
      if (u.pathname !== wsPath) return;

      const token = u.searchParams.get("token") || null;

      params.sessionMiddleware(req, makeFakeRes(), () => {
        let userId: number | null = null;
        let isAdmin = false;

        if (req.session?.userId) {
          userId = Number(req.session.userId);
          isAdmin = Boolean(req.session.isAdmin);
        }

        if (!userId && token) {
          const v = verifyApiToken(token);
          if (v) {
            userId = v.userId;
            isAdmin = v.isAdmin;
          }
        }

        if (!userId) {
          socket.write("HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\r\n\r\n");
          socket.destroy();
          return;
        }

        (req as any).__wsUserId = userId;
        (req as any).__wsIsAdmin = isAdmin;

        wss.handleUpgrade(req, socket, head, (ws) => {
          wss.emit("connection", ws, req);
        });
      });
    } catch {
      socket.destroy();
    }
  });

  wss.on("connection", (ws: any, req: any) => {
    const userId = Number(req.__wsUserId);
    const isAdmin = Boolean(req.__wsIsAdmin);

    const client: WsClient = {
      ws,
      userId,
      isAdmin,
      subs: new Set(["*", "global"]),
    };

    clients.add(client);

    send(ws, { type: "ws:hello", userId, isAdmin, ts: Date.now() });

    ws.on("message", (buf: any) => {
      const msg = safeJsonParse(String(buf || ""));
      if (!msg || typeof msg !== "object") return;

      if (msg.type === "auth") return;

      if (msg.type === "ping") {
        send(ws, { type: "pong", ts: Date.now() });
        return;
      }

      if (msg.type === "subscribe") {
        const topics = Array.isArray(msg.topics) ? msg.topics : [];
        for (const t of topics) {
          const topic = String(t).trim();
          if (topic) client.subs.add(topic);
        }
        send(ws, { type: "subscribed", topics: Array.from(client.subs) });
        return;
      }

      if (msg.type === "unsubscribe") {
        const topics = Array.isArray(msg.topics) ? msg.topics : [];
        for (const t of topics) {
          const topic = String(t).trim();
          if (topic && topic !== "*") client.subs.delete(topic);
        }
        send(ws, { type: "subscribed", topics: Array.from(client.subs) });
        return;
      }
    });

    ws.on("close", () => {
      clients.delete(client);
    });

    ws.on("error", () => {
      clients.delete(client);
    });
  });

  return { emitGlobal, emitToUser };
}
