1) server/security/apiTokens.ts (FULL replacement)
Adds: listApiTokensForUser() + getApiTokenById() (metadata) + revokeApiToken() improvements, keeps extractBearerToken() + verifyApiToken() + createApiToken().

// server/security/apiTokens.ts
// @ts-nocheck
import BetterSqlite3 from "better-sqlite3";
import crypto from "crypto";
import type { Request } from "express";

const DB_PATH = "./trading_app.db";

// Token format: base64url random bytes (no prefix so it stays compact)
function generateTokenString(bytes = 32): string {
  return crypto.randomBytes(bytes).toString("base64url");
}

function hashToken(token: string): string {
  // Store only a hash (never store raw token)
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(token, "utf8").digest("hex");
}

export function extractBearerToken(req: Request): string | null {
  const hdr = req.headers["authorization"];
  if (!hdr || typeof hdr !== "string") return null;
  const m = hdr.match(/^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i);
  return m?.[1]?.trim() ? m[1].trim() : null;
}

export type VerifiedApiToken = {
  tokenId: number;
  userId: number;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  scopes: string[];
  createdAtMs: number;
  lastUsedAtMs: number | null;
  expiresAtMs: number | null;
  revokedAtMs: number | null;
};

export type ApiTokenMeta = {
  id: number;
  userId: number;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  name: string | null;
  scopes: string[];
  createdAtMs: number;
  lastUsedAtMs: number | null;
  expiresAtMs: number | null;
  revokedAtMs: number | null;
  revokedByUserId: number | null;
  revokeReason: string | null;
};

function parseScopes(scopesJson: string | null | undefined): string[] {
  if (!scopesJson) return [];
  try {
    const v = JSON.parse(scopesJson);
    return Array.isArray(v) ? v.map(String) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function db() {
  return new BetterSqlite3(DB_PATH);
}

export function createApiToken(params: {
  userId: number;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  name?: string | null;
  scopes?: string[];
  expiresInDays?: number | null; // null => no expiry
}): { token: string; meta: ApiTokenMeta } {
  const now = Date.now();
  const expiresAtMs =
    params.expiresInDays && Number(params.expiresInDays) > 0
      ? now + Number(params.expiresInDays) * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
      : null;

  const token = generateTokenString(32);
  const tokenHash = hashToken(token);

  const conn = db();
  try {
    const stmt = conn.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO api_tokens (
        user_id, is_admin, name, token_hash, scopes_json,
        created_at_ms, last_used_at_ms, expires_at_ms,
        revoked_at_ms, revoked_by_user_id, revoke_reason
      ) VALUES (
        @user_id, @is_admin, @name, @token_hash, @scopes_json,
        @created_at_ms, @last_used_at_ms, @expires_at_ms,
        NULL, NULL, NULL
      )
    `);

    const info = stmt.run({
      user_id: params.userId,
      is_admin: params.isAdmin ? 1 : 0,
      name: params.name ?? null,
      token_hash: tokenHash,
      scopes_json: JSON.stringify(params.scopes ?? []),
      created_at_ms: now,
      last_used_at_ms: null,
      expires_at_ms: expiresAtMs,
    });

    const meta: ApiTokenMeta = {
      id: Number(info.lastInsertRowid),
      userId: params.userId,
      isAdmin: !!params.isAdmin,
      name: params.name ?? null,
      scopes: params.scopes ?? [],
      createdAtMs: now,
      lastUsedAtMs: null,
      expiresAtMs,
      revokedAtMs: null,
      revokedByUserId: null,
      revokeReason: null,
    };

    return { token, meta };
  } finally {
    conn.close();
  }
}

export function verifyApiToken(token: string): VerifiedApiToken | null {
  const tokenHash = hashToken(token);
  const now = Date.now();

  const conn = db();
  try {
    const row = conn
      .prepare(
        `
      SELECT
        id, user_id, is_admin, scopes_json,
        created_at_ms, last_used_at_ms, expires_at_ms, revoked_at_ms
      FROM api_tokens
      WHERE token_hash = ?
      LIMIT 1
    `
      )
      .get(tokenHash);

    if (!row) return null;
    if (row.revoked_at_ms != null) return null;
    if (row.expires_at_ms != null && Number(row.expires_at_ms) <= now) return null;

    // Touch last_used_at_ms
    try {
      conn
        .prepare(`UPDATE api_tokens SET last_used_at_ms = ? WHERE id = ?`)
        .run(now, row.id);
    } catch {
      // non-fatal
    }

    return {
      tokenId: Number(row.id),
      userId: Number(row.user_id),
      isAdmin: !!row.is_admin,
      scopes: parseScopes(row.scopes_json),
      createdAtMs: Number(row.created_at_ms),
      lastUsedAtMs: now,
      expiresAtMs: row.expires_at_ms != null ? Number(row.expires_at_ms) : null,
      revokedAtMs: row.revoked_at_ms != null ? Number(row.revoked_at_ms) : null,
    };
  } finally {
    conn.close();
  }
}

export function listApiTokensForUser(userId: number, opts?: { includeRevoked?: boolean }): ApiTokenMeta[] {
  const includeRevoked = !!opts?.includeRevoked;
  const conn = db();
  try {
    const rows = conn
      .prepare(
        `
      SELECT
        id, user_id, is_admin, name, scopes_json,
        created_at_ms, last_used_at_ms, expires_at_ms,
        revoked_at_ms, revoked_by_user_id, revoke_reason
      FROM api_tokens
      WHERE user_id = ?
        ${includeRevoked ? "" : "AND revoked_at_ms IS NULL"}
      ORDER BY created_at_ms DESC
    `
      )
      .all(userId);

    return (rows ?? []).map((r: any) => ({
      id: Number(r.id),
      userId: Number(r.user_id),
      isAdmin: !!r.is_admin,
      name: r.name ?? null,
      scopes: parseScopes(r.scopes_json),
      createdAtMs: Number(r.created_at_ms),
      lastUsedAtMs: r.last_used_at_ms != null ? Number(r.last_used_at_ms) : null,
      expiresAtMs: r.expires_at_ms != null ? Number(r.expires_at_ms) : null,
      revokedAtMs: r.revoked_at_ms != null ? Number(r.revoked_at_ms) : null,
      revokedByUserId: r.revoked_by_user_id != null ? Number(r.revoked_by_user_id) : null,
      revokeReason: r.revoke_reason ?? null,
    }));
  } finally {
    conn.close();
  }
}

export function getApiTokenById(tokenId: number): ApiTokenMeta | null {
  const conn = db();
  try {
    const r = conn
      .prepare(
        `
      SELECT
        id, user_id, is_admin, name, scopes_json,
        created_at_ms, last_used_at_ms, expires_at_ms,
        revoked_at_ms, revoked_by_user_id, revoke_reason
      FROM api_tokens
      WHERE id = ?
      LIMIT 1
    `
      )
      .get(tokenId);

    if (!r) return null;

    return {
      id: Number(r.id),
      userId: Number(r.user_id),
      isAdmin: !!r.is_admin,
      name: r.name ?? null,
      scopes: parseScopes(r.scopes_json),
      createdAtMs: Number(r.created_at_ms),
      lastUsedAtMs: r.last_used_at_ms != null ? Number(r.last_used_at_ms) : null,
      expiresAtMs: r.expires_at_ms != null ? Number(r.expires_at_ms) : null,
      revokedAtMs: r.revoked_at_ms != null ? Number(r.revoked_at_ms) : null,
      revokedByUserId: r.revoked_by_user_id != null ? Number(r.revoked_by_user_id) : null,
      revokeReason: r.revoke_reason ?? null,
    };
  } finally {
    conn.close();
  }
}

export function revokeApiToken(params: {
  tokenId: number;
  revokedByUserId: number;
  reason?: string | null;
}): boolean {
  const now = Date.now();
  const conn = db();
  try {
    const info = conn
      .prepare(
        `
      UPDATE api_tokens
      SET revoked_at_ms = ?, revoked_by_user_id = ?, revoke_reason = ?
      WHERE id = ? AND revoked_at_ms IS NULL
    `
      )
      .run(now, params.revokedByUserId, params.reason ?? null, params.tokenId);

    return info.changes > 0;
  } finally {
    conn.close();
  }
}
2) server/security/attachBearerAuth.ts (NEW)
Middleware that does not create sessions. It only sets req.auth when a valid bearer token is present.

// server/security/attachBearerAuth.ts
// @ts-nocheck
import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { extractBearerToken, verifyApiToken } from "./apiTokens";

export type AuthContext =
  | { method: "session"; userId: number; isAdmin: boolean; sessionId: string }
  | { method: "token"; userId: number; isAdmin: boolean; tokenId: number; scopes: string[] };

declare global {
  namespace Express {
    interface Request {
      auth?: AuthContext;
    }
  }
}

export function attachBearerAuth(req: Request, _res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  // If already set (some upstream), keep it.
  if ((req as any).auth?.method === "token") return next();

  const token = extractBearerToken(req);
  if (!token) return next();

  const verified = verifyApiToken(token);
  if (!verified) return next();

  req.auth = {
    method: "token",
    userId: verified.userId,
    isAdmin: verified.isAdmin,
    tokenId: verified.tokenId,
    scopes: verified.scopes ?? [],
  };

  return next();
}
3) server/realtime/live.ts (NEW)

// server/realtime/live.ts
// @ts-nocheck
import type { LiveWsServer } from "./wsServer";

let _live: LiveWsServer | null = null;

export function setLiveServer(live: LiveWsServer) {
  _live = live;
}

export function emitUserEvent(userId: number, payload: any, opts?: any) {
  if (!_live) return;
  _live.emitToUser(userId, payload, opts);
}

export function emitGlobalEvent(payload: any, opts?: any) {
  if (!_live) return;
  _live.emitGlobal(payload, opts);
}

4B) Update ensureAuth to accept bearer tokens + standardize req.auth
Find your current ensureAuth function and replace it with this version:

const ensureAuth = async (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
  try {
    // If bearer auth already attached, accept it.
    if (req.auth?.method === "token") {
      return next();
    }

    // Session auth path
    const userId = req.session?.userId;
    if (!userId) {
      return res.status(401).json({ message: "Not authenticated" });
    }

    // Session revocation guard (existing logic)
    try {
      const sessionDb = new BetterSqlite3("./trading_app.db");
      try {
        const sessionId = req.sessionID;
        const row = sessionDb
          .prepare("SELECT revoked_at_ms FROM user_sessions WHERE session_id = ? AND user_id = ? LIMIT 1")
          .get(sessionId, userId);

        if (row && row.revoked_at_ms != null) {
          req.session.destroy(() => {});
          return res.status(401).json({ message: "Session revoked" });
        }

        // Touch session activity (requires correct import)
        touchSession(sessionId);
      } finally {
        sessionDb.close();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Non-fatal: do not block login due to session trail failures
      console.warn("Session revocation check failed:", e);
    }

    // Attach auth context for downstream handlers (new canonical source)
    req.auth = {
      method: "session",
      userId: Number(userId),
      isAdmin: !!req.session?.isAdmin,
      sessionId: req.sessionID,
    };

    return next();
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("ensureAuth error:", err);
    return res.status(500).json({ message: "Auth middleware error" });
  }
};
4C) Add token endpoints (issue/list/revoke)

// ISSUE a token (SESSION ONLY: prevents token→token minting loops)
app.post("/api/auth/tokens", ensureAuth, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    if (req.auth?.method !== "session") {
      return res.status(403).json({ message: "Token issuance requires a session login (web)."} );
    }

    const name = typeof req.body?.name === "string" ? req.body.name.trim() : null;
    const expiresInDays =
      req.body?.expiresInDays == null ? null : Number(req.body.expiresInDays);

    const { token, meta } = createApiToken({
      userId: req.auth.userId,
      isAdmin: !!req.auth.isAdmin,
      name,
      scopes: Array.isArray(req.body?.scopes) ? req.body.scopes.map(String) : [],
      expiresInDays: Number.isFinite(expiresInDays) ? expiresInDays : null,
    });

    // Return the raw token ONCE (store it client-side)
    return res.json({ token, meta });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Issue token error:", e);
    return res.status(500).json({ message: "Failed to issue token" });
  }
});

// LIST tokens (session OR bearer)
app.get("/api/auth/tokens", ensureAuth, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const includeRevoked = req.query?.includeRevoked === "1";
    const tokens = listApiTokensForUser(req.auth!.userId, { includeRevoked });
    return res.json(tokens);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("List tokens error:", e);
    return res.status(500).json({ message: "Failed to list tokens" });
  }
});

// REVOKE a token (owner can revoke; admin rules can be added later)
app.post("/api/auth/tokens/:id/revoke", ensureAuth, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const tokenId = Number(req.params.id);
    if (!Number.isFinite(tokenId)) {
      return res.status(400).json({ message: "Invalid token id" });
    }

    const meta = getApiTokenById(tokenId);
    if (!meta) return res.status(404).json({ message: "Token not found" });

    // Only owner (for now). If you want admin revoke-any, extend here.
    if (meta.userId !== req.auth!.userId) {
      return res.status(403).json({ message: "Not authorized to revoke this token" });
    }

    const reason = typeof req.body?.reason === "string" ? req.body.reason.trim() : null;

    const ok = revokeApiToken({
      tokenId,
      revokedByUserId: req.auth!.userId,
      reason,
    });

    return res.json({ success: ok });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Revoke token error:", e);
    return res.status(500).json({ message: "Failed to revoke token" });
  }
});

Part 7C-1 — Canonical live topics + scope mapping (NEW)
shared/liveTopics.ts (NEW file)
// shared/liveTopics.ts
export const LIVE_TOPICS = {
  // Global (all clients may subscribe)
  QUOTES_UPDATED: "quotes:updated",
  SYSTEM_BANNER: "system:banner",

  // User-scoped (only the authenticated user receives these)
  TRADES_UPDATED: "trades:updated",
  ACCOUNT_UPDATED: "account:updated",
  ORDERS_UPDATED: "orders:updated",

  // Admin-only global streams (topic prefix "admin:")
  ADMIN_AUDIT_UPDATED: "admin:audit:updated",
  ADMIN_USERS_UPDATED: "admin:users:updated",
} as const;

export type LiveTopic = (typeof LIVE_TOPICS)[keyof typeof LIVE_TOPICS];

// WS scopes (token clients)
export const WS_TOPIC_SCOPES: Record<string, string[]> = {
  [LIVE_TOPICS.QUOTES_UPDATED]: ["ws:quotes", "ws:*", "*"],
  [LIVE_TOPICS.SYSTEM_BANNER]: ["ws:system", "ws:*", "*"],

  [LIVE_TOPICS.TRADES_UPDATED]: ["ws:trades", "ws:*", "*"],
  [LIVE_TOPICS.ACCOUNT_UPDATED]: ["ws:account", "ws:*", "*"],
  [LIVE_TOPICS.ORDERS_UPDATED]: ["ws:orders", "ws:*", "*"],

  // Admin topics require BOTH: isAdmin + scope
  [LIVE_TOPICS.ADMIN_AUDIT_UPDATED]: ["ws:admin", "ws:*", "*"],
  [LIVE_TOPICS.ADMIN_USERS_UPDATED]: ["ws:admin", "ws:*", "*"],
};

// REST scopes (token clients)
export const REST_SCOPES: Record<string, string[]> = {
  // Reads
  "GET:/api/account/summary": ["read:account", "read:*", "*"],
  "GET:/api/trades": ["read:trades", "read:*", "*"],
  "GET:/api/trades/open": ["read:trades", "read:*", "*"],
  "GET:/api/sync": ["sync:read", "read:*", "*"],

  // Writes
  "POST:/api/trades": ["write:orders", "write:*", "*"],
  "POST:/api/trades/:id/close": ["write:orders", "write:*", "*"],
  "POST:/api/trades/:id/update": ["write:orders", "write:*", "*"],
};
Part 7C-2 — Scope utilities + middleware (NEW)
server/security/scopeUtils.ts (NEW file)
// server/security/scopeUtils.ts
// @ts-nocheck
export function normalizeScopes(scopes: any): string[] {
  const arr = Array.isArray(scopes) ? scopes.map(String) : [];
  // Back-compat safety: if token scopes are empty, treat as full access for now.
  // If you want strict behavior later, change this to: return arr;
  return arr.length ? arr : ["*"];
}

export function hasAnyScope(scopes: any, required: string | string[]): boolean {
  const s = new Set(normalizeScopes(scopes));
  const req = Array.isArray(required) ? required : [required];

  if (s.has("*")) return true;

  for (const r of req) {
    if (s.has(r)) return true;
  }

  // Support wildcards like "ws:*", "read:*", "write:*"
  for (const r of req) {
    const [prefix] = String(r).split(":");
    if (prefix && s.has(`${prefix}:*`)) return true;
  }

  return false;
}
server/security/requireScope.ts (NEW file)
// server/security/requireScope.ts
// @ts-nocheck
import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { hasAnyScope } from "./scopeUtils";

export function requireScope(required: string | string[], opts?: { allowSession?: boolean }) {
  const allowSession = opts?.allowSession !== false; // default true

  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const auth = (req as any).auth;

    // Sessions are full-trust inside your platform boundary
    if (auth?.method === "session") {
      if (allowSession) return next();
      return res.status(403).json({ message: "Session not permitted for this endpoint" });
    }

    if (auth?.method !== "token") {
      return res.status(401).json({ message: "Not authenticated" });
    }

    if (!hasAnyScope(auth.scopes, required)) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        message: "Insufficient token scope",
        required,
      });
    }

    return next();
  };
}
Part 7C-3 — Live event log hardening + cursor-too-old detection + pruning (FULL replacement)
server/realtime/liveEventLog.ts (FULL replacement)
// server/realtime/liveEventLog.ts
// @ts-nocheck
import Database from "better-sqlite3";

function getDb() {
  return new Database("./trading_app.db");
}

function safeParseJson(s: string): any {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(s);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function appendLiveEvent(params: {
  userId: number | null;
  topic: string;
  payload: any;
  createdAtMs?: number;
}): number {
  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const now = typeof params.createdAtMs === "number" ? params.createdAtMs : Date.now();
    const payloadJson = JSON.stringify(params.payload ?? {});
    const info = db
      .prepare(`INSERT INTO live_event_log (user_id, topic, payload_json, created_at_ms) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)`)
      .run(params.userId, params.topic, payloadJson, now);
    return Number(info.lastInsertRowid);
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

export type LiveSyncResponse = {
  serverTimeMs: number;
  minCursor: number;      // earliest seq currently retained
  nextCursor: number;     // last seq returned (or requested cursor if none)
  events: Array<any & { seq: number; topic: string; userId: number | null; createdAtMs: number }>;
};

export function getMinLiveCursor(): number {
  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const row = db.prepare(`SELECT MIN(seq) AS minSeq FROM live_event_log`).get() as any;
    const v = row?.minSeq == null ? 0 : Number(row.minSeq);
    return Number.isFinite(v) ? v : 0;
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

export function fetchLiveEventsSince(params: {
  userId: number;
  cursor: number;
  includeGlobal: boolean;
  includeAdmin: boolean;
  topics?: string[];
  limit?: number;
}): LiveSyncResponse {
  const db = getDb();
  try {
    const serverTimeMs = Date.now();
    const cursor = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(params.cursor || 0)));
    const limit = Math.min(2000, Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(params.limit ?? 500))));
    const includeGlobal = !!params.includeGlobal;
    const includeAdmin = !!params.includeAdmin;

    const minCursor = getMinLiveCursor();

    let sql =
      "SELECT seq, user_id, topic, payload_json, created_at_ms FROM live_event_log " +
      "WHERE seq > ? AND (user_id = ?";

    const binds: any[] = [cursor, params.userId];

    if (includeGlobal) {
      sql += " OR user_id IS NULL";
    }

    sql += ")";

    // Admin stream separation: admin topics are stored with user_id NULL but topic prefix "admin:"
    if (!includeAdmin) {
      sql += " AND topic NOT LIKE 'admin:%'";
    }

    const topics = (params.topics || []).map((s) => String(s).trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (topics.length) {
      sql += ` AND topic IN (${topics.map(() => "?").join(",")})`;
      binds.push(...topics);
    }

    sql += " ORDER BY seq ASC LIMIT ?";
    binds.push(limit);

    const rows = db.prepare(sql).all(...binds) as any[];

    const events = (rows || []).map((r) => {
      const payload = safeParseJson(String(r.payload_json || "{}")) || {};
      return {
        seq: Number(r.seq),
        topic: String(r.topic),
        userId: r.user_id == null ? null : Number(r.user_id),
        createdAtMs: Number(r.created_at_ms),
        ...payload,
      };
    });

    const nextCursor = rows.length ? Number(rows[rows.length - 1].seq) : cursor;
    return { serverTimeMs, minCursor, nextCursor, events };
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

export function pruneLiveEventLog(params: { maxAgeDays?: number; maxRows?: number }): { deleted: number } {
  const maxAgeDays = Number(params.maxAgeDays ?? 7);
  const maxRows = Number(params.maxRows ?? 200000);

  const db = getDb();
  try {
    let deleted = 0;

    // Age-based prune
    if (Number.isFinite(maxAgeDays) && maxAgeDays > 0) {
      const cutoff = Date.now() - maxAgeDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
      const info = db.prepare(`DELETE FROM live_event_log WHERE created_at_ms < ?`).run(cutoff);
      deleted += info.changes || 0;
    }

    // Row-count prune (keep newest maxRows)
    if (Number.isFinite(maxRows) && maxRows > 0) {
      const row = db
        .prepare(`SELECT seq FROM live_event_log ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1 OFFSET ?`)
        .get(maxRows) as any;

      if (row?.seq != null) {
        const threshold = Number(row.seq);
        const info = db.prepare(`DELETE FROM live_event_log WHERE seq < ?`).run(threshold);
        deleted += info.changes || 0;
      }
    }

    return { deleted };
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}
Part 7C-4 — WS server hardening: scopes + admin topics + safe defaults (FULL replacement)
server/realtime/wsServer.ts (FULL replacement)
// server/realtime/wsServer.ts
// @ts-nocheck
import type { Server as HttpServer, IncomingMessage } from "http";
import { WebSocketServer } from "ws";
import { verifyApiToken } from "../security/apiTokens";
import { appendLiveEvent } from "./liveEventLog";
import { hasAnyScope, normalizeScopes } from "../security/scopeUtils";
import { WS_TOPIC_SCOPES } from "@shared/liveTopics";

type SessionMiddleware = (req: any, res: any, next: (err?: any) => void) => void;

type WsClient = {
  ws: any;
  userId: number;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  authMethod: "session" | "token";
  scopes: string[];          // token scopes or ["*"] for session
  subs: Set<string>;         // topics
};

function safeJsonParse(s: string) {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(s);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function makeFakeRes() {
  return {
    getHeader: () => undefined,
    setHeader: () => {},
    end: () => {},
  } as any;
}

function urlFor(req: IncomingMessage): URL {
  const raw = req.url || "/";
  return new URL(raw, "http://localhost");
}

export type WsEmitOptions = {
  topic?: string;
  persist?: boolean;
};

export type LiveWsServer = {
  emitGlobal: (payload: any, opts?: WsEmitOptions) => void;
  emitToUser: (userId: number, payload: any, opts?: WsEmitOptions) => void;
};

function topicFromPayload(payload: any, opts?: WsEmitOptions): string {
  return String(opts?.topic || payload?.topic || payload?.type || payload?.t || "global");
}

function isAdminTopic(topic: string): boolean {
  return String(topic).startsWith("admin:");
}

function canSubscribe(client: WsClient, topic: string): boolean {
  topic = String(topic);

  if (topic === "global") return true;

  if (isAdminTopic(topic)) {
    if (!client.isAdmin) return false;
    // admin topics still require ws:admin scope for token auth
    if (client.authMethod === "token") {
      const required = WS_TOPIC_SCOPES[topic] || ["ws:admin", "ws:*", "*"];
      return hasAnyScope(client.scopes, required);
    }
    return true; // session admin
  }

  // Non-admin topics
  if (client.authMethod === "session") return true; // session can subscribe freely

  const required = WS_TOPIC_SCOPES[topic] || ["ws:*", "*"];
  return hasAnyScope(client.scopes, required);
}

export function attachWsServer(params: {
  httpServer: HttpServer;
  sessionMiddleware: SessionMiddleware;
  path?: string;
}): LiveWsServer {
  const wsPath = params.path || "/ws";
  const wss = new WebSocketServer({ noServer: true });
  const clients = new Set<WsClient>();

  function send(ws: any, obj: any) {
    if (!ws || ws.readyState !== 1) return;
    ws.send(JSON.stringify(obj));
  }

  function persistIfNeeded(userId: number | null, topic: string, payload: any, persist: boolean) {
    if (!persist) return { seq: undefined };
    const seq = appendLiveEvent({ userId, topic, payload });
    return { seq };
  }

  function clientWants(client: WsClient, topic: string): boolean {
    if (client.subs.has("*")) return true;
    return client.subs.has(topic) || (topic === "global" && client.subs.has("global"));
  }

  function emitGlobal(payload: any, opts?: WsEmitOptions) {
    const topic = topicFromPayload(payload, opts);
    const persist = opts?.persist === true; // default false for global streams
    const { seq } = persistIfNeeded(null, topic, payload, persist);
    const out = seq ? { ...payload, seq, topic } : { ...payload, topic };

    for (const c of clients) {
      // Admin topics should only reach admin subscribers
      if (isAdminTopic(topic) && !c.isAdmin) continue;
      if (!clientWants(c, topic) && topic !== "global") continue;
      send(c.ws, out);
    }
  }

  function emitToUser(userId: number, payload: any, opts?: WsEmitOptions) {
    const topic = topicFromPayload(payload, opts);
    const persist = opts?.persist !== false; // default true for user streams
    const { seq } = persistIfNeeded(userId, topic, payload, persist);
    const out = seq ? { ...payload, seq, topic } : { ...payload, topic };

    for (const c of clients) {
      if (c.userId !== userId) continue;
      if (!clientWants(c, topic)) continue;
      send(c.ws, out);
    }
  }

  params.httpServer.on("upgrade", (req: any, socket: any, head: any) => {
    try {
      const u = urlFor(req);
      if (u.pathname !== wsPath) return;

      const token = u.searchParams.get("token") || null;

      params.sessionMiddleware(req, makeFakeRes(), () => {
        let userId: number | null = null;
        let isAdmin = false;
        let authMethod: "session" | "token" = "session";
        let scopes: string[] = ["*"];

        if (req.session?.userId) {
          userId = Number(req.session.userId);
          isAdmin = Boolean(req.session.isAdmin);
          authMethod = "session";
          scopes = ["*"];
        }

        if (!userId && token) {
          const v = verifyApiToken(token);
          if (v) {
            userId = v.userId;
            isAdmin = v.isAdmin;
            authMethod = "token";
            scopes = normalizeScopes(v.scopes);
          }
        }

        if (!userId) {
          socket.write("HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\r\n\r\n");
          socket.destroy();
          return;
        }

        req.__wsUserId = userId;
        req.__wsIsAdmin = isAdmin;
        req.__wsAuthMethod = authMethod;
        req.__wsScopes = scopes;

        wss.handleUpgrade(req, socket, head, (ws) => {
          wss.emit("connection", ws, req);
        });
      });
    } catch {
      socket.destroy();
    }
  });

  wss.on("connection", (ws: any, req: any) => {
    const userId = Number(req.__wsUserId);
    const isAdmin = Boolean(req.__wsIsAdmin);
    const authMethod = (req.__wsAuthMethod as any) || "session";
    const scopes = normalizeScopes(req.__wsScopes);

    // Safer defaults:
    // - Everyone gets "global" + quotes updates
    // - User streams are opt-in via subscribe (but we keep back-compat by adding "*" for session)
    const subs = new Set<string>(["global", "quotes:updated"]);

    if (authMethod === "session") {
      subs.add("*"); // back-compat for existing web client logic
    } else {
      // token auth: add common user topics if scopes allow
      if (canSubscribe({ ws, userId, isAdmin, authMethod, scopes, subs } as any, "trades:updated")) subs.add("trades:updated");
      if (canSubscribe({ ws, userId, isAdmin, authMethod, scopes, subs } as any, "account:updated")) subs.add("account:updated");
    }

    const client: WsClient = { ws, userId, isAdmin, authMethod, scopes, subs };
    clients.add(client);

    send(ws, {
      type: "ws:hello",
      userId,
      isAdmin,
      authMethod,
      serverTimeMs: Date.now(),
      subs: Array.from(client.subs),
    });

    ws.on("message", (buf: any) => {
      const msg = safeJsonParse(String(buf || ""));
      if (!msg || typeof msg !== "object") return;

      // Back-compat: ignore old auth message
      if (msg.type === "auth") return;

      if (msg.type === "ping") {
        send(ws, { type: "pong", serverTimeMs: Date.now() });
        return;
      }

      if (msg.type === "subscribe") {
        const requested = Array.isArray(msg.topics) ? msg.topics.map(String) : [];
        const accepted: string[] = [];
        const rejected: string[] = [];

        for (const t of requested) {
          const topic = String(t).trim();
          if (!topic) continue;

          if (!canSubscribe(client, topic)) {
            rejected.push(topic);
            continue;
          }

          client.subs.add(topic);
          accepted.push(topic);
        }

        send(ws, {
          type: "subscribed",
          accepted,
          rejected,
          subs: Array.from(client.subs),
        });
        return;
      }

      if (msg.type === "unsubscribe") {
        const requested = Array.isArray(msg.topics) ? msg.topics.map(String) : [];
        for (const t of requested) {
          const topic = String(t).trim();
          if (!topic) continue;
          if (topic === "*" && client.authMethod === "session") continue; // keep back-compat wildcard for web
          if (topic === "global") continue; // always keep global
          client.subs.delete(topic);
        }
        send(ws, { type: "subscribed", subs: Array.from(client.subs) });
        return;
      }
    });

    ws.on("close", () => clients.delete(client));
    ws.on("error", () => clients.delete(client));
  });

  return { emitGlobal, emitToUser };
}
Part 7C-5 — Prune cron for live_event_log (NEW)
server/cron/liveEventPrune.ts (NEW file)
// server/cron/liveEventPrune.ts
// @ts-nocheck
import * as cron from "node-cron";
import { pruneLiveEventLog } from "../realtime/liveEventLog";
import { log } from "../vite";

const SCHEDULE = process.env.LIVE_EVENT_PRUNE_SCHEDULE || "0 * * * *"; // hourly
const RETENTION_DAYS = Number(process.env.LIVE_EVENT_RETENTION_DAYS ?? 7);
const MAX_ROWS = Number(process.env.LIVE_EVENT_MAX_ROWS ?? 200000);

export function startLiveEventPruneCron() {
  log(`[LiveEventPrune] Starting cron (${SCHEDULE}), retentionDays=${RETENTION_DAYS}, maxRows=${MAX_ROWS}`);

  cron.schedule(SCHEDULE, async () => {
    try {
      const res = pruneLiveEventLog({ maxAgeDays: RETENTION_DAYS, maxRows: MAX_ROWS });
      if (res.deleted > 0) log(`[LiveEventPrune] Deleted ${res.deleted} old live events`);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("[LiveEventPrune] Error:", e);
    }
  });
}
Part 7C-6 — /api/sync hardening: admin gating + cursor-too-old response + topic allowlist
server/routes.ts — Patch block for /api/sync
Replace your existing /api/sync handler with this hardened version.

Key changes:

Supports includeAdmin=1 only if req.auth.isAdmin

Filters requested topics against token scopes (token callers can’t request admin topics unless admin+scope)

Returns 410 if cursor is too old (pruned) with { minCursor }

import { fetchLiveEventsSince } from "./realtime/liveEventLog";
import { requireScope } from "./security/requireScope";
import { REST_SCOPES } from "@shared/liveTopics";
import { hasAnyScope } from "./security/scopeUtils";
import { WS_TOPIC_SCOPES } from "@shared/liveTopics";

// ...

app.get(["/api/sync", "/api/v1/sync"],
  ensureAuth,
  requireScope(REST_SCOPES["GET:/api/sync"]),
  (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    const userId = req.auth!.userId;

    const cursor = Number((req.query as any)?.cursor ?? 0) || 0;
    const limit = Math.min(2000, Math.max(1, Number((req.query as any)?.limit ?? 500) || 500));

    const includeGlobal = String((req.query as any)?.includeGlobal ?? "1") !== "0";
    const includeAdminRequested = String((req.query as any)?.includeAdmin ?? "0") === "1";

    const includeAdmin = includeAdminRequested && !!req.auth!.isAdmin;

    const topicsParam = (req.query as any)?.topics;
    let topics = typeof topicsParam === "string" && topicsParam.trim()
      ? topicsParam.split(",").map((s: string) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
      : undefined;

    // Token callers: filter topics by scope; session callers: allow.
    if (topics && req.auth?.method === "token") {
      const scopes = req.auth.scopes || [];
      topics = topics.filter((t) => {
        // admin topics require admin + ws:admin scope
        if (String(t).startsWith("admin:")) {
          if (!includeAdmin) return false;
          const reqScopes = WS_TOPIC_SCOPES[t] || ["ws:admin", "ws:*", "*"];
          return hasAnyScope(scopes, reqScopes);
        }
        const reqScopes = WS_TOPIC_SCOPES[t] || ["ws:*", "*"];
        return hasAnyScope(scopes, reqScopes);
      });
      if (!topics.length) topics = undefined;
    }

    const result = fetchLiveEventsSince({
      userId,
      cursor,
      includeGlobal,
      includeAdmin,
      topics,
      limit,
    });

    // Cursor too old: if client cursor predates retained minCursor
    if (cursor > 0 && result.minCursor > 0 && cursor < (result.minCursor - 1)) {
      return res.status(410).json({
        message: "Cursor too old (events pruned). Client must resync from a fresh snapshot.",
        error: "CURSOR_TOO_OLD",
        minCursor: result.minCursor,
        serverTimeMs: result.serverTimeMs,
      });
    }

    return res.json(result);
  }
);

1) client/src/lib/authToken.ts (NEW)
const AUTH_TOKEN_KEY = "auth.token";

/**
 * Token auth is optional and co-exists with session cookies.
 *
 * - Web (default): session cookie auth only
 * - Mobile/native: uses Bearer token (stored here) + WS token query param
 */
export function getAuthToken(): string | null {
  try {
    const t = window.localStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
    return t && t.trim().length > 0 ? t : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function setAuthToken(token: string): void {
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, token);
  } catch {
    // ignore (private mode, etc.)
  }
}

export function clearAuthToken(): void {
  try {
    window.localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}
2) client/src/lib/httpBootstrap.ts (NEW)
This makes fetch() + axios automatically include:

cookies (credentials: "include")

identity headers

Authorization: Bearer <token> when auth.token exists

import axios from "axios";
import { getAuthToken } from "./authToken";
import { getIdentityHeaders } from "./identity";

/**
 * Ensures BOTH fetch() and axios() automatically:
 * - include session cookies (credentials)
 * - attach device identity headers
 * - attach Authorization: Bearer <token> when token auth is present
 *
 * This is the "client base truth" for HTTP auth so you don't have to refactor
 * every direct fetch()/axios() usage immediately.
 */
export function bootstrapHttpAuth() {
  // --- fetch() bootstrap
  const originalFetch = window.fetch.bind(window);

  window.fetch = async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const identity = await getIdentityHeaders();
    const token = getAuthToken();

    const headers = new Headers(init?.headers || {});
    // identity headers (do not overwrite if caller set explicitly)
    Object.entries(identity).forEach(([k, v]) => {
      if (!headers.has(k)) headers.set(k, v as string);
    });

    if (token && !headers.has("Authorization")) {
      headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${token}`);
    }

    return originalFetch(input, {
      ...init,
      headers,
      credentials: init?.credentials ?? "include",
    });
  };

  // --- axios bootstrap (admin pages use axios directly)
  axios.defaults.withCredentials = true;

  axios.interceptors.request.use(async (config) => {
    const identity = await getIdentityHeaders();
    const token = getAuthToken();

    config.headers = config.headers || {};

    // identity headers
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(identity)) {
      if (!(k in config.headers!)) {
        // axios headers type is broad; assign directly
        (config.headers as any)[k] = v;
      }
    }

    if (token && !(("Authorization" as any) in config.headers!)) {
      (config.headers as any)["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${token}`;
    }

    return config;
  });
}

4) client/src/lib/queryClient.ts (REPLACE)
Adds Authorization header if token exists (in addition to the global fetch bootstrap).

import { QueryClient, QueryFunction } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  getDeviceInstallId,
  getDeviceFingerprint,
  getClientTimezone,
  getClientLanguage,
} from "./identity";
import { getAuthToken } from "./authToken";

// Create a function that returns the identity headers
async function getIdentityHeaders() {
  return {
    "x-device-install-id": await getDeviceInstallId(),
    "x-device-fingerprint": await getDeviceFingerprint(),
    "x-client-timezone": getClientTimezone(),
    "x-client-language": getClientLanguage(),
  };
}

// Enhanced fetch function that includes identity headers
export async function apiRequest(
  method: string,
  url: string,
  data?: unknown,
  options?: RequestInit,
) {
  const identityHeaders = await getIdentityHeaders();
  const token = getAuthToken();

  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    ...identityHeaders,
    ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    ...(data ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : {}),
    ...(options?.headers ? (options.headers as Record<string, string>) : {}),
  };

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    headers,
    body: data ? JSON.stringify(data) : undefined,
    credentials: "include",
    ...options,
  });

  return res;
}

type UnauthorizedBehavior = "returnNull" | "throw";

const getQueryFn: <T>(
  unauthorizedBehavior: UnauthorizedBehavior,
) => QueryFunction<T> =
  (unauthorizedBehavior) =>
  async ({ queryKey }) => {
    const identityHeaders = await getIdentityHeaders();
    const token = getAuthToken();

    const res = await fetch(queryKey[0] as string, {
      headers: {
        ...identityHeaders,
        ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      },
      credentials: "include",
    });

    if (unauthorizedBehavior === "returnNull" && res.status === 401) {
      return null;
    }

    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Error: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    return await res.json();
  };

export const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      queryFn: getQueryFn("throw"),
      refetchInterval: false,
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
      staleTime: 1000 * 60 * 5,
      retry: false,
    },
    mutations: {
      retry: false,
    },
  },
});
5) client/src/realtime/liveCursor.ts (NEW)
const PREFIX = "live.cursor.";

function keyFor(userId: number | string | null | undefined): string {
  return `${PREFIX}${userId ?? "anon"}`;
}

export function getLiveCursor(userId: number | string | null | undefined): number {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(keyFor(userId));
    const n = raw ? Number(raw) : 0;
    return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : 0;
  } catch {
    return 0;
  }
}

export function setLiveCursor(userId: number | string | null | undefined, cursor: number): void {
  try {
    if (!Number.isFinite(cursor) || cursor <= 0) return;
    window.localStorage.setItem(keyFor(userId), String(Math.floor(cursor)));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}
6) client/src/realtime/LiveUpdatesProvider.tsx (NEW)
Single WS connection + subscribe + /api/sync catch-up + cursor persistence + query invalidation.

import React, {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { getAuthToken } from "@/lib/authToken";
import { getLiveCursor, setLiveCursor } from "./liveCursor";

type LiveEvent = Record<string, any>;

export type LiveUpdatesState = {
  isConnected: boolean;
  reconnecting: boolean;
  lastEventAt: number | null;
  cursor: number;
};

const LiveUpdatesContext = createContext<LiveUpdatesState | null>(null);

export function useLiveUpdates(): LiveUpdatesState {
  const ctx = useContext(LiveUpdatesContext);
  return (
    ctx ?? {
      isConnected: false,
      reconnecting: false,
      lastEventAt: null,
      cursor: 0,
    }
  );
}

type Props = {
  enabled: boolean;
  userId: number | null;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  children: React.ReactNode;
};

/**
 * Single, app-wide WS connection that:
 * - authenticates via session cookie OR token query param (?token=...)
 * - subscribes server-side (no client "auth userId" messages)
 * - persists cursor to localStorage
 * - calls /api/sync on (re)connect to catch up missed events
 * - invalidates react-query keys so UI stays fresh without manual refresh
 */
export function LiveUpdatesProvider({ enabled, userId, isAdmin, children }: Props) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const reconnectTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const attemptRef = useRef(0);

  const [isConnected, setIsConnected] = useState(false);
  const [reconnecting, setReconnecting] = useState(false);
  const [lastEventAt, setLastEventAt] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [cursor, setCursor] = useState<number>(() => getLiveCursor(userId));

  useEffect(() => {
    setCursor(getLiveCursor(userId));
  }, [userId]);

  const buildWsUrl = useCallback(() => {
    const proto = window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss://" : "ws://";
    const base = `${proto}${window.location.host}/ws`;

    const token = getAuthToken();
    if (!token) return base;

    const u = new URL(base);
    u.searchParams.set("token", token);
    return u.toString();
  }, []);

  const invalidateForEvent = useCallback(
    (ev: LiveEvent) => {
      const type = String(ev?.type || "");

      // Dispatch a global browser event for pages that don't use react-query
      // (e.g., certain admin screens).
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("tradehub:live-event", { detail: ev }));

      // Core invalidations (match your existing query keys)
      if (type === "quotes:updated") {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/quotes/latest"] });
        return;
      }

      if (type === "account:updated") {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/account/summary"] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/auth/current-user"] });
        return;
      }

      if (type === "trades:updated") {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades"] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades/open"] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/account/summary"] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/auth/current-user"] });
        return;
      }

      // Broad fallbacks for admin/global scopes (safe + cheap):
      if (type.startsWith("admin:") || type.startsWith("global:") || type.startsWith("audit:")) {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries();
      }
    },
    [queryClient],
  );

  const applyIncoming = useCallback(
    (ev: LiveEvent) => {
      setLastEventAt(Date.now());

      const seq = Number(ev?.seq ?? ev?.cursor ?? ev?.nextCursor ?? 0);
      if (Number.isFinite(seq) && seq > 0) {
        setCursor(seq);
        setLiveCursor(userId, seq);
      }

      invalidateForEvent(ev);
    },
    [invalidateForEvent, userId],
  );

  const runSync = useCallback(
    async (reason: string) => {
      if (!enabled) return;

      const current = getLiveCursor(userId);
      const qs = new URLSearchParams();
      qs.set("cursor", String(current));
      qs.set("includeGlobal", "1");
      if (isAdmin) qs.set("includeAdmin", "1");

      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/sync?${qs.toString()}`, {
          method: "GET",
          headers: {
            "x-sync-reason": reason,
          },
        });

        if (res.status === 401) return;

        if (res.status === 410) {
          // Cursor too old; force a clean re-fetch.
          setLiveCursor(userId, 0);
          setCursor(0);
          queryClient.invalidateQueries();
          return;
        }

        if (!res.ok) return;

        const data = (await res.json()) as {
          nextCursor?: number;
          events?: LiveEvent[];
        };

        const events = Array.isArray(data?.events) ? data.events : [];
        for (const ev of events) applyIncoming(ev);

        const next = Number(data?.nextCursor ?? 0);
        if (Number.isFinite(next) && next > 0) {
          setCursor(next);
          setLiveCursor(userId, next);
        }
      } catch {
        // ignore; WS reconnect will re-run sync
      }
    },
    [applyIncoming, enabled, isAdmin, queryClient, userId],
  );

  const closeWs = useCallback(() => {
    if (reconnectTimerRef.current) {
      window.clearTimeout(reconnectTimerRef.current);
      reconnectTimerRef.current = null;
    }
    const ws = wsRef.current;
    wsRef.current = null;
    if (ws) {
      try {
        ws.close();
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    }
    setIsConnected(false);
  }, []);

  const scheduleReconnect = useCallback(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;

    attemptRef.current += 1;
    const attempt = attemptRef.current;
    const delay = Math.min(30_000, 500 * Math.pow(2, Math.min(attempt, 6))); // up to ~32s
    const jitter = Math.floor(Math.random() * 250);

    setReconnecting(true);

    if (reconnectTimerRef.current) {
      window.clearTimeout(reconnectTimerRef.current);
    }

    reconnectTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      reconnectTimerRef.current = null;
      connect();
    }, delay + jitter);
  }, [enabled]);

  const connect = useCallback(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;

    // Avoid duplicate connections
    if (wsRef.current && (wsRef.current.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN || wsRef.current.readyState === WebSocket.CONNECTING)) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      const ws = new WebSocket(buildWsUrl());
      wsRef.current = ws;

      ws.onopen = () => {
        attemptRef.current = 0;
        setIsConnected(true);
        setReconnecting(false);

        // Server-side subscription handshake (no client auth userId)
        try {
          ws.send(
            JSON.stringify({
              type: "subscribe",
              topics: ["quotes", "account", "trades"],
              includeGlobal: true,
              includeAdmin: isAdmin,
            }),
          );
        } catch {
          // ignore
        }

        // Catch up missed events
        runSync("ws_open");
      };

      ws.onmessage = (evt) => {
        try {
          const payload = JSON.parse(evt.data);
          applyIncoming(payload);
        } catch {
          // ignore
        }
      };

      ws.onclose = () => {
        setIsConnected(false);
        if (enabled) scheduleReconnect();
      };

      ws.onerror = () => {
        // Let onclose handle reconnect
      };
    } catch {
      scheduleReconnect();
    }
  }, [applyIncoming, buildWsUrl, enabled, isAdmin, runSync, scheduleReconnect]);

  // Mount/unmount + auth change
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) {
      closeWs();
      return;
    }
    connect();
    return () => closeWs();
  }, [enabled, userId, isAdmin, connect, closeWs]);

  // Online/offline and tab visibility
  useEffect(() => {
    const onOnline = () => {
      if (enabled) connect();
    };
    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (!enabled) return;
      if (document.visibilityState === "visible") {
        // quick sync when returning to foreground
        runSync("visibility");
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener("online", onOnline);
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("online", onOnline);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
    };
  }, [connect, enabled, runSync]);

  const value = useMemo<LiveUpdatesState>(
    () => ({
      isConnected,
      reconnecting,
      lastEventAt,
      cursor,
    }),
    [cursor, isConnected, lastEventAt, reconnecting],
  );

  return <LiveUpdatesContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</LiveUpdatesContext.Provider>;
}
7) client/src/App.tsx (REPLACE)
Wires the provider into the app so there is exactly one WS connection.

import { Switch, Route } from "wouter";
import { useAuth, AuthProvider } from "@/hooks/use-auth";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster";
import NotFound from "@/pages/not-found";
import AuthPage from "@/pages/AuthPage";
import TradingDashboard from "@/pages/TradingDashboard";
import AdminDashboard from "@/pages/AdminDashboard";
import AccountScreen from "@/pages/AccountScreen";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { VerificationReminderPopup } from "@/components/VerificationReminderPopup";
import { LiveUpdatesProvider } from "@/realtime/LiveUpdatesProvider";

function AppRoutes() {
  const { user, isAuthenticated, loading } = useAuth();
  const [location, navigate] = useLocation();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading) {
      // Redirect logic
      if (!isAuthenticated && location !== "/auth") {
        navigate("/auth");
      } else if (isAuthenticated && location === "/auth") {
        navigate("/trading");
      }
    }
  }, [isAuthenticated, loading, location, navigate]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-background">
        <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-12 w-12 border-b-2 border-primary"></div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Switch>
      <Route path="/auth" component={AuthPage} />
      <Route path="/trading">
        {isAuthenticated ? <TradingDashboard /> : <AuthPage />}
      </Route>
      <Route path="/admin">
        {user?.isAdmin ? <AdminDashboard /> : <NotFound />}
      </Route>
      <Route path="/account">
        {isAuthenticated ? <AccountScreen /> : <AuthPage />}
      </Route>
      <Route component={NotFound} />
    </Switch>
  );
}

function LiveUpdatesGate({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const { user, isAuthenticated } = useAuth();

  return (
    <LiveUpdatesProvider
      enabled={isAuthenticated}
      userId={user?.id ?? null}
      isAdmin={!!user?.isAdmin}
    >
      {children}
    </LiveUpdatesProvider>
  );
}

function App() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <LiveUpdatesGate>
        <AppRoutes />
        <VerificationReminderPopup />
        <Toaster />
      </LiveUpdatesGate>
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}

export default App;
8) client/src/hooks/use-auth.tsx (REPLACE)
Adds:

token persistence if server returns auth.token or token

checkAuth({ silent: true }) background refresh without UI flicker

removes aggressive 2s polling; replaces with a 30s silent refresh (safe baseline)

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback, useEffect } from "react";
import { apiRequest } from "@/lib/queryClient";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { clearAuthToken, setAuthToken } from "@/lib/authToken";

interface User {
  id: number;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  balance: number;
  equity: number;
  isAdmin?: boolean;
  isEmailVerified?: boolean;
  isPhoneVerified?: boolean;
  isFrozen?: boolean;
  isDisabled?: boolean;
  isKycVerified?: boolean;
  isImpersonating?: boolean;
  originalAdminId?: number;
}

interface AuthContextType {
  user: User | null;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  loading: boolean;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  register: (username: string, email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  checkAuth: (opts?: { silent?: boolean }) => Promise<void>;
  impersonateUser: (userId: number) => Promise<void>;
  stopImpersonating: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | null>(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const checkAuth = useCallback(async (opts?: { silent?: boolean }) => {
    const silent = !!opts?.silent;

    try {
      if (!silent) setLoading(true);

      const res = await apiRequest("GET", "/api/auth/current-user");
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Not authenticated");

      const data = await res.json();
      setUser(data);
    } catch {
      setUser(null);
    } finally {
      if (!silent) setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  // Initial load
  useEffect(() => {
    checkAuth();
  }, [checkAuth]);

  // Safety net: periodic silent refresh (WS should handle most updates)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;

    const t = window.setInterval(() => {
      checkAuth({ silent: true });
    }, 30_000);

    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, [user?.id, checkAuth]);

  const extractAndStoreToken = (data: any) => {
    const token = (data?.auth?.token ?? data?.token) as string | undefined;
    if (token && typeof token === "string" && token.trim().length > 0) {
      setAuthToken(token.trim());
    }
  };

  const login = async (email: string, password: string) => {
    const res = await apiRequest("POST", "/api/auth/login", { email, password });
    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(err?.message || "Login failed");
    }
    const data = await res.json();
    extractAndStoreToken(data);
    setUser(data?.user ?? data);
  };

  const register = async (username: string, email: string, password: string) => {
    const res = await apiRequest("POST", "/api/auth/register", { username, email, password });
    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(err?.message || "Registration failed");
    }
    const data = await res.json();
    extractAndStoreToken(data);
    setUser(data?.user ?? data);
  };

  const logout = async () => {
    try {
      await apiRequest("POST", "/api/auth/logout");
    } finally {
      clearAuthToken();
      setUser(null);
      queryClient.clear();
    }
  };

  const impersonateUser = async (userId: number) => {
    const res = await apiRequest("POST", "/api/admin/impersonate", { userId });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Failed to impersonate");
    const data = await res.json();
    setUser(data);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries();
  };

  const stopImpersonating = async () => {
    const res = await apiRequest("POST", "/api/admin/stop-impersonating");
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Failed to stop impersonating");
    const data = await res.json();
    setUser(data);
    queryClient.invalidateQueries();
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        isAuthenticated: !!user,
        loading,
        login,
        register,
        logout,
        checkAuth,
        impersonateUser,
        stopImpersonating,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useAuth must be used within AuthProvider");
  return ctx;
}

9) client/src/hooks/use-trades.tsx (REPLACE)
Removes per-hook WS and adds clientRequestId (idempotency key) into order placement payload.

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { apiRequest } from "@/lib/queryClient";
import { useAuth } from "./use-auth";
import { useToast } from "./use-toast";
import type { Trade, InsertTrade } from "@shared/schema";

function createClientRequestId(): string {
  // Works on modern browsers; fallback for older.
  const anyCrypto = (globalThis as any).crypto;
  if (anyCrypto?.randomUUID) return anyCrypto.randomUUID();
  return `crid_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}`;
}

export function useTrades() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { toast } = useToast();

  const {
    data: trades = [],
    isLoading,
    error,
  } = useQuery<Trade[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/trades"],
    enabled: !!user,
  });

  const createTrade = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (data: {
      symbolId: number;
      type: "BUY" | "SELL";
      size: number;
      openPrice: number;
      takeProfit?: number;
      stopLoss?: number;
    }) => {
      // Idempotency key (critical for native/mobile reliability on flaky networks)
      const payload = { ...data, clientRequestId: createClientRequestId() };
      return apiRequest("POST", "/api/trades", payload);
    },
    onSuccess: async (res) => {
      if (!res.ok) {
        const error = await res.json();
        throw new Error(error.message || "Failed to create trade");
      }

      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades"] });
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/account/summary"] });

      toast({
        title: "Trade Created",
        description: "Your trade has been successfully created.",
      });
    },
    onError: (error) => {
      toast({
        title: "Trade Error",
        description: error.message,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    },
  });

  const closeTrade = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (tradeId: number) => {
      return apiRequest("POST", `/api/trades/${tradeId}/close`);
    },
    onSuccess: async (res) => {
      if (!res.ok) {
        const error = await res.json();
        throw new Error(error.message || "Failed to close trade");
      }

      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades"] });
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/account/summary"] });

      toast({
        title: "Trade Closed",
        description: "Your trade has been successfully closed.",
      });
    },
    onError: (error) => {
      toast({
        title: "Close Error",
        description: error.message,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    },
  });

  return {
    trades,
    isLoading,
    error,
    createTrade,
    closeTrade,
  };
}
10) client/src/hooks/use-quotes.tsx (REPLACE)
Stops creating WS connections and uses LiveUpdatesProvider’s connection state to disable polling when WS is healthy.

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useLiveUpdates } from "@/realtime/LiveUpdatesProvider";
import type { Quote } from "@shared/schema";

export function useQuotes() {
  const [quotes, setQuotes] = useState<Quote[]>([]);
  const [isConnected, setIsConnected] = useState(false);

  const { isConnected: isWsConnected } = useLiveUpdates();

  // Get latest quotes with polling fallback only when WS is not connected
  const { data: latestQuotes = [], error } = useQuery<Quote[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/quotes/latest"],
    refetchInterval: isWsConnected ? false : 5000,
  });

  const updateQuotes = useCallback(() => {
    if (latestQuotes && latestQuotes.length > 0) {
      setQuotes(latestQuotes);
      setIsConnected(true);
    }
  }, [latestQuotes]);

  useEffect(() => {
    updateQuotes();
  }, [updateQuotes]);

  return {
    quotes,
    isConnected,
    error,
  };
}

// LiveUpdatesProvider dispatches a window CustomEvent("tradehub:live-event") for global/admin scopes.

    const ce = ev as CustomEvent;
    const detail = ce?.detail || {};
    const type = String(detail?.type || "");

    }, 350);

8.1 NEW FILE: server/middleware/tradeIdempotency.ts
Create this file:

import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { db } from "@db";
import { orderIntentAudit, trades } from "@shared/schema";
import { and, desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";

/**
 * Trade placement idempotency guard.
 *
 * Purpose:
 * - Prevent duplicate order placement when a client retries the same request on a flaky network.
 * - Client provides `clientRequestId` (idempotency key).
 * - Server persists `client_request_id` only on ORDER_RECEIVED rows in order_intent_audit.
 * - UNIQUE(user_id, client_request_id) ensures at-most-one ORDER_RECEIVED per key.
 *
 * Behavior:
 * - If key already exists, short-circuit and return the existing trade (if created),
 *   otherwise return an ACK (202) so client can reconcile via WS/sync.
 */
export async function tradeIdempotencyMiddleware(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  const userId = (req.session as any)?.userId;
  if (!userId) return next();

  const raw = (req.body as any)?.clientRequestId;
  const clientRequestId = typeof raw === "string" ? raw.trim() : "";
  if (!clientRequestId) return next();

  // Basic hygiene (UUIDs fit; do not over-constrain)
  if (clientRequestId.length < 16 || clientRequestId.length > 128) {
    return res.status(400).json({ message: "clientRequestId must be 16-128 characters" });
  }

  try {
    const existingReceived = await db.query.orderIntentAudit.findFirst({
      where: and(eq(orderIntentAudit.userId, userId), eq(orderIntentAudit.clientRequestId, clientRequestId)),
      orderBy: desc(orderIntentAudit.id),
    });

    if (!existingReceived) {
      // Let downstream handler know the normalized key (optional)
      (req as any).clientRequestId = clientRequestId;
      return next();
    }

    const correlationId = (existingReceived as any).correlationId;

    const existingTrade = await db.query.trades.findFirst({
      where: and(eq(trades.userId, userId), eq(trades.correlationId, correlationId)),
      orderBy: desc(trades.id),
    });

    res.setHeader("x-idempotent-replay", "1");

    if (existingTrade) {
      return res.status(200).json(existingTrade);
    }

    return res.status(202).json({ status: "RECEIVED", correlationId });
  } catch (err) {
    // Never block trade placement if idempotency lookup fails
    console.error("tradeIdempotencyMiddleware error:", err);
    return next();
  }
}

8.3 UPDATE: server/db/ensureSchema.ts (persist + unique index)
In ensureOrderIntentAuditTable(), ensure:

client_request_id exists (DDL + runtime migration)

Your function should include these lines (keep your existing indexes too):

export function ensureOrderIntentAuditTable() {
  const db = getDb();
  try {
    db.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS order_intent_audit (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        client_request_id TEXT,
        event_at INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
        event_at_ms INTEGER,
        event_code TEXT NOT NULL,
        decision TEXT,
        reject_check TEXT,
        reject_reason TEXT,
        actor_type TEXT NOT NULL,
        user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
        session_id TEXT,
        ip TEXT,
        user_agent TEXT,
        symbol TEXT,
        side TEXT,
        order_type TEXT,
        time_in_force TEXT,
        qty_lots REAL,
        requested_price REAL,
        limit_price REAL,
        stop_price REAL,
        take_profit REAL,
        stop_loss REAL,
        quote_bid REAL,
        quote_ask REAL,
        quote_mid REAL,
        quote_ts INTEGER,
        quote_is_stale INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        risk_limit_json TEXT,
        risk_observed_json TEXT,
        risk_snapshot_json TEXT,
        payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
        prev_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
        event_hash TEXT NOT NULL
      );
    `);

    // Runtime migration for existing DBs
    if (!hasColumn(db, "order_intent_audit", "client_request_id")) {
      db.exec("ALTER TABLE order_intent_audit ADD COLUMN client_request_id TEXT");
      console.log("Added column client_request_id to order_intent_audit");
    }

    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_correlation ON order_intent_audit(correlation_id);`);
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_user ON order_intent_audit(user_id);`);
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_event_code ON order_intent_audit(event_code);`);
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_decision ON order_intent_audit(decision);`);

    // For idempotency lookups
    db.exec(`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_intent_client_req ON order_intent_audit(user_id, client_request_id);`);

    // Enforce at-most-one ORDER_RECEIVED row per (user, clientRequestId).
    // (Multiple NULLs are allowed in SQLite, so non-ORDER_RECEIVED rows can keep client_request_id NULL.)
    db.exec(
      `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ux_order_intent_user_client_req
       ON order_intent_audit(user_id, client_request_id);`
    );

    console.log("Order intent audit table initialized/verified.");
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

  const isUniqueClientReq =
    !!clientRequestId &&
    msg.includes("UNIQUE") &&
    msg.includes("order_intent_audit") &&

        where: and(eq(orderIntentAudit.userId, userId), eq(orderIntentAudit.clientRequestId, clientRequestId)),
        orderBy: desc(orderIntentAudit.id),

        const existingCorr = existing.correlationId;
        const existingTrade = await db.query.trades.findFirst({
          where: and(eq(trades.userId, userId), eq(trades.correlationId, existingCorr)),
          orderBy: desc(trades.id),
        });

        if (existingTrade) return res.status(200).json(existingTrade);
        return res.status(202).json({ status: "RECEIVED", correlationId: existingCorr });

What you should see after this (acceptance criteria)
First request with clientRequestId places trade normally.

Retry same request returns:

200 with the same trade, header x-idempotent-replay: 1, OR

202 { status:"RECEIVED", correlationId } if trade not yet persisted (rare here, but safe).

Double-tap race: second request will not create a second trade. It will replay due to UNIQUE constraint fallback.

ORDER_RECEIVED has client_request_id populated

later rows (DECISION, etc.) have client_request_id = NULL

// WS ACK: publish clientRequestId → correlationId mapping as soon as ORDER_RECEIVED is recorded.
// This is critical for native/mobile reliability: the client can store correlationId immediately.
if (clientRequestId) {
  broadcast(
    {
      type: "orders:ack",
      correlationId,
      clientRequestId,
      receivedAtMs,
      symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
      side: data.type,
      orderType: orderType ?? "Market",

Notes

This event is purposely “small” and safe. It gives the mobile client enough to persist the mapping without leaking extra account state.

It works whether client.userId is assigned by your older auth message or your newer token-handshake (as long as the server sets client.userId).

Part 9B — Optional REST reconcile endpoint (for reconnects / background resume)
Mobile reality: the app can miss WS events when backgrounded, or it may crash after sending the order. This endpoint lets the client ask: “For these clientRequestIds, what correlationId/trade did the server attach?”

9B.1 Add endpoints in server/routes.ts
Place these endpoints near other authenticated API endpoints (a good location is right after your /api/trades endpoints block).

// Reconcile idempotency mappings for mobile reconnect/background recovery.
// Client sends up to 50 clientRequestIds; server returns correlationId + trade status if available.
app.post("/api/orders/reconcile", ensureAuth, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const userId = req.session.userId;

  const bodySchema = z.object({
    clientRequestIds: z.array(z.string().min(16).max(128)).max(50),
  });

  const parsed = bodySchema.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return res.status(400).json({ message: "INVALID_REQUEST", details: parsed.error.flatten() });
  }

  const ids = Array.from(
    new Set(parsed.data.clientRequestIds.map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean))
  ).slice(0, 50);

  if (ids.length === 0) {
    return res.json({ serverTimeMs: Date.now(), results: [] });
  }

  // Pull ORDER_RECEIVED rows (only those should carry client_request_id)
  const intents = await db
    .select({
      clientRequestId: orderIntentAudit.clientRequestId,
      correlationId: orderIntentAudit.correlationId,
      receivedAtMs: orderIntentAudit.eventAtMs,
      symbol: orderIntentAudit.symbol,
      side: orderIntentAudit.side,
      orderType: orderIntentAudit.orderType,
      qtyLots: orderIntentAudit.qtyLots,
    })
    .from(orderIntentAudit)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(orderIntentAudit.userId, userId),
        eq(orderIntentAudit.eventCode, "ORDER_RECEIVED"),
        inArray(orderIntentAudit.clientRequestId, ids)
      )
    );

  const correlationIds = Array.from(
    new Set(intents.map((r) => r.correlationId).filter(Boolean))
  );

  const tradesByCorr = new Map<string, any>();
  if (correlationIds.length > 0) {
    const tradeRows = await db
      .select({
        id: trades.id,
        correlationId: trades.correlationId,
        status: trades.status,
        symbolId: trades.symbolId,
        type: trades.type,
        orderType: trades.orderType,
        lots: trades.lots,
        openPrice: trades.openPrice,
        openedAt: trades.openedAt,
        executedAt: trades.executedAt,
        closedAt: trades.closedAt,
      })
      .from(trades)
      .where(and(eq(trades.userId, userId), inArray(trades.correlationId, correlationIds)));

    for (const t of tradeRows) {
      if (t.correlationId) tradesByCorr.set(String(t.correlationId), t);
    }
  }

  const intentByClientReq = new Map<string, any>();
  for (const r of intents) {
    if (r.clientRequestId) intentByClientReq.set(String(r.clientRequestId), r);
  }

  const results = ids.map((clientRequestId) => {
    const intent = intentByClientReq.get(clientRequestId);
    const correlationId = intent?.correlationId ?? null;
    const trade = correlationId ? tradesByCorr.get(String(correlationId)) : null;

    return {
      clientRequestId,
      found: Boolean(intent),
      correlationId,
      receivedAtMs: intent?.receivedAtMs ?? null,
      symbol: intent?.symbol ?? null,
      side: intent?.side ?? null,
      orderType: intent?.orderType ?? null,
      qtyLots: intent?.qtyLots ?? null,
      trade: trade
        ? {
            id: trade.id,
            status: trade.status,
            openPrice: trade.openPrice,
            openedAt: trade.openedAt,
            executedAt: trade.executedAt,
            closedAt: trade.closedAt,
          }
        : null,
    };
  });

  return res.json({ serverTimeMs: Date.now(), results });
});

// Convenience single-key reconcile
app.get("/api/orders/reconcile/:clientRequestId", ensureAuth, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const userId = req.session.userId;
  const clientRequestId = String(req.params.clientRequestId || "").trim();

  if (clientRequestId.length < 16 || clientRequestId.length > 128) {
    return res.status(400).json({ message: "INVALID_CLIENT_REQUEST_ID" });
  }

  const intent = await db.query.orderIntentAudit.findFirst({
    where: and(
      eq(orderIntentAudit.userId, userId),
      eq(orderIntentAudit.eventCode, "ORDER_RECEIVED"),
      eq(orderIntentAudit.clientRequestId, clientRequestId)
    ),
    orderBy: desc(orderIntentAudit.id),
  });

  if (!intent) {
    return res.json({ found: false, clientRequestId, correlationId: null, trade: null });
  }

  const correlationId = String((intent as any).correlationId);
  const trade = await db.query.trades.findFirst({
    where: and(eq(trades.userId, userId), eq(trades.correlationId, correlationId)),
    orderBy: desc(trades.id),
  });

  return res.json({
    found: true,
    clientRequestId,
    correlationId,
    receivedAtMs: (intent as any).eventAtMs ?? null,
    trade: trade
      ? { id: (trade as any).id, status: (trade as any).status, openPrice: (trade as any).openPrice }
      : null,
  });
});

A) Add/ensure realtime bus + lifecycle emitter (server-side)
server/realtime/liveBus.ts (NEW)
/**
 * LiveBus - minimal in-process event bridge used by the order engine, risk engine,
 * and other server subsystems to push realtime updates to connected clients.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * - The backend remains the system of record.
 * - This bus is only for realtime notifications (delta events), not truth.
 */

export type EmitToUserFn = (userId: number, event: unknown) => void;
export type EmitToAllFn = (event: unknown) => void;

let _emitToUser: EmitToUserFn = () => {};
let _emitToAll: EmitToAllFn = () => {};

/**
 * Called once during server boot (routes.ts) after the WebSocket server is created.
 */
export function setLiveEmitter(emitToUser: EmitToUserFn, emitToAll?: EmitToAllFn) {
  _emitToUser = emitToUser;
  if (emitToAll) _emitToAll = emitToAll;
}

export function emitToUser(userId: number, event: unknown) {
  try {
    _emitToUser(userId, event);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[LiveBus] emitToUser failed:", err);
  }
}

export function emitToAll(event: unknown) {
  try {
    _emitToAll(event);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[LiveBus] emitToAll failed:", err);
  }
}
server/realtime/orderLifecycle.ts (NEW)
import { emitToUser } from "./liveBus";

export type OrderLifecycleStage =
  | "RECEIVED"
  | "ACCEPTED"
  | "REJECTED"
  | "FILLED"
  | "CANCELLED";

export type OrderLifecycleEvent = {
  type: "orders:lifecycle";
  stage: OrderLifecycleStage;
  ts: number;
  correlationId: string;
  clientRequestId: string | null;

  // Optional context for UI convenience (truth still comes from snapshots)
  tradeId?: number;
  symbol?: string;
  side?: "BUY" | "SELL";
  orderType?: string;
  fillPrice?: number;
  reasonCode?: string;
  note?: string;
};

/**
 * Emit a lifecycle event to a specific user.
 *
 * This is the primary "native-network reliability" mechanism:
 * the client can deterministically tie every lifecycle event back to the
 * originating user intent using (clientRequestId, correlationId).
 */
export function emitOrderLifecycle(
  userId: number,
  e: Omit<OrderLifecycleEvent, "type" | "ts">
) {
  const payload: OrderLifecycleEvent = {
    type: "orders:lifecycle",
    ts: Date.now(),
    ...e,
  };
  emitToUser(userId, payload);
}

C) WebSocket lifecycle stream: add server-side emitter (engine + routes share it)

C1) server/realtime/liveBus.ts (NEW)
// server/realtime/liveBus.ts
import type WebSocket from "ws";

export type WsTopic = "trades" | "orders" | "account" | "quotes" | "global";

export type LiveClient = {
  ws: WebSocket;
  userId: number;
  topics: Set<WsTopic>;
  connectedAtMs: number;
};

const clients = new Set<LiveClient>();

function safeSend(ws: WebSocket, obj: unknown) {
  try {
    // @ts-ignore - ws types
    if (ws.readyState !== 1) return; // OPEN
    ws.send(JSON.stringify(obj));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export function registerLiveClient(c: LiveClient) {
  clients.add(c);
}

export function unregisterLiveClient(c: LiveClient) {
  clients.delete(c);
}

export function subscribeTopics(c: LiveClient, topics: WsTopic[]) {
  for (const t of topics) c.topics.add(t);
}

export function unsubscribeTopics(c: LiveClient, topics: WsTopic[]) {
  for (const t of topics) c.topics.delete(t);
}

export function publishToUser(userId: number, payload: unknown, topic: WsTopic = "global") {
  for (const c of clients) {
    if (c.userId !== userId) continue;
    if (!c.topics.has(topic)) continue;
    safeSend(c.ws, payload);
  }
}

export function publishGlobal(payload: unknown, topic: WsTopic = "global") {
  for (const c of clients) {
    if (!c.topics.has(topic)) continue;
    safeSend(c.ws, payload);
  }
}
C2) server/realtime/orderLifecycle.ts (NEW)
// server/realtime/orderLifecycle.ts
import { publishToUser } from "./liveBus";

export type OrderLifecycleStage =
  | "RECEIVED"
  | "ACCEPTED"
  | "REJECTED"
  | "FILLED"
  | "CANCELLED";

export type OrderLifecycleEvent = {
  type: "orders:lifecycle";
  ts: number;

  userId: number;
  correlationId: string;
  clientRequestId: string;

  tradeId?: number;
  symbol?: string;
  side?: "BUY" | "SELL";
  orderType?: string;

  stage: OrderLifecycleStage;

  reasonCode?: string; // machine-friendly
  reason?: string; // human-friendly

  fillPrice?: number;
  requestedPrice?: number;
};

export function emitOrderLifecycle(userId: number, e: Omit<OrderLifecycleEvent, "type" | "ts" | "userId"> & { ts?: number }) {
  const evt: OrderLifecycleEvent = {
    type: "orders:lifecycle",
    ts: e.ts ?? Date.now(),
    userId,
    ...e,
  };
  publishToUser(userId, evt, "orders");
}

// server/routes.ts (top imports)
