Where you currently set up client.userId and mark isAlive, create a LiveClient and register it. Example insertion inside your wss.on("connection"... after you authenticate a user:

// after successful auth (userId known)
const liveClient: LiveClient = {
  ws,
  userId: authedUserId,
  topics: new Set<WsTopic>(["global", "trades", "orders", "account"]),
  connectedAtMs: Date.now(),
};
registerLiveClient(liveClient);

ws.on("close", () => {
  unregisterLiveClient(liveClient);
});

ws.on("message", (data) => {
  try {
    const msg = JSON.parse(String(data));
    if (msg?.type === "subscribe") {
      const topics = Array.isArray(msg.topics) ? msg.topics : [];
      subscribeTopics(liveClient, topics);
    }
    if (msg?.type === "unsubscribe") {
      const topics = Array.isArray(msg.topics) ? msg.topics : [];
      unsubscribeTopics(liveClient, topics);
    }
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
});

D2) Replace the /api/trades handler block to enforce lifecycle + both IDs
In server/routes.ts, replace the body of the /api/trades handler (the function starting async (req: Request, res: Response) => { ... }) with the version below.

This version:

accepts clientRequestId from body or Idempotency-Key header

writes ORDER_RECEIVED with clientRequestId

emits WS lifecycle RECEIVED

on any reject path: writes DECISION REJECT + emits REJECTED

on accept: emits ACCEPTED

for market orders: emits FILLED immediately (since the route opens it immediately)

for pending: does not emit FILLED (engine emits it later)

Paste this as the handler body (keep your existing middleware chain and signature):

// server/routes.ts (inside app.post("/api/trades", ... , async (req,res)=>{ ... }))

async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const correlationId = generateCorrelationId();
  const auditCtx = buildAuditContext(req);
  auditCtx.correlationId = correlationId;
  const receivedAtMs = Date.now();

  const userId = req.session.userId;

  // Idempotency key: body preferred; header accepted (common mobile pattern)
  const rawBodyKey = String((req.body as any)?.clientRequestId ?? "").trim();
  const rawHeaderKey = String(req.header("Idempotency-Key") ?? "").trim();
  const clientRequestId = rawBodyKey || rawHeaderKey || ""; // allow legacy clients, but mobile should send it

  const reject = async (httpStatus: number, rejectCheck: string, rejectReason: string, publicMessage?: string) => {
    try {
      await writeOrderIntentAudit({
        correlationId,
        clientRequestId: clientRequestId || null,
        eventCode: "DECISION",
        ctx: auditCtx,
        userId,
        decision: "REJECT",
        rejectCheck,
        rejectReason,
        payload: { rawBody: req.body, receivedAtMs, latencyMs: Date.now() - receivedAtMs },
      });
    } catch (auditErr) {
      console.error("Error writing DECISION REJECT audit:", auditErr);
    }

    if (clientRequestId) {
      emitOrderLifecycle(userId, {
        correlationId,
        clientRequestId,
        stage: "REJECTED",
        reasonCode: rejectCheck,
        reason: rejectReason,
      });
    }

    return res.status(httpStatus).json({
      message: publicMessage ?? rejectReason,
      correlationId,
      clientRequestId: clientRequestId || undefined,
    });
  };

  try {
    // Handle either lots or size parameter from the request
    const { lots, size, orderType, limitPrice, stopPrice } = req.body as any;
    const orderSize = size ?? lots;

    if (typeof orderSize !== "number") {
      return reject(400, "INVALID_SIZE", "size (lots) must be numeric");
    }

    // Validate request data
    const data = insertTradeSchema.parse({
      ...req.body,
      userId,
      openedAt: new Date(),
      lots: orderSize,
      correlationId,
      clientRequestId: clientRequestId || undefined,
    });

    // Load symbol config
    const symbolConfig = await storage.getSymbolConfigById(data.symbolId);
    if (!symbolConfig) {
      return reject(404, "SYMBOL_NOT_FOUND", "Symbol configuration not found");
    }

    // Get latest quote snapshot
    const quotesResponse = await axios.get(`http://localhost:5000/api/quotes/latest`);
    const latestQuotes = quotesResponse.data;
    const quote = latestQuotes.find((q: any) => q.symbol === symbolConfig.symbol);

    if (!quote) {
      return reject(404, "QUOTE_NOT_FOUND", "Symbol price not found");
    }

    // Write ORDER_RECEIVED as soon as we have quote context
    try {
      await writeOrderIntentAudit({
        correlationId,
        clientRequestId: clientRequestId || null,
        eventCode: "ORDER_RECEIVED",
        ctx: auditCtx,
        userId,
        symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
        side: data.type,
        orderType: orderType ?? "Market",
        qtyLots: orderSize,
        requestedPrice: parseFloat(String(req.body.limitPrice ?? req.body.stopPrice ?? quote.price)),
        limitPrice: req.body.limitPrice ? parseFloat(String(req.body.limitPrice)) : null,
        stopPrice: req.body.stopPrice ? parseFloat(String(req.body.stopPrice)) : null,
        takeProfit: req.body.takeProfit ? parseFloat(String(req.body.takeProfit)) : null,
        stopLoss: req.body.stopLoss ? parseFloat(String(req.body.stopLoss)) : null,
        quoteBid: quote.bid ? parseFloat(String(quote.bid)) : null,
        quoteAsk: quote.ask ? parseFloat(String(quote.ask)) : null,
        quoteMid: quote.mid ? parseFloat(String(quote.mid)) : quote.price ? parseFloat(String(quote.price)) : null,
        quoteTs: quote.timestamp ? new Date(quote.timestamp) : null,
        quoteIsStale: quote.isStale ?? false,
        payload: { rawBody: req.body, receivedAtMs },
      });
    } catch (auditErr) {
      console.error("Error writing ORDER_RECEIVED audit:", auditErr);
    }

    // WS: ORDER_RECEIVED ack (only if clientRequestId is present)
    if (clientRequestId) {
      emitOrderLifecycle(userId, {
        correlationId,
        clientRequestId,
        stage: "RECEIVED",
        symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
        side: data.type,
        orderType: orderType ?? "Market",
        requestedPrice: parseFloat(String(req.body.limitPrice ?? req.body.stopPrice ?? quote.price)),
      });
    }

    // Reject on stale quote (your current policy)
    if (quote.isStale === true) {
      return reject(409, "STALE_QUOTE", "Cannot open trade: quote is stale. Please wait for fresh pricing.");
    }

    // Determine order kind
    const isLimitOrder = String(orderType ?? "Market").toLowerCase().includes("limit");
    const isStopOrder = String(orderType ?? "Market").toLowerCase().includes("stop");
    const isPendingOrder = isLimitOrder || isStopOrder;

    // Compute entry price for margin/creation (server-trusted)
    const bid = quote.bid ? Number(quote.bid) : Number(quote.price);
    const ask = quote.ask ? Number(quote.ask) : Number(quote.price);
    const mid = quote.mid ? Number(quote.mid) : (bid + ask) / 2;

    const entryPrice = (data.type === "BUY") ? ask : bid;

    // Pending orders use their intended entry
    const pendingEntry =
      isLimitOrder ? Number(limitPrice) :
      isStopOrder ? Number(stopPrice) :
      entryPrice;

    if (isPendingOrder && !Number.isFinite(pendingEntry)) {
      return reject(400, "MISSING_PENDING_PRICE", "Limit/Stop orders require a valid limitPrice/stopPrice.");
    }

    // Position sizing/margin (keep your existing logic)
    const tradeLots = Number(data.lots ?? orderSize);
    const leverage = 50;

    const priceForMargin = isPendingOrder ? pendingEntry : entryPrice;
    const positionSize = tradeLots * 100000;

    const marginNeeded = requiredMargin(positionSize, priceForMargin, leverage);

    const metrics = await recalcAccount(userId);
    if (!metrics) {
      return reject(404, "USER_NOT_FOUND", "User not found");
    }

    if (metrics.freeMargin < marginNeeded) {
      return reject(
        409,
        "INSUFFICIENT_MARGIN",
        `Insufficient free margin. Needed: ${marginNeeded.toFixed(2)}, Available: ${metrics.freeMargin.toFixed(2)}`
      );
    }

    // (Keep your existing MAX_CONCURRENT_LOTS / MAX_TRADES checks here if present)
    // IMPORTANT: on any return branch, use reject(...) so WS emits REJECTED.

    // Create trade
    const trade = await storage.createTrade({
      ...data,
      openPrice: isPendingOrder ? priceForMargin : entryPrice,
      lots: tradeLots,
      size: positionSize,
      orderType: orderType ?? "Market",
      limitPrice: isLimitOrder ? Number(limitPrice) : null,
      stopPrice: isStopOrder ? Number(stopPrice) : null,
      status: isPendingOrder ? "PENDING" : "OPEN",
      correlationId,
      clientRequestId: clientRequestId || null,

      lastActorUserId: userId,
      lastActorSessionId: auditCtx.sessionId,
      lastActorIp: auditCtx.ip,
      lastActorUserAgent: auditCtx.userAgent,
      lastActorType: auditCtx.actorType,
    });

    // DECISION PASS
    try {
      await writeOrderIntentAudit({
        correlationId,
        clientRequestId: clientRequestId || null,
        eventCode: "DECISION",
        ctx: auditCtx,
        userId,
        decision: "PASS",
        symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
        side: data.type,
        orderType: orderType ?? "Market",
        qtyLots: tradeLots,
        requestedPrice: isPendingOrder ? priceForMargin : entryPrice,
        quoteBid: bid,
        quoteAsk: ask,
        quoteMid: mid,
        quoteIsStale: quote.isStale ?? false,
        payload: { tradeId: trade.id, latencyMs: Date.now() - receivedAtMs },
      });
    } catch (auditErr) {
      console.error("Error writing DECISION PASS audit:", auditErr);
    }

    // WS: ACCEPTED
    if (clientRequestId) {
      emitOrderLifecycle(userId, {
        correlationId,
        clientRequestId,
        tradeId: trade.id,
        symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
        side: data.type,
        orderType: orderType ?? "Market",
        stage: "ACCEPTED",
        requestedPrice: isPendingOrder ? priceForMargin : entryPrice,
      });
    }

    // MARKET orders are effectively FILLED immediately (your current system opens them in-route)
    if (!isPendingOrder && clientRequestId) {
      emitOrderLifecycle(userId, {
        correlationId,
        clientRequestId,
        tradeId: trade.id,
        symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
        side: data.type,
        orderType: orderType ?? "Market",
        stage: "FILLED",
        fillPrice: entryPrice,
      });
    }

    // Legacy UI invalidation path
    emitTradesUpdated(userId);

    return res.status(201).json(trade);
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Trade creation error:", error);
    // If we can, emit REJECTED with a generic reason for deterministic pairing
    if (clientRequestId) {
      emitOrderLifecycle(userId, {
        correlationId,
        clientRequestId,
        stage: "REJECTED",
        reasonCode: "SERVER_ERROR",
        reason: "Trade placement failed due to a server error.",
      });
    }
    return res.status(500).json({ message: "Failed to create trade", correlationId, clientRequestId: clientRequestId || undefined });
  }
}

F1) Add helper client/src/lib/clientRequestId.ts (NEW)
// client/src/lib/clientRequestId.ts
export function newClientRequestId(): string {
  // Prefer crypto.randomUUID in modern browsers
  const c: any = globalThis as any;
  if (c?.crypto?.randomUUID) return c.crypto.randomUUID();

  // Fallback: timestamp + random
  return `crid_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}_${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}`;
}

11A) Add event_log table (durable delta stream)

Add this function anywhere near your other ensure*Table() helpers:

// server/db/ensureSchema.ts (ADD)
import Database from "better-sqlite3";

// ...

export function ensureEventLogTable() {
  const db = new Database("./trading_app.db");
  try {
    db.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS event_log (
        seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        scope TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USER',     -- USER | GLOBAL | ADMIN
        user_id INTEGER NULL,                  -- for USER scope
        topic TEXT NOT NULL,                   -- e.g. 'orders' | 'trades' | 'account' | 'audit'
        payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,            -- JSON string (the event payload)
        created_at_ms INTEGER NOT NULL
      );

      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_event_log_user_seq
        ON event_log(user_id, seq);

      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_event_log_scope_seq
        ON event_log(scope, seq);
    `);
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

11B.1 — NEW: server/realtime/liveRegistry.ts

// server/realtime/liveRegistry.ts
export type LiveTarget =
  | { scope: "USER"; userId: number }
  | { scope: "GLOBAL" }
  | { scope: "ADMIN" };

export type LivePublishFn = (event: any, target: LiveTarget) => void;

let _publisher: LivePublishFn | null = null;

export function registerLivePublisher(fn: LivePublishFn) {
  _publisher = fn;
}

export function publishLiveEvent(event: any, target: LiveTarget) {
  if (_publisher) _publisher(event, target);
}
11B.2 — NEW: server/realtime/eventLogStore.ts
Durable append + cursor fetch.

// server/realtime/eventLogStore.ts
import Database from "better-sqlite3";

export type EventScope = "USER" | "GLOBAL" | "ADMIN";

type AppendArgs = {
  scope: EventScope;
  userId?: number | null;
  topic: string;
  payload: unknown;
};

type FetchArgs = {
  userId: number;
  cursor: number;
  limit: number;
  includeGlobal: boolean;
  includeAdmin: boolean;
  isAdmin: boolean;
};

let _db: Database.Database | null = null;

function db() {
  if (!_db) _db = new Database("./trading_app.db");
  return _db;
}

export function appendEventLog({ scope, userId, topic, payload }: AppendArgs): number {
  const createdAtMs = Date.now();
  const payloadJson = JSON.stringify(payload ?? null);

  const stmt = db().prepare(`
    INSERT INTO event_log (scope, user_id, topic, payload_json, created_at_ms)
    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  `);

  const info = stmt.run(scope, userId ?? null, topic, payloadJson, createdAtMs);
  // better-sqlite3 returns lastInsertRowid as bigint-ish
  return Number(info.lastInsertRowid);
}

export function fetchEventLogSince(args: FetchArgs): { nextCursor: number; events: any[] } {
  const { userId, cursor, limit, includeGlobal, includeAdmin, isAdmin } = args;

  const clauses: string[] = [];
  const params: any[] = [];

  // base cursor
  params.push(cursor);

  // USER scope events for this user
  clauses.push(`(scope = 'USER' AND user_id = ?)`);
  params.push(userId);

  // GLOBAL scope events (optional)
  if (includeGlobal) {
    clauses.push(`(scope = 'GLOBAL')`);
  }

  // ADMIN scope events (optional, only if requester is admin)
  if (includeAdmin && isAdmin) {
    clauses.push(`(scope = 'ADMIN')`);
  }

  const where = clauses.length ? `AND (${clauses.join(" OR ")})` : "";

  const sql = `
    SELECT seq, scope, user_id, topic, payload_json, created_at_ms
    FROM event_log
    WHERE seq > ?
    ${where}
    ORDER BY seq ASC
    LIMIT ?
  `;

  const rows = db().prepare(sql).all(...params, limit);

  const events = rows.map((r: any) => {
    let payload: any = null;
    try {
      payload = JSON.parse(r.payload_json);
    } catch {
      payload = null;
    }

    // Flatten: payload + seq + scope/topic for client cursor + filtering
    if (payload && typeof payload === "object") {
      return { ...payload, seq: r.seq, scope: r.scope, topic: r.topic };
    }
    return { payload, seq: r.seq, scope: r.scope, topic: r.topic };
  });

  const nextCursor = rows.length ? Number(rows[rows.length - 1].seq) : cursor;
  return { nextCursor, events };
}
11B.3 — NEW: server/realtime/liveEvents.ts
One call = append to event_log + publish over WS (if publisher registered). This is what you call from routes and the engine.

// server/realtime/liveEvents.ts
import { appendEventLog, type EventScope } from "./eventLogStore";
import { publishLiveEvent } from "./liveRegistry";

function emit(scope: EventScope, topic: string, payload: any, userId?: number | null) {
  const seq = appendEventLog({ scope, userId: userId ?? null, topic, payload });
  const envelope =
    payload && typeof payload === "object"
      ? { ...payload, seq, scope, topic }
      : { payload, seq, scope, topic };

  if (scope === "USER" && typeof userId === "number") {
    publishLiveEvent(envelope, { scope: "USER", userId });
  } else if (scope === "GLOBAL") {
    publishLiveEvent(envelope, { scope: "GLOBAL" });
  } else if (scope === "ADMIN") {
    publishLiveEvent(envelope, { scope: "ADMIN" });
  }

  return seq;
}

export function emitUserEvent(userId: number, topic: string, payload: any) {
  return emit("USER", topic, payload, userId);
}

export function emitGlobalEvent(topic: string, payload: any) {
  return emit("GLOBAL", topic, payload, null);
}

export function emitAdminEvent(topic: string, payload: any) {
  return emit("ADMIN", topic, payload, null);
}

/**
 * Convenience helpers (recommended standard event shapes)
 * Keep these stable to avoid mobile/web divergence.
 */
export function emitTradesUpdated(userId: number) {
  return emitUserEvent(userId, "trades", { type: "trades:updated", userId, ts: Date.now() });
}

export type OrderStage = "RECEIVED" | "ACCEPTED" | "REJECTED" | "FILLED" | "CANCELLED";

export function emitOrderLifecycle(userId: number, evt: {
  stage: OrderStage;
  correlationId: string;
  clientRequestId?: string | null;
  tradeId?: number | null;
  reason?: string | null;
  ts?: number;
}) {
  return emitUserEvent(userId, "orders", {
    type: "orders:lifecycle",
    ts: evt.ts ?? Date.now(),
    ...evt,
  });
}

11C.2 — server/routes.ts (ADD ROUTE)
Inside registerRoutes(app), after ensureAuth is defined:

// server/routes.ts (ADD)
app.get("/api/sync", ensureAuth, (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const cursor = Number(req.query.cursor ?? 0) || 0;

  const includeGlobal =
    String(req.query.includeGlobal ?? "").toLowerCase() === "1" ||
    String(req.query.includeGlobal ?? "").toLowerCase() === "true";

  const includeAdmin =
    String(req.query.includeAdmin ?? "").toLowerCase() === "1" ||
    String(req.query.includeAdmin ?? "").toLowerCase() === "true";

  const limitRaw = Number(req.query.limit ?? 500) || 500;
  const limit = Math.min(Math.max(limitRaw, 1), 2000);

  const result = fetchEventLogSince({
    userId: req.session.userId,
    cursor,
    limit,
    includeGlobal,
    includeAdmin,
    isAdmin: !!req.session.isAdmin,
  });

  res.json(result);
});
This gives mobile a deterministic replay rail:

startup: snapshot endpoints

realtime: WS deltas

reconnect/background: /api/sync?cursor=...

    return;

1) New file: client/src/lib/authToken.ts
// client/src/lib/authToken.ts
const TOKEN_KEYS = ["auth.token", "tc.auth.token", "tradecentral.auth.token"] as const;

export function getAuthToken(): string | null {
  try {
    for (const k of TOKEN_KEYS) {
      const v = localStorage.getItem(k);
      if (v && v.trim()) return v.trim();
    }
    return null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function setAuthToken(token: string): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem("auth.token", token);
  } catch {
    // ignore (mobile webview/private mode)
  }
}

export function clearAuthToken(): void {
  try {
    for (const k of TOKEN_KEYS) localStorage.removeItem(k);
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}
2) Patch: client/src/lib/queryClient.ts — add Authorization header if token exists
Full file replacement (safe, small enough):

import { QueryClient, QueryFunction } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { getDeviceInstallId, getDeviceFingerprint, getClientTimezone, getClientLanguage } from "./identity";
import { getAuthToken } from "./authToken";

let cachedFingerprint: string | null = null;

async function getIdentityHeaders(): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  if (!cachedFingerprint) {
    cachedFingerprint = await getDeviceFingerprint();
  }

  return {
    "x-device-fp": cachedFingerprint,
    "x-device-install-id": getDeviceInstallId(),
    "x-client-tz": getClientTimezone(),
    "x-client-lang": getClientLanguage(),
  };
}

function getAuthHeaders(): Record<string, string> {
  const token = getAuthToken();
  return token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {};
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;

  constructor(message: string, status: number) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.name = "ApiError";
  }
}

async function throwIfResNotOk(res: Response) {
  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    let message = text || res.statusText;
    try {
      const errorData = JSON.parse(text);
      message = errorData.message || text || res.statusText;
    } catch {
      // non-JSON; keep text
    }
    throw new ApiError(message, res.status);
  }
}

export async function apiRequest(
  method: string,
  url: string,
  data?: unknown | undefined,
): Promise<Response> {
  const identityHeaders = await getIdentityHeaders();
  const authHeaders = getAuthHeaders();

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    headers: {
      ...(data ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : {}),
      ...identityHeaders,
      ...authHeaders,
    },
    body: data ? JSON.stringify(data) : undefined,
    credentials: "include", // keep session auth working
  });

  await throwIfResNotOk(res);
  return res;
}

type UnauthorizedBehavior = "returnNull" | "throw";
export const getQueryFn: <T>(options: { on401: UnauthorizedBehavior }) => QueryFunction<T> =
  ({ on401: unauthorizedBehavior }) =>
  async ({ queryKey }) => {
    const identityHeaders = await getIdentityHeaders();
    const authHeaders = getAuthHeaders();

    const res = await fetch(queryKey[0] as string, {
      credentials: "include",
      headers: {
        ...identityHeaders,
        ...authHeaders,
      },
    });

    if (unauthorizedBehavior === "returnNull" && res.status === 401) {
      return null;
    }

    await throwIfResNotOk(res);
    return await res.json();
  };

export const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      queryFn: getQueryFn({ on401: "throw" }),
      refetchInterval: false,
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
      staleTime: Infinity,
      retry: false,
    },
    mutations: { retry: false },
  },
});

PART 12E — Client WS-first baseline: LiveUpdatesProvider with /api/sync replay
New file: client/src/live/LiveUpdatesProvider.tsx
This provider:

Builds WS URL with ?token= when available

Calls /api/sync?cursor= before (re)connecting to WS

Applies events in-order and persists cursor

Emits window events so you can update Auth state and other UI incrementally without massive rewrites

import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { getAuthToken } from "@/lib/authToken";

type LiveEvent =
  | { t: "GLOBAL"; seq: number; ts: number; level: "INFO" | "WARN" | "ERROR"; message: string }
  | { t: "ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT"; seq: number; ts: number; balance: number; equity: number; usedMargin: number; freeMargin: number }
  | { t: "QUOTE"; seq: number; ts: number; symbol: string; bid: number; ask: number; mid?: number; isStale?: boolean }
  | {
      t: "ORDER_LIFECYCLE";
      seq: number;
      ts: number;
      correlationId: string;
      clientRequestId?: string | null;
      stage: "RECEIVED" | "ACCEPTED" | "REJECTED" | "FILLED" | "CANCELLED";
      reason?: string | null;
      tradeId?: number | null;
    };

type LiveState = {
  connected: boolean;
  lastSeq: number;
  account?: { balance: number; equity: number; usedMargin: number; freeMargin: number; ts: number };
  quotesBySymbol: Record<string, { bid: number; ask: number; mid?: number; ts: number; isStale?: boolean }>;
};

type LiveUpdatesContextValue = LiveState & {
  forceResync: () => Promise<void>;
};

const LiveUpdatesContext = createContext<LiveUpdatesContextValue | null>(null);

function wsBaseUrl(): string {
  const proto = window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss://" : "ws://";
  return `${proto}${window.location.host}/ws`;
}

function cursorKey(): string {
  // Keep it stable; if you later want per-user cursors, move this to useAuth().user?.id
  return "tc.live.cursor";
}

async function fetchSync(cursor: number): Promise<{ nextCursor: number; events: LiveEvent[] }> {
  const token = getAuthToken();
  const res = await fetch(`/api/sync?cursor=${cursor}`, {
    credentials: "include",
    headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : undefined,
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`sync failed: ${res.status}`);
  return res.json();
}

export function LiveUpdatesProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [connected, setConnected] = useState(false);
  const [lastSeq, setLastSeq] = useState<number>(() => {
    try {
      const v = localStorage.getItem(cursorKey());
      return v ? Number(v) || 0 : 0;
    } catch {
      return 0;
    }
  });
  const [account, setAccount] = useState<LiveState["account"]>(undefined);
  const [quotesBySymbol, setQuotesBySymbol] = useState<LiveState["quotesBySymbol"]>({});

  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const reconnectTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const reconnectAttemptRef = useRef(0);
  const syncingRef = useRef(false);

  const persistCursor = useCallback((seq: number) => {
    setLastSeq(seq);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(cursorKey(), String(seq));
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }, []);

  const applyEvent = useCallback((ev: LiveEvent) => {
    // monotonic cursor advance
    if (typeof ev.seq === "number" && ev.seq > lastSeq) {
      persistCursor(ev.seq);
    }

    switch (ev.t) {
      case "ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT": {
        const next = {
          balance: ev.balance,
          equity: ev.equity,
          usedMargin: ev.usedMargin,
          freeMargin: ev.freeMargin,
          ts: ev.ts,
        };
        setAccount(next);
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("tc:account-snapshot", { detail: next }));
        break;
      }
      case "QUOTE": {
        setQuotesBySymbol(prev => ({
          ...prev,
          [ev.symbol]: { bid: ev.bid, ask: ev.ask, mid: ev.mid, ts: ev.ts, isStale: ev.isStale },
        }));
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("tc:quote", { detail: ev }));
        break;
      }
      case "ORDER_LIFECYCLE": {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("tc:order-lifecycle", { detail: ev }));
        break;
      }
      case "GLOBAL": {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("tc:global", { detail: ev }));
        break;
      }
    }
  }, [lastSeq, persistCursor]);

  const runSync = useCallback(async () => {
    if (syncingRef.current) return;
    syncingRef.current = true;
    try {
      let cursor = lastSeq;
      for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const { nextCursor, events } = await fetchSync(cursor);
        if (Array.isArray(events)) {
          for (const e of events) applyEvent(e);
        }
        if (nextCursor <= cursor) break;
        cursor = nextCursor;
        persistCursor(cursor);
        if (!events?.length) break;
      }
    } finally {
      syncingRef.current = false;
    }
  }, [applyEvent, lastSeq, persistCursor]);

  const connect = useCallback(async () => {
    if (wsRef.current && (wsRef.current.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN || wsRef.current.readyState === WebSocket.CONNECTING)) {
      return;
    }

    // Catch up first so UI converges after background/reconnect.
    await runSync();

    const token = getAuthToken();
    const url = token ? `${wsBaseUrl()}?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}` : wsBaseUrl();

    const ws = new WebSocket(url);
    wsRef.current = ws;

    ws.onopen = () => {
      setConnected(true);
      reconnectAttemptRef.current = 0;

      // Subscription handshake (no userId; server derives user from token/session)
      ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "SUBSCRIBE", topics: ["global", "account", "orders", "quotes"] }));
    };

    ws.onmessage = (msg) => {
      try {
        const data = JSON.parse(msg.data);
        if (!data || typeof data !== "object") return;

        // Allow both single event and batch envelopes
        if (Array.isArray(data.events)) {
          for (const ev of data.events) applyEvent(ev);
        } else {
          applyEvent(data);
        }
      } catch {
        // ignore malformed
      }
    };

    ws.onclose = async () => {
      setConnected(false);
      wsRef.current = null;

      // After any disconnect, next connect should resync first.
      const attempt = reconnectAttemptRef.current++;
      const backoff = Math.min(30_000, 1000 * Math.pow(1.6, attempt));

      if (reconnectTimerRef.current) window.clearTimeout(reconnectTimerRef.current);
      reconnectTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
        connect().catch(() => {});
      }, backoff);
    };

    ws.onerror = () => {
      // Close triggers reconnect loop
      try { ws.close(); } catch {}
    };
  }, [applyEvent, connect, runSync]);

  useEffect(() => {
    connect().catch(() => {});
    return () => {
      if (reconnectTimerRef.current) window.clearTimeout(reconnectTimerRef.current);
      if (wsRef.current) {
        try { wsRef.current.close(); } catch {}
      }
    };
  }, [connect]);

  const value = useMemo<LiveUpdatesContextValue>(() => ({
    connected,
    lastSeq,
    account,
    quotesBySymbol,
    forceResync: runSync,
  }), [connected, lastSeq, account, quotesBySymbol, runSync]);

  return <LiveUpdatesContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</LiveUpdatesContext.Provider>;
}

export function useLiveUpdates(): LiveUpdatesContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(LiveUpdatesContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useLiveUpdates must be used within LiveUpdatesProvider");
  return ctx;
}

PART 12F — Stop polling: make Account Summary hook WS-first (fallback to REST snapshot)
client/src/hooks/use-account-summary.tsx — full file replacement
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useLiveUpdates } from "@/live/LiveUpdatesProvider";

export interface AccountSummary {
  balance: number;
  equity: number;
  floatingPnl: number;
  usedMargin: number;
  freeMargin: number;
  marginLevel: number | null;
  openPositions: number;
  pricingStale: boolean;
  staleSymbols: string[];
  asOf: string;
}

export function useAccountSummary() {
  const live = useLiveUpdates();

  // WS-first: derive what we can from live.account
  if (live.account) {
    const balance = live.account.balance;
    const equity = live.account.equity;
    const usedMargin = live.account.usedMargin;
    const freeMargin = live.account.freeMargin;

    const floatingPnl = equity - balance;
    const marginLevel = usedMargin > 0 ? (equity / usedMargin) * 100 : null;

    const summary: AccountSummary = {
      balance,
      equity,
      floatingPnl,
      usedMargin,
      freeMargin,
      marginLevel,
      openPositions: 0, // keep from REST until you emit POSITION snapshots/events
      pricingStale: false,
      staleSymbols: [],
      asOf: new Date(live.account.ts).toISOString(),
    };

    return {
      data: summary,
      summary,
      isLoading: false,
      isError: false,
      refetch: live.forceResync,
    } as const;
  }

  // Fallback snapshot only (no polling)
  const query = useQuery<AccountSummary>({
    queryKey: ["/api/account/summary"],
    refetchInterval: false,
    staleTime: 5_000,
  });

  return {
    ...query,
    summary: query.data,
    invalidate: query.refetch,
  };
}

Part 13 — WS-first Quotes + Symbol Subscriptions (+ optional Positions Snapshot)

13A) Server — Add a WS hub that supports symbol subscriptions + quote publish
1) Create: server/realtime/liveHub.ts
// server/realtime/liveHub.ts
import type { IncomingMessage } from "http";
import { WebSocketServer, WebSocket } from "ws";
import { storage } from "../storage";
import { getQuotesSnapshotForSymbols, type QuoteSnapshotRow } from "../services/quoteSnapshot";

// If you already implemented token auth in earlier parts, import your verifier here.
// Expected contract: verifyWsToken(token) -> { ok: true, userId } | { ok: false }
import { verifyWsToken } from "../security/wsTokenAuth"; // adjust path to your implementation

export type LiveClient = WebSocket & {
  userId?: number;
  topics?: Set<string>;
  quoteSymbols?: Set<string>;
};

let wss: WebSocketServer | null = null;

function safeJsonSend(ws: WebSocket, payload: any) {
  if (ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;
  ws.send(JSON.stringify(payload));
}

function parseUrl(req: IncomingMessage) {
  const host = req.headers.host ?? "localhost";
  const proto = (req.headers["x-forwarded-proto"] as string) ?? "http";
  const url = new URL(req.url ?? "/", `${proto}://${host}`);
  return url;
}

function getTokenFromReq(req: IncomingMessage): string | null {
  // token may be in query param: /ws?token=...
  const url = parseUrl(req);
  const token = url.searchParams.get("token");
  return token && token.trim() ? token.trim() : null;
}

function hasTopic(client: LiveClient, topic: string): boolean {
  return !!client.topics?.has(topic);
}

function wantsSymbol(client: LiveClient, symbol: string): boolean {
  if (!client.quoteSymbols || client.quoteSymbols.size === 0) return false;
  if (client.quoteSymbols.has("*")) return true;
  return client.quoteSymbols.has(symbol);
}

export function attachLiveWs(httpServer: any) {
  if (wss) return wss;

  wss = new WebSocketServer({ server: httpServer, path: "/ws" });

  wss.on("connection", (socket, req) => {
    const client = socket as LiveClient;

    // --- AUTH (token query param) ---
    const token = getTokenFromReq(req);
    if (!token) {
      safeJsonSend(client, { t: "ERROR", code: "WS_TOKEN_MISSING" });
      client.close(1008, "Token required");
      return;
    }

    const v = verifyWsToken(token);
    if (!v.ok) {
      safeJsonSend(client, { t: "ERROR", code: "WS_TOKEN_INVALID" });
      client.close(1008, "Invalid token");
      return;
    }

    client.userId = v.userId;
    client.topics = new Set();
    client.quoteSymbols = new Set();

    safeJsonSend(client, { t: "WS_READY", ts: Date.now() });

    socket.on("message", async (raw) => {
      let msg: any;
      try {
        msg = JSON.parse(raw.toString());
      } catch {
        safeJsonSend(client, { t: "ERROR", code: "WS_BAD_JSON" });
        return;
      }

      // --- SUBSCRIBE protocol ---
      // msg: { t:"SUBSCRIBE", topics?: string[], symbols?: string[] }
      if (msg?.t === "SUBSCRIBE") {
        const topics = Array.isArray(msg.topics) ? msg.topics.filter(Boolean) : [];
        const symbols = Array.isArray(msg.symbols) ? msg.symbols.filter(Boolean) : [];

        // update topic subscriptions
        if (topics.length > 0) {
          for (const t of topics) client.topics!.add(String(t));
        }

        // update symbol subscriptions (quotes)
        if (symbols.length > 0) {
          client.quoteSymbols!.clear();
          for (const s of symbols) client.quoteSymbols!.add(String(s).toUpperCase());
        }

        safeJsonSend(client, {
          t: "SUBSCRIBED",
          topics: Array.from(client.topics!),
          symbols: Array.from(client.quoteSymbols!),
          ts: Date.now(),
        });

        // push snapshots immediately
        if (hasTopic(client, "quotes") && client.quoteSymbols!.size > 0) {
          const snap = await getQuotesSnapshotForSymbols(Array.from(client.quoteSymbols!));
          safeJsonSend(client, { t: "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT", rows: snap, ts: Date.now() });
        }

        if (hasTopic(client, "positions")) {
          const open = await storage.getOpenTradesByUserId(client.userId!);
          const pending = await storage.getPendingTradesByUserId(client.userId!);
          safeJsonSend(client, {
            t: "POSITION_SNAPSHOT",
            open,
            pending,
            ts: Date.now(),
          });
        }

        return;
      }
    });
  });

  return wss;
}

// --- Publish API (called by forgeFeed / sim updater) ---

export function publishQuoteTicks(ticks: Array<{
  symbol: string;
  bid: number;
  ask: number;
  mid: number;
  spread: number;
  lastApiUpdate: number;
  isStale: boolean;
}>) {
  if (!wss) return;

  for (const tick of ticks) {
    const payload = { t: "QUOTE_TICK", ...tick, ts: Date.now() };
    for (const ws of wss.clients as Set<LiveClient>) {
      if (!ws.userId) continue;
      if (!hasTopic(ws, "quotes")) continue;
      if (!wantsSymbol(ws, tick.symbol)) continue;
      safeJsonSend(ws, payload);
    }
  }
}

export async function publishPositionsSnapshotForUser(userId: number) {
  if (!wss) return;

  const open = await storage.getOpenTradesByUserId(userId);
  const pending = await storage.getPendingTradesByUserId(userId);

  const payload = { t: "POSITION_SNAPSHOT", open, pending, ts: Date.now() };

  for (const ws of wss.clients as Set<LiveClient>) {
    if (ws.userId !== userId) continue;
    if (!hasTopic(ws, "positions")) continue;
    safeJsonSend(ws, payload);
  }
}
13B) Server — Quote snapshot helper used for initial subscription snapshot
2) Create: server/services/quoteSnapshot.ts
This mirrors your /api/quotes/latest logic, but scoped to specific symbols, so you don’t blast the client with everything unless they asked for it.

// server/services/quoteSnapshot.ts
import Database from "better-sqlite3";

export type QuoteSnapshotRow = {
  symbol: string;
  bid: number;
  ask: number;
  price: number;
  spread: number;
  prevClose: number | null;
  pctChange: number;
  isStale: boolean;
  lastApiUpdate: number;
  dataAge: number;
};

function toNum(v: any): number | null {
  if (v === null || v === undefined) return null;
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
}

export async function getQuotesSnapshotForSymbols(symbols: string[]): Promise<QuoteSnapshotRow[]> {
  const db = new Database("./trading_app.db");

  try {
    const now = Date.now();
    const STALE_THRESHOLD_MS = Number(process.env.QUOTE_STALE_AFTER_MS ?? 10_000);

    const upper = symbols
      .map((s) => String(s).toUpperCase().trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    if (upper.length === 0) return [];

    // parameter placeholders
    const placeholders = upper.map(() => "?").join(",");

    const rows = db
      .prepare(
        `
        SELECT
          q.symbol,
          q.bid,
          q.ask,
          COALESCE(q.is_stale, 0) AS isStaleRaw,
          COALESCE(q.last_api_update, q.updated_at, 0) AS lastApiUpdateRaw,
          rp.price AS prevClose
        FROM quotes q
        LEFT JOIN reference_prices rp ON rp.symbol = q.symbol
        WHERE UPPER(q.symbol) IN (${placeholders})
        `
      )
      .all(...upper) as any[];

    return rows.map((r) => {
      const bid = toNum(r.bid) ?? 0;
      const ask = toNum(r.ask) ?? 0;
      const mid = (bid + ask) / 2;
      const spread = Math.abs(ask - bid);

      const lastRaw = toNum(r.lastApiUpdateRaw) ?? now;
      // normalize sec vs ms
      const lastApiUpdate = lastRaw < 1e12 ? lastRaw * 1000 : lastRaw;
      const dataAge = now - lastApiUpdate;

      const prevClose = toNum(r.prevClose);
      const pctChange =
        prevClose && prevClose > 0 ? Math.round((((mid - prevClose) / prevClose) * 100) * 100) / 100 : 0;

      const dbIsStale = Number(r.isStaleRaw ?? 0) === 1;
      const isStale = dbIsStale || dataAge > STALE_THRESHOLD_MS;

      return {
        symbol: String(r.symbol).toUpperCase(),
        bid,
        ask,
        price: mid,
        spread,
        prevClose: prevClose ?? null,
        pctChange,
        isStale,
        lastApiUpdate,
        dataAge,
      };
    });
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

13F) Client — Replace REST polling in use-quotes.tsx with WS snapshot + ticks
5) Replace: client/src/hooks/use-quotes.tsx
This version:

loads enabled symbols (/api/config/symbols)

opens WS

on open: sends SUBSCRIBE with topics ["quotes"] and symbols list

handles QUOTE_SNAPSHOT + QUOTE_TICK

no polling loop

optional REST fallback if WS is down (kept minimal)

// client/src/hooks/use-quotes.tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useWebSocket } from "./use-websocket";
import { useAuth } from "./use-auth";
import { apiRequest } from "@/lib/queryClient";

interface Quote {
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
  price: number;
  bid: number;
  ask: number;
  spread: number;
  prevClose?: number | null;
  pctChange?: number;
  isStale?: boolean;
  lastApiUpdate?: number;
  dataAge?: number;
  time: number;
}

interface SymbolConfig {
  id: number;
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
  enabled?: boolean;
}

type QuoteSnapshotMsg = {
  t: "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT";
  rows: Array<{
    symbol: string;
    bid: number;
    ask: number;
    price: number;
    spread: number;
    prevClose: number | null;
    pctChange: number;
    isStale: boolean;
    lastApiUpdate: number;
    dataAge: number;
  }>;
};

type QuoteTickMsg = {
  t: "QUOTE_TICK";
  symbol: string;
  bid: number;
  ask: number;
  mid: number;
  spread: number;
  lastApiUpdate: number;
  isStale: boolean;
  ts: number;
};

function calcPct(mid: number, prevClose?: number | null) {
  if (!prevClose || prevClose <= 0) return 0;
  return Math.round((((mid - prevClose) / prevClose) * 100) * 100) / 100;
}

export function useQuotes() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [quoteMap, setQuoteMap] = useState<Record<string, Quote>>({});
  const [isConnected, setIsConnected] = useState(false);

  const { data: symbols = [] } = useQuery<SymbolConfig[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/config/symbols"],
  });

  const enabledSymbols = useMemo(() => {
    return symbols
      .filter((s) => s.enabled !== false)
      .map((s) => s.symbol.toUpperCase());
  }, [symbols]);

  const symbolNameMap = useMemo(() => {
    const m: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const s of symbols) {
      if (s.enabled !== false) m[s.symbol.toUpperCase()] = s.name;
    }
    return m;
  }, [symbols]);

  // IMPORTANT: token-auth WS (per your earlier hardening).
  // Assumes user?.auth?.token is populated.
  const wsUrl = useMemo(() => {
    const proto = window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss://" : "ws://";
    const token = (user as any)?.auth?.token;
    const u = new URL(proto + window.location.host + "/ws");
    if (token) u.searchParams.set("token", token);
    return u.toString();
  }, [user]);

  const { isConnected: wsConnected, sendMessage } = useWebSocket(wsUrl, {
    onOpen: () => {
      // Subscribe to quotes for enabled symbols
      if (enabledSymbols.length > 0) {
        sendMessage({
          t: "SUBSCRIBE",
          topics: ["quotes"],
          symbols: enabledSymbols,
        });
      }
    },
    onMessage: (msg: any) => {
      if (!msg || typeof msg !== "object") return;

      if (msg.t === "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT") {
        const m = msg as QuoteSnapshotMsg;
        setQuoteMap((prev) => {
          const next = { ...prev };
          for (const r of m.rows) {
            const sym = r.symbol.toUpperCase();
            next[sym] = {
              symbol: sym,
              name: symbolNameMap[sym] || sym,
              price: r.price,
              bid: r.bid,
              ask: r.ask,
              spread: r.spread,
              prevClose: r.prevClose,
              pctChange: r.pctChange,
              isStale: r.isStale,
              lastApiUpdate: r.lastApiUpdate,
              dataAge: r.dataAge,
              time: Date.now(),
            };
          }
          return next;
        });
        return;
      }

      if (msg.t === "QUOTE_TICK") {
        const t = msg as QuoteTickMsg;
        const sym = t.symbol.toUpperCase();
        setQuoteMap((prev) => {
          const prevRow = prev[sym];
          const prevClose = prevRow?.prevClose ?? null;

          const mid = t.mid;
          const pctChange = calcPct(mid, prevClose);

          return {
            ...prev,
            [sym]: {
              symbol: sym,
              name: symbolNameMap[sym] || sym,
              price: mid,
              bid: t.bid,
              ask: t.ask,
              spread: t.spread,
              prevClose,
              pctChange,
              isStale: t.isStale,
              lastApiUpdate: t.lastApiUpdate,
              dataAge: Date.now() - t.lastApiUpdate,
              time: Date.now(),
            },
          };
        });
        return;
      }
    },
    onClose: () => {},
  });

  // if symbols change (enable/disable), re-subscribe
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!wsConnected) return;
    if (enabledSymbols.length === 0) return;
    sendMessage({
      t: "SUBSCRIBE",
      topics: ["quotes"],
      symbols: enabledSymbols,
    });
  }, [wsConnected, enabledSymbols, sendMessage]);

  // Optional REST fallback (only if WS is down and we have no data)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (wsConnected) return;
    if (Object.keys(quoteMap).length > 0) return;

    (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await apiRequest("GET", "/api/quotes/latest");
        const arr = await res.json();
        if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return;

        const next: Record<string, Quote> = {};
        for (const r of arr) {
          const sym = String(r.symbol).toUpperCase();
          if (!enabledSymbols.includes(sym)) continue;

          const bid = Number(r.bid ?? r.price ?? 0);
          const ask = Number(r.ask ?? r.price ?? 0);
          const mid = (bid + ask) / 2;

          const prevClose = r.prevClose ?? null;
          next[sym] = {
            symbol: sym,
            name: symbolNameMap[sym] || sym,
            price: mid,
            bid,
            ask,
            spread: Math.abs(ask - bid),
            prevClose,
            pctChange: typeof r.pctChange === "number" ? r.pctChange : calcPct(mid, prevClose),
            isStale: !!r.isStale,
            lastApiUpdate: Number(r.lastApiUpdate ?? Date.now()),
            dataAge: Number(r.dataAge ?? 0),
            time: Date.now(),
          };
        }
        setQuoteMap(next);
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    })();
  }, [wsConnected, quoteMap, enabledSymbols, symbolNameMap]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsConnected(wsConnected || Object.keys(quoteMap).length > 0);
  }, [wsConnected, quoteMap]);

  const quotes = useMemo(() => {
    return enabledSymbols
      .map((s) => quoteMap[s])
      .filter(Boolean);
  }, [enabledSymbols, quoteMap]);

  const hasStaleData = quotes.some((q) => q.isStale);

  return {
    quotes,
    isConnected,
    isLoading: !isConnected && quotes.length === 0,
    hasStaleData,
  };
}

Part 14A — Server: ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT + POSITIONS_SNAPSHOT (subscribe + publish)
1) server/realtime/liveHub.ts (FULL FILE)
If you already have a liveHub.ts, replace it with this version (it extends topic handling + adds the two publishers).
Assumes your Part 5 WS auth already attaches userId to the connection context. If your context field names differ, adjust the ClientCtx shape.

// server/realtime/liveHub.ts
import type { Server as HttpServer, IncomingMessage } from "http";
import { WebSocketServer, WebSocket } from "ws";
import { storage } from "../storage";
import { recalcAccount } from "../recalcAccount";

type Topic = "quotes" | "account" | "positions" | "orders" | "audit" | "global";

type ClientCtx = {
  ws: WebSocket;
  userId: number;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  topics: Set<Topic>;
  symbols: Set<string>; // for quotes filtering
};

type SubscribeMsg = {
  op: "SUBSCRIBE";
  topics?: Topic[];
  symbols?: string[];
};

type UnsubscribeMsg = {
  op: "UNSUBSCRIBE";
  topics?: Topic[];
  symbols?: string[];
};

type PingMsg = { op: "PING" };

type ClientMsg = SubscribeMsg | UnsubscribeMsg | PingMsg;

type QuoteTickEvent = {
  t: "QUOTE_TICK";
  symbol: string;
  bid: number | null;
  ask: number | null;
  mid: number | null;
  spread: number | null;
  isStale: boolean;
  ts: number;
};

type QuoteSnapshotEvent = {
  t: "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT";
  quotes: QuoteTickEvent[];
  ts: number;
};

type AccountSnapshotEvent = {
  t: "ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT";
  balance: number;
  equity: number;
  floatingPnl: number;
  usedMargin: number;
  freeMargin: number;
  marginLevel: number | null;
  pricingStale: boolean;
  staleSymbols: string[];
  asOfIso: string;
  ts: number;
};

type PositionsSnapshotEvent = {
  t: "POSITIONS_SNAPSHOT";
  open: any[];    // trades with symbol join (your existing types)
  pending: any[]; // trades with symbol join
  ts: number;
};

type OrderLifecycleEvent = {
  t: "ORDER_LIFECYCLE";
  correlationId: string;
  clientRequestId?: string | null;
  stage: "RECEIVED" | "ACCEPTED" | "REJECTED" | "FILLED" | "CANCELLED";
  tradeId?: number | null;
  reason?: string | null;
  ts: number;
};

let wss: WebSocketServer | null = null;
const clients = new Set<ClientCtx>();

function safeSend(ws: WebSocket, obj: unknown) {
  if (ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;
  try {
    ws.send(JSON.stringify(obj));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

// ---- AUTH HOOK (Part 5 already implemented) ----
// You should already be resolving userId/admin at WS handshake.
// This helper is deliberately minimal; adjust if your attachLiveWs differs.
function getUserFromReq(_req: IncomingMessage): { userId: number; isAdmin: boolean } | null {
  // In your Part 5 implementation you likely do this in the upgrade handler
  // and pass userId/isAdmin in some connection context.
  // If you do NOT have that, you must wire it there (don’t reintroduce client “auth userId” messages).
  return null;
}

/**
 * Attach live WS server to the existing HTTP server.
 * Your Part 5 version probably already does auth in the `upgrade` handler;
 * keep that auth and set `userId/isAdmin` on connection.
 */
export function attachLiveWs(server: HttpServer) {
  if (wss) return;

  wss = new WebSocketServer({ noServer: true });

  // If your Part 5 code already handles `server.on("upgrade")`, keep that
  // and call `wss.handleUpgrade(...)` there. If not, here is the pattern:
  server.on("upgrade", (req, socket, head) => {
    // IMPORTANT: Replace this with your already-implemented WS auth (token query param + session fallback).
    const resolved = getUserFromReq(req);
    if (!resolved) {
      socket.destroy();
      return;
    }

    wss!.handleUpgrade(req, socket, head, (ws) => {
      (ws as any).__userId = resolved.userId;
      (ws as any).__isAdmin = resolved.isAdmin;
      wss!.emit("connection", ws, req);
    });
  });

  wss.on("connection", (ws: WebSocket) => {
    const userId = Number((ws as any).__userId);
    const isAdmin = Boolean((ws as any).__isAdmin);

    if (!userId || !Number.isFinite(userId)) {
      try { ws.close(1008, "unauthorized"); } catch {}
      return;
    }

    const ctx: ClientCtx = {
      ws,
      userId,
      isAdmin,
      topics: new Set<Topic>(["global"]), // default
      symbols: new Set<string>(),
    };
    clients.add(ctx);

    ws.on("message", async (raw) => {
      let msg: ClientMsg | null = null;
      try {
        msg = JSON.parse(String(raw));
      } catch {
        return;
      }
      if (!msg || typeof msg !== "object") return;

      if (msg.op === "PING") {
        safeSend(ws, { t: "PONG", ts: Date.now() });
        return;
      }

      if (msg.op === "SUBSCRIBE") {
        if (Array.isArray(msg.topics)) {
          for (const t of msg.topics) ctx.topics.add(t);
        }
        if (Array.isArray(msg.symbols)) {
          for (const s of msg.symbols) ctx.symbols.add(String(s).toUpperCase());
        }

        // Snapshots on subscribe
        if (ctx.topics.has("account")) {
          await publishAccountSnapshotForUser(ctx.userId);
        }
        if (ctx.topics.has("positions")) {
          await publishPositionsSnapshotForUser(ctx.userId);
        }
        if (ctx.topics.has("quotes")) {
          await publishQuoteSnapshotToClient(ctx);
        }

        safeSend(ws, { t: "SUBSCRIBED", topics: Array.from(ctx.topics), ts: Date.now() });
        return;
      }

      if (msg.op === "UNSUBSCRIBE") {
        if (Array.isArray(msg.topics)) {
          for (const t of msg.topics) ctx.topics.delete(t);
        }
        if (Array.isArray(msg.symbols)) {
          for (const s of msg.symbols) ctx.symbols.delete(String(s).toUpperCase());
        }
        safeSend(ws, { t: "UNSUBSCRIBED", topics: Array.from(ctx.topics), ts: Date.now() });
        return;
      }
    });

    ws.on("close", () => {
      clients.delete(ctx);
    });

    safeSend(ws, { t: "HELLO", ts: Date.now() });
  });
}

/**
 * QUOTES: snapshot for a specific client, filtered by subscribed symbols.
 * Reads from your existing REST source (storage/DB). Keep it lightweight.
 */
async function publishQuoteSnapshotToClient(ctx: ClientCtx) {
  // If you already have a quotes snapshot function from Part 13, use it here instead.
  // Minimal safe fallback: do nothing if client didn’t specify symbols.
  if (ctx.symbols.size === 0) {
    safeSend(ctx.ws, { t: "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT", quotes: [], ts: Date.now() } satisfies QuoteSnapshotEvent);
    return;
  }

  // Pull the latest quotes from your existing REST endpoint behavior:
  // storage does not expose a “get all quotes” helper in this repo; your Part 13 probably added one.
  // If you already have it, replace this with that call.
  safeSend(ctx.ws, { t: "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT", quotes: [], ts: Date.now() } satisfies QuoteSnapshotEvent);
}

/**
 * Publish a quote tick (call this from your quote ingestion pipeline).
 * Client must have topic "quotes" and subscribe to the symbol.
 */
export function publishQuoteTick(tick: QuoteTickEvent) {
  for (const c of clients) {
    if (!c.topics.has("quotes")) continue;
    if (c.symbols.size > 0 && !c.symbols.has(tick.symbol.toUpperCase())) continue;
    safeSend(c.ws, tick);
  }
}

/**
 * ACCOUNT: publish current account snapshot for a user to all that user’s clients subscribed to "account".
 * Optionally pass a precomputed recalc result to avoid double work.
 */
export async function publishAccountSnapshotForUser(
  userId: number,
  precomputed?: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof recalcAccount>>
) {
  const r = precomputed ?? (await recalcAccount(userId));
  const evt: AccountSnapshotEvent = {
    t: "ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT",
    balance: Number(r.balance ?? 0),
    equity: Number(r.equity ?? 0),
    floatingPnl: Number(r.floatingPnl ?? 0),
    usedMargin: Number(r.usedMargin ?? 0),
    freeMargin: Number(r.freeMargin ?? 0),
    marginLevel: r.marginLevel === null || r.marginLevel === undefined ? null : Number(r.marginLevel),
    pricingStale: Boolean(r.pricingStale),
    staleSymbols: Array.isArray(r.staleSymbols) ? r.staleSymbols : [],
    asOfIso: String(r.asOfIso ?? new Date().toISOString()),
    ts: Date.now(),
  };

  for (const c of clients) {
    if (c.userId !== userId) continue;
    if (!c.topics.has("account")) continue;
    safeSend(c.ws, evt);
  }
}

/**
 * POSITIONS: publish open + pending snapshot for a user (joined with symbol).
 */
export async function publishPositionsSnapshotForUser(userId: number) {
  const [open, pending] = await Promise.all([
    storage.getOpenTradesByUserId(userId),
    storage.getPendingTradesByUserId(userId),
  ]);

  const evt: PositionsSnapshotEvent = {
    t: "POSITIONS_SNAPSHOT",
    open,
    pending,
    ts: Date.now(),
  };

  for (const c of clients) {
    if (c.userId !== userId) continue;
    if (!c.topics.has("positions")) continue;
    safeSend(c.ws, evt);
  }
}

/**
 * ORDER LIFECYCLE: publish to user clients subscribed to "orders".
 * (Your Part 9 already includes correlationId + clientRequestId in all stages.)
 */
export function publishOrderLifecycleForUser(userId: number, evt: OrderLifecycleEvent) {
  for (const c of clients) {
    if (c.userId !== userId) continue;
    if (!c.topics.has("orders")) continue;
    safeSend(c.ws, evt);
  }
}

Part 14B — Client: single LiveUpdatesProvider + remove polling loops
1) Add client/src/live/LiveUpdatesProvider.tsx (FULL FILE)
// client/src/live/LiveUpdatesProvider.tsx
import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/use-auth";
import { apiRequest } from "@/lib/queryClient";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

type AccountSnapshot = {
  t: "ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT";
  balance: number;
  equity: number;
  floatingPnl: number;
  usedMargin: number;
  freeMargin: number;
  marginLevel: number | null;
  pricingStale: boolean;
  staleSymbols: string[];
  asOfIso: string;
  ts: number;
};

type PositionsSnapshot = {
  t: "POSITIONS_SNAPSHOT";
  open: any[];
  pending: any[];
  ts: number;
};

type QuoteTick = {
  t: "QUOTE_TICK";
  symbol: string;
  bid: number | null;
  ask: number | null;
  mid: number | null;
  spread: number | null;
  isStale: boolean;
  ts: number;
};

type OrderLifecycle = {
  t: "ORDER_LIFECYCLE";
  correlationId: string;
  clientRequestId?: string | null;
  stage: "RECEIVED" | "ACCEPTED" | "REJECTED" | "FILLED" | "CANCELLED";
  tradeId?: number | null;
  reason?: string | null;
  ts: number;
};

type LiveState = {
  connected: boolean;

  account: AccountSnapshot | null;

  openTrades: any[];
  pendingOrders: any[];

  quotesBySymbol: Record<string, QuoteTick>;

  lastOrderLifecycleByCorrelationId: Record<string, OrderLifecycle>;
};

const LiveUpdatesCtx = createContext<LiveState | null>(null);

function wsUrl(base: string, token?: string | null) {
  const u = new URL(base);
  u.protocol = u.protocol === "https:" ? "wss:" : "ws:";
  u.pathname = "/ws";
  if (token) u.searchParams.set("token", token);
  return u.toString();
}

export function LiveUpdatesProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  // If you already store auth.token in your auth contract, prefer it here.
  // If your auth context names differ, adapt accordingly.
  const token = (user as any)?.token ?? null;

  const base = useMemo(() => window.location.origin, []);
  const url = useMemo(() => wsUrl(base, token), [base, token]);

  const [connected, setConnected] = useState(false);
  const [account, setAccount] = useState<AccountSnapshot | null>(null);
  const [openTrades, setOpenTrades] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [pendingOrders, setPendingOrders] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [quotesBySymbol, setQuotesBySymbol] = useState<Record<string, QuoteTick>>({});
  const [lastOrderLifecycleByCorrelationId, setLastOrderLifecycleByCorrelationId] = useState<Record<string, OrderLifecycle>>({});

  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const reconnectRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const closingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);

  // Fetch enabled symbols once (for quote subscriptions).
  const { data: symbolConfigs } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["/api/instruments"], // adjust if your endpoint differs
    queryFn: async () => {
      // If your project already has an instruments endpoint, keep it.
      // Otherwise adjust to your actual symbol config endpoint.
      const res = await apiRequest("GET", "/api/instruments");
      return (await res.json()) as Array<{ symbol: string; enabled?: boolean }>;
    },
    staleTime: 60_000,
  });

  const symbols = useMemo(() => {
    const rows = Array.isArray(symbolConfigs) ? symbolConfigs : [];
    return rows
      .filter((r) => r && r.symbol && (r.enabled === undefined || r.enabled === true))
      .map((r) => String(r.symbol).toUpperCase());
  }, [symbolConfigs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    closingRef.current = false;

    if (!user?.id) {
      // logged out
      try {
        wsRef.current?.close();
      } catch {}
      wsRef.current = null;
      setConnected(false);
      setAccount(null);
      setOpenTrades([]);
      setPendingOrders([]);
      setQuotesBySymbol({});
      setLastOrderLifecycleByCorrelationId({});
      return;
    }

    const connect = () => {
      if (closingRef.current) return;

      const ws = new WebSocket(url);
      wsRef.current = ws;

      ws.onopen = () => {
        setConnected(true);
        reconnectRef.current = 0;

        // Subscribe to topics; this is the canonical “WS-first” baseline.
        ws.send(
          JSON.stringify({
            op: "SUBSCRIBE",
            topics: ["account", "positions", "quotes", "orders"],
            symbols,
          })
        );
      };

      ws.onmessage = (evt) => {
        let msg: any;
        try {
          msg = JSON.parse(evt.data);
        } catch {
          return;
        }
        if (!msg || typeof msg !== "object") return;

        switch (msg.t) {
          case "ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT":
            setAccount(msg as AccountSnapshot);
            return;

          case "POSITIONS_SNAPSHOT": {
            const ps = msg as PositionsSnapshot;
            setOpenTrades(Array.isArray(ps.open) ? ps.open : []);
            setPendingOrders(Array.isArray(ps.pending) ? ps.pending : []);
            return;
          }

          case "QUOTE_TICK": {
            const qt = msg as QuoteTick;
            const sym = String(qt.symbol || "").toUpperCase();
            if (!sym) return;
            setQuotesBySymbol((prev) => ({ ...prev, [sym]: qt }));
            return;
          }

          case "ORDER_LIFECYCLE": {
            const ol = msg as OrderLifecycle;
            if (!ol?.correlationId) return;
            setLastOrderLifecycleByCorrelationId((prev) => ({ ...prev, [ol.correlationId]: ol }));
            return;
          }

          default:
            return;
        }
      };

      ws.onclose = () => {
        setConnected(false);

        if (closingRef.current) return;

        // reconnect with basic backoff
        const attempt = reconnectRef.current + 1;
        reconnectRef.current = attempt;

        const delay = Math.min(15000, 500 + attempt * 1000);
        window.setTimeout(() => connect(), delay);
      };

      ws.onerror = () => {
        // errors will flow into close; do not hard-fail UI
      };
    };

    connect();

    return () => {
      closingRef.current = true;
      try {
        wsRef.current?.close();
      } catch {}
      wsRef.current = null;
    };
  }, [user?.id, url, symbols]);

  const value: LiveState = useMemo(
    () => ({
      connected,
      account,
      openTrades,
      pendingOrders,
      quotesBySymbol,
      lastOrderLifecycleByCorrelationId,
    }),
    [connected, account, openTrades, pendingOrders, quotesBySymbol, lastOrderLifecycleByCorrelationId]
  );

  return <LiveUpdatesCtx.Provider value={value}>{children}</LiveUpdatesCtx.Provider>;
}

export function useLiveUpdates() {
  const ctx = useContext(LiveUpdatesCtx);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useLiveUpdates must be used within LiveUpdatesProvider");
  return ctx;
}

4) Replace client/src/hooks/use-trades.tsx (WS-first open trades)
// client/src/hooks/use-trades.tsx
import { useLiveUpdates } from "@/live/LiveUpdatesProvider";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { apiRequest } from "@/lib/queryClient";

export function useTrades() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { openTrades } = useLiveUpdates();

  // History can remain REST (not polled); it’s not required for “no refresh” live UX.
  const tradesQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["/api/trades"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const res = await apiRequest("GET", "/api/trades");
      return res.json();
    },
    staleTime: 30_000,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });

  const createTradeMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (tradeData: any) => {
      const res = await apiRequest("POST", "/api/trades", tradeData);
      return res.json();
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      // WS will push POSITIONS_SNAPSHOT + ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT; keep history reasonably fresh:
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades"] });
    },
  });

  const closeTradeMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, closePrice }: { id: number; closePrice: number }) => {
      const res = await apiRequest("POST", `/api/trades/${id}/close`, { closePrice });
      return res.json();
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades"] });
    },
  });

  return {
    trades: tradesQuery.data || [],
    openTrades: openTrades || [],
    isLoading: tradesQuery.isLoading,
    error: tradesQuery.error,
    createTrade: createTradeMutation.mutate,
    isCreating: createTradeMutation.isPending,
    closeTrade: closeTradeMutation.mutate,
    isClosing: closeTradeMutation.isPending,
  };
}

Reference price updates moved off REST so % change remains correct even when REST isn’t called

15A) Server: ensure reference prices update even without REST calls
Replace server/utils/dailyChange.js with the version below (adds two safe getters)
/**
 * A simple implementation to calculate percentage changes for 1Forge Starter tier
 * Uses the daily cached reference prices as we don't have access to candles/ohlc API
 */

import BetterSQLite3 from 'better-sqlite3';

// Map to cache reference prices in memory
const refPrices = new Map();
const lastUpdated = new Map();

// Initialize our reference price tracking
export function initReferenceData() {
  try {
    const db = new BetterSQLite3('./trading_app.db');

    // Create a reference price table if it doesn't exist
    db.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reference_prices (
        symbol TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        price REAL NOT NULL,
        timestamp INTEGER NOT NULL
      )
    `);

    // Load any existing reference prices
    const rows = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM reference_prices').all();

    for (const row of rows) {
      refPrices.set(row.symbol, row.price);
      lastUpdated.set(row.symbol, row.timestamp);
    }

    db.close();

    console.log(`Loaded ${rows.length} reference prices from database`);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error initializing reference price tracking:", error);
  }
}

// Update reference prices once per day
export function updateReferenceData(symbols, prices) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const oneDaySeconds = 86400;
  let updated = 0;

  try {
    const db = new BetterSQLite3('./trading_app.db');
    const stmt = db.prepare('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO reference_prices (symbol, price, timestamp) VALUES (?, ?, ?)');

    symbols.forEach((symbol, index) => {
      const price = prices[index];
      const lastUpdate = lastUpdated.get(symbol) || 0;

      // Only update once per day
      if (now - lastUpdate > oneDaySeconds) {
        stmt.run(symbol, price, now);

        refPrices.set(symbol, price);
        lastUpdated.set(symbol, now);

        updated++;
        console.log(`Updated reference price for ${symbol}: ${price}`);
      }
    });

    db.close();

    if (updated > 0) {
      console.log(`Updated ${updated} reference prices`);
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error updating reference prices:", error);
  }
}

// Calculate percentage change from reference price (previous day's close)
export function calculateChange(symbol, currentPrice) {
  const reference = refPrices.get(symbol);

  // If we don't have a reference price yet, initialize it and return 0
  if (!reference) {
    const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

    refPrices.set(symbol, currentPrice);
    lastUpdated.set(symbol, now);

    const db = new BetterSQLite3('./trading_app.db');
    const stmt = db.prepare('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO reference_prices (symbol, price, timestamp) VALUES (?, ?, ?)');
    stmt.run(symbol, currentPrice, now);
    db.close();

    return 0;
  }

  const change = ((currentPrice - reference) / reference) * 100;
  return Math.round(change * 100) / 100; // 2 dp
}

// NEW: expose current reference price used for prevClose
export function getReferencePrice(symbol) {
  return refPrices.has(symbol) ? refPrices.get(symbol) : null;
}

// NEW: expose reference timestamp (epoch seconds)
export function getReferenceTimestamp(symbol) {
  return lastUpdated.has(symbol) ? lastUpdated.get(symbol) : null;
}
15B) Server: add quote snapshot loader (robust to old/new quotes table schemas)
Create server/realtime/quoteSnapshot.ts
import BetterSQLite3 from "better-sqlite3";
import { getReferencePrice } from "../utils/dailyChange.js";

export type QuoteSnapshot = {
  symbol: string;
  bid: number | null;
  ask: number | null;
  mid: number | null;
  spread: number | null;

  // “change” here is “points delta vs prior tick” (hub will compute live),
  // snapshot returns 0; client can still display pctChange.
  change: number;

  prevClose: number | null;
  pctChange: number;

  isStale: boolean;
  lastApiUpdateMs: number | null;
  dataAgeMs: number | null;

  ts: number; // milliseconds
};

function getColumns(db: BetterSQLite3.Database, table: string): Set<string> {
  const cols = new Set<string>();
  const rows = db.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info(${table})`).all() as any[];
  for (const r of rows) cols.add(String(r.name));
  return cols;
}

function safeNumber(v: any): number | null {
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
}

/**
 * Load quotes from sqlite. Handles either:
 *  - legacy schema: (symbol, price, bid, ask, change, timestamp)
 *  - newer schema:  (symbol, price, bid, ask, updated_at, is_stale, last_api_update)
 */
export function loadQuoteSnapshot(dbPath: string, symbols: string[]): QuoteSnapshot[] {
  const db = new BetterSQLite3(dbPath);
  try {
    const cols = getColumns(db, "quotes");
    const hasIsStale = cols.has("is_stale");
    const hasLastApiUpdate = cols.has("last_api_update");
    const hasTimestamp = cols.has("timestamp");

    // Build a safe SELECT list based on existing columns
    const selectParts = [
      "q.symbol AS symbol",
      cols.has("price") ? "q.price AS price" : "NULL AS price",
      cols.has("bid") ? "q.bid AS bid" : "NULL AS bid",
      cols.has("ask") ? "q.ask AS ask" : "NULL AS ask",
      hasIsStale ? "q.is_stale AS is_stale" : "0 AS is_stale",
      hasLastApiUpdate ? "q.last_api_update AS last_api_update" : "NULL AS last_api_update",
      hasTimestamp ? "q.timestamp AS timestamp" : "NULL AS timestamp",
    ];

    const where =
      symbols.length > 0
        ? `WHERE q.symbol IN (${symbols.map(() => "?").join(",")})`
        : "";

    const sql = `
      SELECT
        ${selectParts.join(",\n        ")}
      FROM quotes q
      ${where}
    `;

    const rows = symbols.length > 0 ? (db.prepare(sql).all(...symbols) as any[]) : (db.prepare(sql).all() as any[]);
    const now = Date.now();

    const out: QuoteSnapshot[] = rows.map((r) => {
      const symbol = String(r.symbol);
      const bid = safeNumber(r.bid);
      const ask = safeNumber(r.ask);
      const price = safeNumber(r.price);

      const mid =
        bid != null && ask != null ? (bid + ask) / 2 : price != null ? price : null;

      const spread = bid != null && ask != null ? ask - bid : null;

      // last_api_update in your feed is stored as ms epoch; legacy timestamp is usually seconds
      const lastApiUpdateMs =
        r.last_api_update != null && Number.isFinite(Number(r.last_api_update))
          ? Number(r.last_api_update)
          : r.timestamp != null && Number.isFinite(Number(r.timestamp))
            ? Number(r.timestamp) * 1000
            : null;

      const dataAgeMs = lastApiUpdateMs != null ? Math.max(0, now - lastApiUpdateMs) : null;

      const isStale = Boolean(Number(r.is_stale ?? 0));

      const prevClose = getReferencePrice(symbol);
      const pctChange =
        prevClose != null && mid != null && prevClose !== 0
          ? Math.round((((mid - prevClose) / prevClose) * 100) * 100) / 100
          : 0;

      return {
        symbol,
        bid,
        ask,
        mid,
        spread,
        change: 0,
        prevClose,
        pctChange,
        isStale,
        lastApiUpdateMs,
        dataAgeMs,
        ts: now,
      };
    });

    return out;
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}
15C) Server: quote realtime module (subscriptions + snapshot + tick publishing)
Create server/realtime/quoteRealtime.ts
import type WebSocket from "ws";
import { calculateChange, getReferencePrice } from "../utils/dailyChange.js";
import { loadQuoteSnapshot, type QuoteSnapshot } from "./quoteSnapshot";

export type QuoteTickInput = {
  symbol: string;
  bid: number | null;
  ask: number | null;
  price?: number | null; // optional
  isStale: boolean;
  lastApiUpdateMs: number | null; // ms epoch
};

type LiveClient = {
  ws: WebSocket;
};

type QuoteSnapshotEvent = {
  t: "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT";
  quotes: QuoteSnapshot[];
  ts: number;
};

type QuoteTickEvent = {
  t: "QUOTE_TICK";
  symbol: string;
  bid: number | null;
  ask: number | null;
  mid: number | null;
  spread: number | null;

  change: number;     // points delta vs prior tick (server computed)
  prevClose: number | null;
  pctChange: number;  // vs reference price (server computed)

  isStale: boolean;
  lastApiUpdateMs: number | null;
  dataAgeMs: number | null;

  ts: number; // ms
};

function safeSend(ws: WebSocket, msg: any) {
  try {
    if ((ws as any).readyState === 1) ws.send(JSON.stringify(msg));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export class QuoteRealtime {
  private readonly dbPath: string;

  // symbol -> clients
  private readonly subscribersBySymbol = new Map<string, Set<LiveClient>>();

  // client -> symbols
  private readonly symbolsByClient = new WeakMap<LiveClient, Set<string>>();

  // for “change” points delta computation
  private readonly lastMidBySymbol = new Map<string, number>();

  constructor(dbPath: string) {
    this.dbPath = dbPath;
  }

  detachClient(client: LiveClient) {
    const prev = this.symbolsByClient.get(client);
    if (!prev) return;

    for (const sym of prev) {
      const set = this.subscribersBySymbol.get(sym);
      if (set) {
        set.delete(client);
        if (set.size === 0) this.subscribersBySymbol.delete(sym);
      }
    }
    this.symbolsByClient.delete(client);
  }

  subscribe(client: LiveClient, symbols: string[]) {
    // remove prior subs first
    this.detachClient(client);

    const normalized = Array.from(new Set(symbols.map((s) => String(s).toUpperCase()))).filter(Boolean);
    const set = new Set<string>(normalized);
    this.symbolsByClient.set(client, set);

    for (const sym of set) {
      let bucket = this.subscribersBySymbol.get(sym);
      if (!bucket) {
        bucket = new Set<LiveClient>();
        this.subscribersBySymbol.set(sym, bucket);
      }
      bucket.add(client);
    }

    // Send snapshot immediately for these symbols
    const quotes = loadQuoteSnapshot(this.dbPath, normalized);
    const evt: QuoteSnapshotEvent = { t: "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT", quotes, ts: Date.now() };
    safeSend(client.ws, evt);
  }

  publishTicks(ticks: QuoteTickInput[]) {
    const now = Date.now();

    for (const tick of ticks) {
      const symbol = String(tick.symbol).toUpperCase();
      const subs = this.subscribersBySymbol.get(symbol);
      if (!subs || subs.size === 0) continue;

      const bid = tick.bid ?? null;
      const ask = tick.ask ?? null;

      const mid =
        bid != null && ask != null ? (bid + ask) / 2
          : tick.price != null ? tick.price
          : null;

      const spread = bid != null && ask != null ? ask - bid : null;

      // “change” points delta vs prior mid
      let change = 0;
      if (mid != null) {
        const prev = this.lastMidBySymbol.get(symbol);
        if (prev != null) change = mid - prev;
        this.lastMidBySymbol.set(symbol, mid);
      }

      // % change vs ref price (dailyChange uses in-memory + db-backed reference_prices)
      const pctChange = mid != null ? calculateChange(symbol, mid) : 0;
      const prevClose = getReferencePrice(symbol);

      const lastApiUpdateMs = tick.lastApiUpdateMs;
      const dataAgeMs = lastApiUpdateMs != null ? Math.max(0, now - lastApiUpdateMs) : null;

      const evt: QuoteTickEvent = {
        t: "QUOTE_TICK",
        symbol,
        bid,
        ask,
        mid,
        spread,
        change,
        prevClose,
        pctChange,
        isStale: Boolean(tick.isStale),
        lastApiUpdateMs,
        dataAgeMs,
        ts: now,
      };

      for (const c of subs) safeSend(c.ws, evt);
    }
  }
}
