15F) Client: bind UI to QUOTE_SNAPSHOT + QUOTE_TICK (remove quote polling as primary path)
15F-1) Create client/src/live/LiveUpdatesProvider.tsx (quotes-focused; can be extended)
import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";

export type QuoteTick = {
  symbol: string;
  bid: number | null;
  ask: number | null;
  mid: number | null;
  spread: number | null;

  change: number;
  prevClose: number | null;
  pctChange: number;

  isStale: boolean;
  lastApiUpdateMs: number | null;
  dataAgeMs: number | null;

  ts: number;
};

type LiveState = {
  isConnected: boolean;
  quotesBySymbol: Record<string, QuoteTick>;
  subscribeQuotes: (symbols: string[]) => void;
};

const LiveUpdatesContext = createContext<LiveState | null>(null);

function buildWsUrl(token?: string | null) {
  const proto = window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss:" : "ws:";
  const base = `${proto}//${window.location.host}/ws`;
  if (!token) return base;
  return `${base}?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`;
}

function getStoredToken(): string | null {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem("auth.token");
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function LiveUpdatesProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [isConnected, setIsConnected] = useState(false);
  const [quotesBySymbol, setQuotesBySymbol] = useState<Record<string, QuoteTick>>({});

  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const desiredQuoteSymbolsRef = useRef<string[]>([]);

  const subscribeQuotes = (symbols: string[]) => {
    const normalized = Array.from(new Set(symbols.map((s) => String(s).toUpperCase()))).filter(Boolean);
    desiredQuoteSymbolsRef.current = normalized;

    const ws = wsRef.current;
    if (!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;

    // Send SUBSCRIBE in a shape the server accepts (topics form)
    ws.send(JSON.stringify({
      t: "SUBSCRIBE",
      topics: [{ name: "quotes", symbols: normalized }],
    }));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const token = getStoredToken(); // token auth (session cookie still works if server allows both)
    const wsUrl = buildWsUrl(token);
    const ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl);
    wsRef.current = ws;

    ws.onopen = () => {
      setIsConnected(true);
      const syms = desiredQuoteSymbolsRef.current;
      if (syms.length > 0) {
        ws.send(JSON.stringify({
          t: "SUBSCRIBE",
          topics: [{ name: "quotes", symbols: syms }],
        }));
      }
    };

    ws.onclose = () => {
      setIsConnected(false);
    };

    ws.onerror = () => {
      setIsConnected(false);
    };

    ws.onmessage = (evt) => {
      let msg: any;
      try {
        msg = JSON.parse(evt.data);
      } catch {
        return;
      }

      if (msg?.t === "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT" && Array.isArray(msg.quotes)) {
        setQuotesBySymbol((prev) => {
          const next = { ...prev };
          for (const q of msg.quotes) {
            if (!q?.symbol) continue;
            next[String(q.symbol).toUpperCase()] = {
              symbol: String(q.symbol).toUpperCase(),
              bid: q.bid ?? null,
              ask: q.ask ?? null,
              mid: q.mid ?? null,
              spread: q.spread ?? null,
              change: typeof q.change === "number" ? q.change : 0,
              prevClose: typeof q.prevClose === "number" ? q.prevClose : null,
              pctChange: typeof q.pctChange === "number" ? q.pctChange : 0,
              isStale: Boolean(q.isStale),
              lastApiUpdateMs: typeof q.lastApiUpdateMs === "number" ? q.lastApiUpdateMs : null,
              dataAgeMs: typeof q.dataAgeMs === "number" ? q.dataAgeMs : null,
              ts: typeof q.ts === "number" ? q.ts : Date.now(),
            };
          }
          return next;
        });
        return;
      }

      if (msg?.t === "QUOTE_TICK" && msg?.symbol) {
        const sym = String(msg.symbol).toUpperCase();
        setQuotesBySymbol((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          [sym]: {
            symbol: sym,
            bid: msg.bid ?? null,
            ask: msg.ask ?? null,
            mid: msg.mid ?? null,
            spread: msg.spread ?? null,
            change: typeof msg.change === "number" ? msg.change : 0,
            prevClose: typeof msg.prevClose === "number" ? msg.prevClose : null,
            pctChange: typeof msg.pctChange === "number" ? msg.pctChange : 0,
            isStale: Boolean(msg.isStale),
            lastApiUpdateMs: typeof msg.lastApiUpdateMs === "number" ? msg.lastApiUpdateMs : null,
            dataAgeMs: typeof msg.dataAgeMs === "number" ? msg.dataAgeMs : null,
            ts: typeof msg.ts === "number" ? msg.ts : Date.now(),
          },
        }));
      }
    };

    return () => {
      try { ws.close(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    };
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo<LiveState>(() => ({
    isConnected,
    quotesBySymbol,
    subscribeQuotes,
  }), [isConnected, quotesBySymbol]);

  return <LiveUpdatesContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</LiveUpdatesContext.Provider>;
}

export function useLiveUpdates() {
  const ctx = useContext(LiveUpdatesContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useLiveUpdates must be used within LiveUpdatesProvider");
  return ctx;
}
15F-2) Replace client/src/hooks/use-quotes.tsx (WS-first, REST fallback only)
import { useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { apiRequest } from "@/lib/queryClient";
import { useLiveUpdates } from "@/live/LiveUpdatesProvider";

export type Quote = {
  symbol: string;
  name: string;

  bid: number;
  ask: number;
  price: number;
  spread: number;

  change: number;       // points delta vs prior tick
  changePct: number;    // % vs reference
  prevClose?: number | null;

  isStale?: boolean;
  lastUpdate?: Date;
};

type SymbolConfig = {
  symbol: string;
  name?: string;
  enabled?: boolean | number;
};

export function useQuotes() {
  const { isConnected, quotesBySymbol, subscribeQuotes } = useLiveUpdates();

  const { data: symbolConfigs, isLoading: configsLoading } = useQuery<SymbolConfig[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/config/symbols"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const res = await apiRequest("GET", "/api/config/symbols");
      return res.json();
    },
    staleTime: 60_000,
  });

  const enabledSymbols = useMemo(() => {
    const rows = Array.isArray(symbolConfigs) ? symbolConfigs : [];
    return rows
      .filter((r) => r && r.symbol && (r.enabled === true || r.enabled === 1 || r.enabled === undefined))
      .map((r) => String(r.symbol).toUpperCase());
  }, [symbolConfigs]);

  // Subscribe once symbols are known (WS snapshot + ticks)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (enabledSymbols.length > 0) subscribeQuotes(enabledSymbols);
  }, [enabledSymbols, subscribeQuotes]);

  // REST fallback only if WS has no data (e.g., first load / offline)
  const { data: restFallback } = useQuery<any[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/quotes/latest"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const res = await apiRequest("GET", "/api/quotes/latest");
      return res.json();
    },
    enabled: !isConnected && enabledSymbols.length > 0,
    refetchInterval: 10_000, // slow fallback
    staleTime: 5_000,
  });

  const quotes: Quote[] = useMemo(() => {
    const configs = Array.isArray(symbolConfigs) ? symbolConfigs : [];

    return enabledSymbols.map((sym) => {
      const cfg = configs.find((c) => String(c.symbol).toUpperCase() === sym);
      const live = quotesBySymbol[sym];

      // fallback row (if needed)
      const fb = Array.isArray(restFallback)
        ? restFallback.find((q: any) => String(q.symbol).toUpperCase() === sym)
        : null;

      const bid = live?.bid ?? (typeof fb?.bid === "number" ? fb.bid : 0);
      const ask = live?.ask ?? (typeof fb?.ask === "number" ? fb.ask : 0);
      const mid = live?.mid ?? (typeof fb?.midPrice === "number" ? fb.midPrice : (bid && ask ? (bid + ask) / 2 : 0));
      const spread = live?.spread ?? (typeof fb?.spread === "number" ? fb.spread : (bid && ask ? ask - bid : 0));

      const change = typeof live?.change === "number" ? live.change : 0;
      const pct = typeof live?.pctChange === "number" ? live.pctChange : (typeof fb?.pctChange === "number" ? fb.pctChange : 0);

      return {
        symbol: sym,
        name: cfg?.name ?? sym,
        bid,
        ask,
        price: mid,
        spread,
        change,
        changePct: pct,
        prevClose: live?.prevClose ?? (typeof fb?.prevClose === "number" ? fb.prevClose : null),
        isStale: Boolean(live?.isStale ?? fb?.isStale),
        lastUpdate: live?.ts ? new Date(live.ts) : undefined,
      };
    });
  }, [enabledSymbols, symbolConfigs, quotesBySymbol, restFallback]);

  const hasStaleData = useMemo(() => quotes.some((q) => q.isStale), [quotes]);

  const isLoading = configsLoading || (enabledSymbols.length > 0 && quotes.length === 0);

  return {
    quotes,
    isLoading,
    isConnected,
    hasStaleData,
  };
}

1) client/src/lib/queryClient.ts (FULL REPLACEMENT)
Adds Authorization: Bearer <token> automatically (while keeping credentials: "include" for sessions).

import { QueryClient, QueryFunction } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { getDeviceInstallId, getDeviceFingerprint, getClientTimezone, getClientLanguage } from "./identity";

let cachedFingerprint: string | null = null;

function getAuthHeaders(): Record<string, string> {
  try {
    const token = localStorage.getItem("auth.token");
    return token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {};
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

async function getIdentityHeaders(): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  if (!cachedFingerprint) {
    cachedFingerprint = await getDeviceFingerprint();
  }

  return {
    "x-device-fp": cachedFingerprint,
    "x-device-install-id": getDeviceInstallId(),
    "x-client-tz": getClientTimezone(),
    "x-client-lang": getClientLanguage(),
  };
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;

  constructor(message: string, status: number) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.name = "ApiError";
  }
}

async function throwIfResNotOk(res: Response) {
  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    let message = text || res.statusText;
    try {
      const errorData = JSON.parse(text);
      message = errorData.message || text || res.statusText;
    } catch {
      // non-JSON response
    }
    throw new ApiError(message, res.status);
  }
}

export async function apiRequest(method: string, url: string, data?: unknown | undefined): Promise<Response> {
  const identityHeaders = await getIdentityHeaders();
  const authHeaders = getAuthHeaders();

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    headers: {
      ...(data ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : {}),
      ...identityHeaders,
      ...authHeaders,
    },
    body: data ? JSON.stringify(data) : undefined,
    credentials: "include", // keep session auth working
  });

  await throwIfResNotOk(res);
  return res;
}

type UnauthorizedBehavior = "returnNull" | "throw";
export const getQueryFn: <T>(options: { on401: UnauthorizedBehavior }) => QueryFunction<T> =
  ({ on401: unauthorizedBehavior }) =>
  async ({ queryKey }) => {
    const identityHeaders = await getIdentityHeaders();
    const authHeaders = getAuthHeaders();

    const res = await fetch(queryKey[0] as string, {
      credentials: "include",
      headers: { ...identityHeaders, ...authHeaders },
    });

    if (unauthorizedBehavior === "returnNull" && res.status === 401) {
      return null;
    }

    await throwIfResNotOk(res);
    return await res.json();
  };

export const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      queryFn: getQueryFn({ on401: "throw" }),
      refetchInterval: false,
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
      staleTime: Infinity,
      retry: false,
    },
    mutations: {
      retry: false,
    },
  },
});
2) client/src/hooks/use-auth.tsx (FULL REPLACEMENT)
Removes the 2-second polling loop and persists token if returned by backend.

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback, useEffect } from "react";
import { apiRequest } from "@/lib/queryClient";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";

interface User {
  id: number;
  email: string;
  username: string;
  name?: string;
  phone?: string;
  countryIso2?: string | null;
  balance: string;

  equity?: number;
  freeMargin?: number;
  usedMargin?: number;
  leverage?: number;
  isAdmin?: boolean;
  createdAt?: string;

  userTier?: "CANDIDATE" | "PERFORMER" | "SELECTED";
  contenderTier?: string;

  emailVerified?: boolean;
  emailVerifiedAt?: number | null;
  inGracePeriod?: boolean;
  gracePeriodEndsAt?: number | null;

  isImpersonating?: boolean;
  realAdminId?: number | null;
  realAdminEmail?: string | null;

  // reserved contract
  auth?: { token?: string };
}

interface RegisterOpts {
  countryIso2: string;
  termsToken: string;
  combinedSha256: string;
  captchaToken?: string | null;
  phone?: string | null;
}

interface AuthContextType {
  user: User | null;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  loading: boolean;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  register: (email: string, username: string, password: string, opts?: RegisterOpts) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  checkAuth: () => Promise<void>;
  stopImpersonating: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType>({
  user: null,
  isAuthenticated: false,
  loading: true,
  login: async () => {},
  register: async () => {},
  logout: async () => {},
  checkAuth: async () => {},
  stopImpersonating: async () => {},
});

function persistTokenFromResponse(data: any) {
  const token = data?.auth?.token ?? data?.token ?? null;
  if (typeof token === "string" && token.length > 10) {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem("auth.token", token);
    } catch {
      // ignore storage failure
    }
  }
}

function clearToken() {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem("auth.token");
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export const AuthProvider = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const checkAuth = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const res = await apiRequest("GET", "/api/auth/current-user");
      const data = await res.json();
      persistTokenFromResponse(data);
      setUser(data);
    } catch {
      setUser(null);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const login = async (email: string, password: string) => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const res = await apiRequest("POST", "/api/auth/login", { email, password });
      const data = await res.json();
      persistTokenFromResponse(data);
      setUser(data);
      queryClient.invalidateQueries();
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const register = async (email: string, username: string, password: string, opts?: RegisterOpts) => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const res = await apiRequest("POST", "/api/auth/register", {
        email,
        username,
        password,
        ...(opts || {}),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      persistTokenFromResponse(data);
      setUser(data);
      queryClient.invalidateQueries();
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const logout = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const userId = user?.id;
      await apiRequest("POST", "/api/auth/logout");
      if (userId) {
        sessionStorage.removeItem(`verification_reminder_dismissed_${userId}`);
      }
      clearToken();
      setUser(null);
      queryClient.clear();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Logout error:", error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const stopImpersonating = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const res = await apiRequest("POST", "/api/admin/view-as/stop");
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.success) {
        await checkAuth();
        queryClient.invalidateQueries();
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Stop impersonation error:", error);
      throw error;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    checkAuth();
  }, [checkAuth]);

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        isAuthenticated: !!user,
        loading,
        login,
        register,
        logout,
        checkAuth,
        stopImpersonating,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = () => useContext(AuthContext);
3) Add client/src/live/liveTypes.ts (NEW FILE)
This is intentionally permissive to support “old + new” server event shapes during transition.

export type WsInbound =
  | { t: "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT"; symbols: any[]; ts?: number }
  | { t: "QUOTE_TICK"; symbol: string; bid?: number; ask?: number; mid?: number; ts?: number; isStale?: boolean; dataAgeMs?: number }
  | { t: "ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT"; summary: any; ts?: number }
  | { t: "POSITIONS_SNAPSHOT"; openTrades: any[]; pendingOrders: any[]; ts?: number }
  | {
      t: "ORDER_LIFECYCLE";
      stage: "RECEIVED" | "ACCEPTED" | "REJECTED" | "FILLED" | "CANCELLED";
      correlationId: string;
      clientRequestId?: string | null;
      tradeId?: number | null;
      reason?: string | null;
      ts?: number;
    }
  | { t: "GLOBAL_EVENT"; code: string; message?: string; ts?: number }
  // legacy compatibility
  | { type: "quotes:updated" }
  | { type: "trades:updated"; userId?: number }
  | Record<string, any>;

export type WsOutbound =
  | { op: "SUBSCRIBE"; topics: string[]; symbols?: string[]; includeSnapshot?: boolean }
  | { op: "UNSUBSCRIBE"; topics: string[]; symbols?: string[] }
  // allow legacy subscribe shape if still needed
  | { t: "SUBSCRIBE"; topics: any[] };
4) Add client/src/live/LiveUpdatesProvider.tsx (NEW FILE)
Single WS connection, token-in-query-param supported, per-user topics, plus quote symbol subscription.

import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { apiRequest } from "@/lib/queryClient";
import type { WsInbound, WsOutbound } from "./liveTypes";

type LiveCtx = {
  wsConnected: boolean;
  lastEventAtMs: number | null;

  // quotes keyed by symbol
  quotesBySymbol: Record<string, any>;
  // account summary object (same shape as /api/account/summary)
  accountSummary: any | null;

  openTrades: any[];
  pendingOrders: any[];

  // lifecycle map keyed by correlationId (also contains clientRequestId when present)
  orderLifecycleByCorrelationId: Record<string, any>;

  subscribeQuotes: (symbols: string[]) => void;
  unsubscribeQuotes: (symbols: string[]) => void;

  forceResyncSnapshots: () => Promise<void>;
};

const Ctx = createContext<LiveCtx | null>(null);

function getStoredToken(): string | null {
  try {
    const t = localStorage.getItem("auth.token");
    return t && t.length > 10 ? t : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function buildWsUrl(): string {
  const proto = window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss://" : "ws://";
  const base = `${proto}${window.location.host}/ws`;
  const token = getStoredToken();
  return token ? `${base}?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}` : base;
}

async function safeJson(res: Response) {
  try {
    return await res.json();
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function LiveUpdatesProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const reconnectTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const [wsConnected, setWsConnected] = useState(false);
  const [lastEventAtMs, setLastEventAtMs] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [quotesBySymbol, setQuotesBySymbol] = useState<Record<string, any>>({});
  const [accountSummary, setAccountSummary] = useState<any | null>(null);

  const [openTrades, setOpenTrades] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [pendingOrders, setPendingOrders] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const [orderLifecycleByCorrelationId, setOrderLifecycleByCorrelationId] = useState<Record<string, any>>({});

  const subscribedQuoteSymbolsRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const baseTopicsSubscribedRef = useRef(false);

  const clearReconnectTimer = () => {
    if (reconnectTimerRef.current) {
      window.clearTimeout(reconnectTimerRef.current);
      reconnectTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  };

  const send = useCallback((msg: WsOutbound) => {
    const ws = wsRef.current;
    if (!ws || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return false;
    ws.send(JSON.stringify(msg));
    return true;
  }, []);

  const forceResyncSnapshots = useCallback(async () => {
    // These are one-shot snapshots; WS remains the delta stream.
    try {
      const [acctRes, openRes, pendRes] = await Promise.all([
        apiRequest("GET", "/api/account/summary"),
        apiRequest("GET", "/api/trades/open"),
        apiRequest("GET", "/api/trades/pending"),
      ]);

      const [acct, openT, pendT] = await Promise.all([safeJson(acctRes), safeJson(openRes), safeJson(pendRes)]);
      if (acct) setAccountSummary(acct);
      if (Array.isArray(openT)) setOpenTrades(openT);
      if (Array.isArray(pendT)) setPendingOrders(pendT);
    } catch {
      // snapshot refresh is best-effort
    }
  }, []);

  const subscribeBaseTopics = useCallback(() => {
    if (baseTopicsSubscribedRef.current) return;
    baseTopicsSubscribedRef.current = true;

    // include global events + per-user streams; server should scope by token/session
    send({ op: "SUBSCRIBE", topics: ["global", "account", "positions", "orders"], includeSnapshot: true });
  }, [send]);

  const subscribeQuotes = useCallback(
    (symbols: string[]) => {
      const normalized = symbols.map((s) => s.toUpperCase().trim()).filter(Boolean);
      let changed = false;

      for (const s of normalized) {
        if (!subscribedQuoteSymbolsRef.current.has(s)) {
          subscribedQuoteSymbolsRef.current.add(s);
          changed = true;
        }
      }

      if (changed) {
        send({
          op: "SUBSCRIBE",
          topics: ["quotes"],
          symbols: Array.from(subscribedQuoteSymbolsRef.current),
          includeSnapshot: true,
        });
      }
    },
    [send]
  );

  const unsubscribeQuotes = useCallback(
    (symbols: string[]) => {
      const normalized = symbols.map((s) => s.toUpperCase().trim()).filter(Boolean);
      let changed = false;

      for (const s of normalized) {
        if (subscribedQuoteSymbolsRef.current.has(s)) {
          subscribedQuoteSymbolsRef.current.delete(s);
          changed = true;
        }
      }

      if (changed) {
        send({
          op: "SUBSCRIBE",
          topics: ["quotes"],
          symbols: Array.from(subscribedQuoteSymbolsRef.current),
          includeSnapshot: true,
        });
      }
    },
    [send]
  );

  const handleInbound = useCallback(
    (raw: any) => {
      const msg = raw as WsInbound;

      setLastEventAtMs(Date.now());

      // New protocol
      if (msg?.t === "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT" && Array.isArray((msg as any).symbols)) {
        setQuotesBySymbol((prev) => {
          const next = { ...prev };
          for (const q of (msg as any).symbols) {
            if (q?.symbol) next[String(q.symbol).toUpperCase()] = q;
          }
          return next;
        });
        return;
      }

      if (msg?.t === "QUOTE_TICK" && msg.symbol) {
        const sym = String(msg.symbol).toUpperCase();
        setQuotesBySymbol((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          [sym]: {
            ...(prev[sym] || {}),
            ...msg,
            symbol: sym,
          },
        }));
        return;
      }

      if (msg?.t === "ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT") {
        setAccountSummary((msg as any).summary ?? msg);
        return;
      }

      if (msg?.t === "POSITIONS_SNAPSHOT") {
        const anyMsg: any = msg;
        if (Array.isArray(anyMsg.openTrades)) setOpenTrades(anyMsg.openTrades);
        if (Array.isArray(anyMsg.pendingOrders)) setPendingOrders(anyMsg.pendingOrders);
        return;
      }

      if (msg?.t === "ORDER_LIFECYCLE") {
        const anyMsg: any = msg;
        if (anyMsg.correlationId) {
          setOrderLifecycleByCorrelationId((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            [anyMsg.correlationId]: {
              ...(prev[anyMsg.correlationId] || {}),
              ...anyMsg,
            },
          }));
        }
        return;
      }

      // Legacy compatibility (invalidate-by-event style)
      if (msg?.type === "quotes:updated") {
        // ask server for snapshot via REST once (best-effort)
        apiRequest("GET", "/api/quotes/latest")
          .then(safeJson)
          .then((arr) => {
            if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return;
            setQuotesBySymbol((prev) => {
              const next = { ...prev };
              for (const q of arr) {
                if (q?.symbol) next[String(q.symbol).toUpperCase()] = q;
              }
              return next;
            });
          })
          .catch(() => {});
        return;
      }

      if (msg?.type === "trades:updated") {
        // best-effort snapshots; WS should eventually replace this path entirely
        forceResyncSnapshots().catch(() => {});
        return;
      }
    },
    [forceResyncSnapshots]
  );

  const connect = useCallback(() => {
    clearReconnectTimer();

    try {
      const url = buildWsUrl();
      const ws = new WebSocket(url);
      wsRef.current = ws;

      ws.onopen = () => {
        setWsConnected(true);
        subscribeBaseTopics();
        forceResyncSnapshots().catch(() => {});
      };

      ws.onmessage = (evt) => {
        try {
          const data = JSON.parse(evt.data);
          handleInbound(data);
        } catch {
          // ignore malformed
        }
      };

      ws.onclose = () => {
        setWsConnected(false);
        // reconnect with backoff
        clearReconnectTimer();
        reconnectTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => connect(), 1500);
      };

      ws.onerror = () => {
        // allow onclose to handle reconnection
      };
    } catch {
      setWsConnected(false);
      clearReconnectTimer();
      reconnectTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => connect(), 2000);
    }
  }, [forceResyncSnapshots, handleInbound, subscribeBaseTopics]);

  // Connect once, keep alive
  useEffect(() => {
    connect();
    return () => {
      clearReconnectTimer();
      const ws = wsRef.current;
      wsRef.current = null;
      if (ws && (ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CONNECTING)) {
        ws.close();
      }
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  // If connected and we already had quote subs, re-assert them after reconnect.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!wsConnected) return;
    if (subscribedQuoteSymbolsRef.current.size > 0) {
      subscribeQuotes(Array.from(subscribedQuoteSymbolsRef.current));
    }
  }, [wsConnected, subscribeQuotes]);

  // Auto-subscribe to enabled symbols once at startup (API snapshot; WS is delta)
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await apiRequest("GET", "/api/config/symbols");
        const data = await safeJson(res);
        if (cancelled || !Array.isArray(data)) return;

        const enabled = data.filter((s: any) => s && s.symbol && s.enabled !== false).map((s: any) => String(s.symbol));
        subscribeQuotes(enabled);

        // also seed quotes snapshot if server still relies on REST
        const qRes = await apiRequest("GET", "/api/quotes/latest");
        const qData = await safeJson(qRes);
        if (cancelled || !Array.isArray(qData)) return;

        setQuotesBySymbol((prev) => {
          const next = { ...prev };
          for (const q of qData) {
            if (q?.symbol) next[String(q.symbol).toUpperCase()] = q;
          }
          return next;
        });
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    })();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [subscribeQuotes]);

  const value: LiveCtx = useMemo(
    () => ({
      wsConnected,
      lastEventAtMs,
      quotesBySymbol,
      accountSummary,
      openTrades,
      pendingOrders,
      orderLifecycleByCorrelationId,
      subscribeQuotes,
      unsubscribeQuotes,
      forceResyncSnapshots,
    }),
    [
      wsConnected,
      lastEventAtMs,
      quotesBySymbol,
      accountSummary,
      openTrades,
      pendingOrders,
      orderLifecycleByCorrelationId,
      subscribeQuotes,
      unsubscribeQuotes,
      forceResyncSnapshots,
    ]
  );

  return <Ctx.Provider value={value}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

export function useLiveUpdates() {
  const v = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!v) throw new Error("useLiveUpdates must be used inside LiveUpdatesProvider");
  return v;
}

Wrap routes with the LiveUpdatesProvider.

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Switch, Route, useLocation } from "wouter";
import { queryClient } from "./lib/queryClient";
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster";
import NotFound from "@/pages/not-found";
import Dashboard from "@/pages/Dashboard";
import LoginPage from "@/pages/LoginPage";
import AdminDashboard from "@/pages/AdminDashboard";
import JournalPage from "@/pages/JournalPage";
import ProfileSettings from "@/pages/ProfileSettings";
import VerifyEmail from "@/pages/VerifyEmail";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/use-auth";
import { AuthProvider } from "./hooks/use-auth";
import { VerificationReminderPopup } from "@/components/VerificationReminderPopup";
import { LiveUpdatesProvider } from "@/live/LiveUpdatesProvider";

function ImpersonationBanner() {
  const { user, stopImpersonating } = useAuth();
  const [stopping, setStopping] = useState(false);
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();

  if (!user?.isImpersonating) return null;

  const handleStop = async () => {
    setStopping(true);
    try {
      await stopImpersonating();
      navigate("/admin");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Failed to stop impersonation:", error);
    } finally {
      setStopping(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed top-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 bg-amber-500 text-black px-4 py-2 flex items-center justify-between shadow-lg">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className="font-semibold">View As Mode:</span>
        <span>You are viewing as {user.email} (ID: {user.id})</span>
      </div>
      <button
        onClick={handleStop}
        disabled={stopping}
        className="bg-black text-white px-4 py-1 rounded font-medium hover:bg-gray-800 disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        {stopping ? "Exiting..." : "Exit View As"}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function AppRoutes() {
  const { user, isAuthenticated, loading, checkAuth } = useAuth();
  const [location, navigate] = useLocation();

  useEffect(() => {
    checkAuth();
  }, [checkAuth]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !isAuthenticated && location !== "/login") {
      navigate("/login");
    }

    if (!loading && isAuthenticated && location === "/login") {
      navigate("/");
    }
  }, [isAuthenticated, loading, location, navigate]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-screen">
        <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-12 w-12 border-t-2 border-b-2 border-primary"></div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <ImpersonationBanner />
      <VerificationReminderPopup />
      <div className={user?.isImpersonating ? "pt-10" : ""}>
        <Switch>
          <Route path="/login" component={LoginPage} />
          <Route path="/verify-email" component={VerifyEmail} />
          <Route path="/admin" component={AdminDashboard} />
          <Route path="/journal" component={JournalPage} />
          <Route path="/profile" component={ProfileSettings} />
          <Route path="/" component={Dashboard} />
          <Route component={NotFound} />
        </Switch>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function App() {
  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <AuthProvider>
        <LiveUpdatesProvider>
          <AppRoutes />
          <Toaster />
        </LiveUpdatesProvider>
      </AuthProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

7) client/src/hooks/use-quotes.tsx (FULL REPLACEMENT)
No polling; uses WS-delivered quote map.

import { useMemo } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useLiveUpdates } from "@/live/LiveUpdatesProvider";

interface Quote {
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
  price: number;
  change: number;
  time: number;
  bid?: number;
  ask?: number;
  spread?: number;
  prevClose?: number;
  changePct?: number;
  percent_change?: number;
  isStale?: boolean;
  lastApiUpdate?: number;
  dataAge?: number;
}

interface SymbolConfig {
  id: number;
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
  enabled?: boolean;
}

const calculatePctChange = (current: number, previous: number): number => {
  if (!previous || previous === 0) return 0;
  return ((current - previous) / previous) * 100;
};

export function useQuotes() {
  const { quotesBySymbol, wsConnected } = useLiveUpdates();

  const { data: symbols = [] } = useQuery<SymbolConfig[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/config/symbols"],
  });

  const symbolNameMap = useMemo(() => {
    const m: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const s of symbols) {
      if (s?.symbol && s.enabled !== false) m[String(s.symbol).toUpperCase()] = s.name || s.symbol;
    }
    return m;
  }, [symbols]);

  const quotes: Quote[] = useMemo(() => {
    const out: Quote[] = [];
    for (const sym of Object.keys(symbolNameMap)) {
      const q = quotesBySymbol[sym];
      if (!q) continue;

      const bid = typeof q.bid === "number" ? q.bid : q.bid ? Number(q.bid) : undefined;
      const ask = typeof q.ask === "number" ? q.ask : q.ask ? Number(q.ask) : undefined;

      const priceRaw =
        typeof q.mid === "number"
          ? q.mid
          : typeof q.price === "number"
          ? q.price
          : ask ?? bid ?? 0;

      const price = Number.isFinite(priceRaw) ? priceRaw : 0;

      const prevCloseRaw = q.prevClose ?? q.prev_close;
      const prevClose =
        typeof prevCloseRaw === "number" ? prevCloseRaw : prevCloseRaw ? Number(prevCloseRaw) : undefined;

      const pct = prevClose ? calculatePctChange(price, prevClose) : (typeof q.pctChange === "number" ? q.pctChange : 0);

      out.push({
        symbol: sym,
        name: symbolNameMap[sym] || sym,
        price,
        change: typeof q.change === "number" ? q.change : Number(q.change ?? 0),
        time: q.ts ? Number(q.ts) : q.timestamp ? Number(q.timestamp) : Date.now(),
        bid,
        ask,
        spread:
          typeof q.spread === "number"
            ? q.spread
            : bid !== undefined && ask !== undefined
            ? Math.abs(ask - bid)
            : undefined,
        prevClose,
        changePct: pct,
        percent_change: pct,
        isStale: !!q.isStale,
        lastApiUpdate: q.lastApiUpdate,
        dataAge: q.dataAge ?? q.dataAgeMs,
      });
    }
    return out;
  }, [quotesBySymbol, symbolNameMap]);

  const hasStaleData = quotes.some((q) => q.isStale);

  return {
    quotes,
    isConnected: wsConnected,
    isLoading: symbols.length > 0 && quotes.length === 0,
    hasStaleData,
  };
}
8) client/src/hooks/use-trades.tsx (FULL REPLACEMENT)
Stops using WS “auth userId message”. Pulls open/pending from LiveUpdatesProvider, history from REST.

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { apiRequest } from "@/lib/queryClient";
import { useToast } from "./use-toast";
import { useAuth } from "./use-auth";
import { useLiveUpdates } from "@/live/LiveUpdatesProvider";

export function useTrades() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { openTrades, forceResyncSnapshots } = useLiveUpdates();

  // Trade history can stay REST (not realtime-critical); open trades are realtime via WS.
  const {
    data: trades = [],
    isLoading: isLoadingTrades,
    error: tradesError,
    refetch: refetchTrades,
  } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["/api/trades"],
    enabled: !!user,
  });

  const createTrade = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (data: {
      symbolId: number;
      type: "BUY" | "SELL";
      size: number;
      openPrice: number;
      takeProfit?: number;
      stopLoss?: number;
    }) => apiRequest("POST", "/api/trades", data),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      toast({ title: "Trade Created", description: "Your trade was successfully placed" });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades"] });
      await forceResyncSnapshots();
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      toast({
        title: "Error",
        description: error.message || "Failed to place trade",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    },
  });

  const closeTrade = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id }: { id: number }) => apiRequest("POST", `/api/trades/${id}/close`, {}),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      toast({ title: "Trade Closed", description: "Your trade was successfully closed" });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades"] });
      await forceResyncSnapshots();
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      toast({
        title: "Error",
        description: error.message || "Failed to close trade",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    },
  });

  return {
    trades,
    openTrades: openTrades || [],
    isLoadingTrades,
    isLoadingOpenTrades: false,
    tradesError,
    openTradesError: null,
    refetchTrades,
    refetchOpenTrades: () => forceResyncSnapshots(),
    createTrade,
    closeTrade,
  };
}
9) client/src/hooks/usePendingOrders.ts (FULL REPLACEMENT)
Pending orders are realtime now.

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { useLiveUpdates } from "@/live/LiveUpdatesProvider";

export const usePendingOrders = () => {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { pendingOrders, forceResyncSnapshots } = useLiveUpdates();

  const cancelOrder = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (tradeId: number) => {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/trades/${tradeId}/cancel`, {
        method: "PATCH",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        credentials: "include",
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(await response.text());
      }

      return response.json();
    },
    onSuccess: async () => {
      toast({ title: "Success", description: "Pending order canceled successfully" });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/trades"] });
      await forceResyncSnapshots();
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      toast({
        title: "Error",
        description: error.message || "Failed to cancel order",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    },
  });

  return {
    pendingOrders: pendingOrders || [],
    isLoading: false,
    error: null,
    refetch: () => forceResyncSnapshots(),
    cancelOrder,
  };
};
10) client/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx (MINIMAL FULL REPLACEMENT)
Uses WS-fed account summary for balance display.

import { useState } from "react";
import { Header } from "@/components/Header";
import { MobileNavigation, SideNavigation } from "@/components/Navigation";
import QuotesScreen from "./QuotesScreen";
import ChartScreen from "./ChartScreen";
import TradeScreen from "./TradeScreen";
import HistoryScreen from "./HistoryScreen";
import LeaderboardScreen from "./LeaderboardScreen";
import AccountScreen from "./AccountScreen";
import { useQuotes } from "@/hooks/use-quotes";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/use-auth";
import axios from "axios";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { useAccountSummary } from "@/hooks/use-account-summary";

export default function Dashboard() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("quotes");
  const [selectedSymbol, setSelectedSymbol] = useState("USDJPY");
  const { quotes } = useQuotes();
  const { user, checkAuth } = useAuth();
  const { summary } = useAccountSummary();
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const [adminPromotion, setAdminPromotion] = useState({
    isPromoting: false,
    success: false,
    error: "",
  });

  const promoteToAdmin = async () => {
    if (!user) return;

    setAdminPromotion({ isPromoting: true, success: false, error: "" });

    try {
      await axios.post("/api/promote-to-admin", { userId: user.id });
      setAdminPromotion({ isPromoting: false, success: true, error: "" });
      await checkAuth();
    } catch {
      setAdminPromotion({ isPromoting: false, success: false, error: "Failed to promote to admin" });
    }
  };

  const currentQuote = quotes?.find((quote) => quote.symbol === selectedSymbol);

  const handleSelectSymbol = (symbol: string) => {
    setSelectedSymbol(symbol);
    if (activeTab !== "chart" && activeTab !== "trade") {
      setActiveTab("chart");
    }
  };

  const displayedBalance =
    typeof summary?.balance === "number"
      ? summary.balance.toFixed(2)
      : user?.balance
      ? Number(user.balance).toFixed(2)
      : "0.00";

  return (
    <div className="h-screen flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
      <Header />

      {user && (
        <div className="bg-neutral-850 px-4 py-2 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-gray-800 shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            {user.isAdmin ? (
              <Link href="/admin">
                <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="bg-blue-900 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white border-blue-700">
                  Admin Dashboard
                </Button>
              </Link>
            ) : (
              <Button
                size="sm"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={promoteToAdmin}
                disabled={adminPromotion.isPromoting}
                className="bg-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-600 text-gray-300"
              >
                {adminPromotion.isPromoting ? "Processing..." : "Enable Admin Mode"}
              </Button>
            )}

            {adminPromotion.success && <span className="text-green-500 text-sm">Admin access granted! You can now visit the Admin Dashboard.</span>}
            {adminPromotion.error && <span className="text-red-500 text-sm">{adminPromotion.error}</span>}
          </div>

          <div className="text-sm text-gray-400">
            Balance: <span className="font-semibold text-white">${displayedBalance}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <main className="flex-1 min-h-0 flex flex-col md:flex-row overflow-hidden">
        <MobileNavigation activeTab={activeTab} setActiveTab={setActiveTab} />
        <SideNavigation activeTab={activeTab} setActiveTab={setActiveTab} />

        <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
          {activeTab === "quotes" && <QuotesScreen onSelectSymbol={handleSelectSymbol} />}

          {activeTab === "chart" && <ChartScreen selectedSymbol={selectedSymbol} />}

          {activeTab === "trade" && <TradeScreen selectedSymbol={selectedSymbol} currentPrice={currentQuote?.price} />}

          {activeTab === "history" && <HistoryScreen />}

          {activeTab === "leaderboard" && <LeaderboardScreen />}

          {activeTab === "account" && <AccountScreen />}
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

2) Add: server/realtime/liveHub.ts
This file:

Authenticates WS via token query param (if present) OR session cookie (via passed sessionParser)

Supports op:"SUBSCRIBE" with topics, symbols, includeSnapshot

Sends immediate snapshots for account, positions, quotes (quotes optional)

// server/realtime/liveHub.ts
import type { Server as HttpServer } from "http";
import { WebSocketServer } from "ws";
import type WebSocket from "ws";
import crypto from "crypto";
import BetterSQLite3 from "better-sqlite3";
import { storage } from "../storage";

// Topic names used by client SUBSCRIBE
type Topic = "global" | "quotes" | "account" | "positions" | "orders" | "audit";

type ClientState = {
  ws: WebSocket;
  userId: number | null;
  topics: Set<Topic>;
  quoteSymbols: Set<string>;
  connectedAt: number;
  lastSeenAt: number;
};

export type LiveHub = {
  publishToUser: (userId: number, event: any) => void;
  publishGlobal: (event: any) => void;
  publishQuoteTick: (symbol: string, tick: any) => void;
  pushAccountSnapshot: (userId: number) => Promise<void>;
  pushPositionsSnapshot: (userId: number) => Promise<void>;
  close: () => void;
};

function safeJsonParse(s: string): any | null {
  try { return JSON.parse(s); } catch { return null; }
}

function nowMs() {
  return Date.now();
}

function sha256Hex(input: string) {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(input).digest("hex");
}

/**
 * Token verification is self-contained to avoid tight coupling.
 * Expected DB table (created earlier in your token-auth part):
 *   auth_tokens(user_id INTEGER, token_hash TEXT, revoked_at INTEGER NULL, expires_at INTEGER NULL, last_used_at INTEGER NULL, ...)
 *
 * Pepper: AUTH_TOKEN_PEPPER or SESSION_SECRET.
 */
function resolveUserIdFromToken(rawToken: string | null | undefined): number | null {
  if (!rawToken) return null;

  // Accept "Bearer x" if someone passed it that way
  const token = rawToken.startsWith("Bearer ") ? rawToken.slice("Bearer ".length).trim() : rawToken.trim();
  if (!token) return null;

  const pepper = process.env.AUTH_TOKEN_PEPPER || process.env.SESSION_SECRET || "";
  const tokenHash = sha256Hex(`${token}:${pepper}`);

  const db = new BetterSQLite3("./trading_app.db");
  try {
    const tableExists = db
      .prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='auth_tokens'")
      .get();

    if (!tableExists) return null;

    const row = db.prepare(`
      SELECT user_id as userId
      FROM auth_tokens
      WHERE token_hash = ?
        AND (revoked_at IS NULL OR revoked_at = 0)
        AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at = 0 OR expires_at > ?)
      LIMIT 1
    `).get(tokenHash, nowMs());

    if (!row?.userId) return null;

    // best-effort last_used_at update
    try {
      db.prepare(`UPDATE auth_tokens SET last_used_at = ? WHERE token_hash = ?`).run(nowMs(), tokenHash);
    } catch { /* ignore */ }

    return Number(row.userId);
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

async function buildAccountSnapshot(userId: number) {
  const u = await storage.getUserById(userId);
  if (!u) return null;

  const balance = Number(u.balance);
  const usedMargin = Number(u.usedMargin ?? 0);
  const equity = Number(u.equity ?? 0);
  const freeMargin = Number(u.freeMargin ?? 0);
  const leverage = Number(u.leverage ?? 0);

  const marginLevel = usedMargin > 0 ? (equity / usedMargin) * 100 : null;

  return {
    t: "ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT",
    userId,
    balance,
    equity,
    usedMargin,
    freeMargin,
    leverage,
    marginLevel,
    ts: nowMs(),
  };
}

async function buildPositionsSnapshot(userId: number) {
  const openTrades = await storage.getOpenTradesByUserId(userId);
  const pendingTrades = await storage.getPendingTradesByUserId(userId);

  return {
    t: "POSITIONS_SNAPSHOT",
    userId,
    openTrades,
    pendingTrades,
    ts: nowMs(),
  };
}

function buildQuotesSnapshot(symbols: string[]) {
  const db = new BetterSQLite3("./trading_app.db");
  try {
    const tableExists = db
      .prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='quotes'")
      .get();
    if (!tableExists) return { t: "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT", quotes: [], symbols, ts: nowMs() };

    // Cap for safety (avoid accidental subscribe-all exploding payloads)
    const capped = symbols.slice(0, 200).map(s => s.toUpperCase());
    if (capped.length === 0) {
      return { t: "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT", quotes: [], symbols: [], ts: nowMs() };
    }

    const placeholders = capped.map(() => "?").join(",");
    const rows = db.prepare(`
      SELECT
        q.symbol,
        q.bid,
        q.ask,
        (q.bid + q.ask) / 2 AS price,
        COALESCE(q.is_stale, 0) AS isStale,
        COALESCE(q.last_api_update, 0) AS lastApiUpdate,
        rp.price AS prevClose
      FROM quotes q
      LEFT JOIN reference_prices rp ON rp.symbol = q.symbol
      WHERE q.symbol IN (${placeholders})
    `).all(...capped);

    const now = nowMs();
    const STALE_THRESHOLD_MS = 10_000;

    const quotes = rows.map((r: any) => {
      const bid = r.bid ?? null;
      const ask = r.ask ?? null;
      const mid = (bid != null && ask != null) ? (bid + ask) / 2 : (r.price ?? null);
      const spread = (bid != null && ask != null) ? Math.abs(ask - bid) : null;

      let pctChange = 0;
      if (r.prevClose && r.prevClose > 0 && mid != null) {
        pctChange = ((mid - r.prevClose) / r.prevClose) * 100;
        pctChange = Math.round(pctChange * 100) / 100;
      }

      const lastUpdate = Number(r.lastApiUpdate || 0) || now;
      const ageMs = now - lastUpdate;
      const dbIsStale = r.isStale === 1 || r.isStale === true;
      const isStale = dbIsStale || ageMs > STALE_THRESHOLD_MS;

      return {
        symbol: String(r.symbol),
        bid,
        ask,
        mid,
        spread,
        pctChange,
        isStale,
        lastApiUpdate: lastUpdate,
        dataAge: ageMs,
        ts: now,
      };
    });

    // Send BOTH keys to be compatible with older/newer clients
    return { t: "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT", quotes, symbols: capped, ts: now };
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

function topicForEvent(evt: any): Topic | null {
  const t = evt?.t;
  if (!t) return null;
  if (t === "QUOTE_TICK" || t === "QUOTE_SNAPSHOT") return "quotes";
  if (t === "ACCOUNT_SNAPSHOT") return "account";
  if (t === "POSITIONS_SNAPSHOT") return "positions";
  if (t === "ORDER_EVENT") return "orders";
  if (t === "AUDIT_EVENT") return "audit";
  if (t === "GLOBAL_EVENT") return "global";
  return null;
}

export function createLiveHub(opts: { httpServer: HttpServer; sessionParser: any }): LiveHub {
  const { httpServer, sessionParser } = opts;

  const wss = new WebSocketServer({ server: httpServer, path: "/ws" });
  const clients = new Set<ClientState>();

  function send(ws: WebSocket, obj: any) {
    try { ws.send(JSON.stringify(obj)); } catch { /* ignore */ }
  }

  function matchesQuoteSub(c: ClientState, symbol: string) {
    if (!c.topics.has("quotes")) return false;
    if (c.quoteSymbols.size === 0) return true; // subscribe-all-quotes behavior
    return c.quoteSymbols.has(symbol.toUpperCase());
  }

  async function handleSubscribe(c: ClientState, msg: any) {
    const topicsIn: string[] = Array.isArray(msg.topics) ? msg.topics : [];
    const includeSnapshot = !!msg.includeSnapshot;

    // normalize topic names
    for (const raw of topicsIn) {
      const t = String(raw).toLowerCase();
      if (t === "global") c.topics.add("global");
      if (t === "quotes") c.topics.add("quotes");
      if (t === "account") c.topics.add("account");
      if (t === "positions") c.topics.add("positions");
      if (t === "orders") c.topics.add("orders");
      if (t === "audit") c.topics.add("audit");
    }

    // symbols for quotes
    if (Array.isArray(msg.symbols)) {
      c.quoteSymbols = new Set(msg.symbols.map((s: any) => String(s).toUpperCase()));
    }

    if (!includeSnapshot) return;

    // snapshots: only send if subscribed
    if (c.topics.has("quotes")) {
      const syms = Array.from(c.quoteSymbols);
      const snap = buildQuotesSnapshot(syms);
      send(c.ws, snap);
    }

    if (c.userId && c.topics.has("account")) {
      const snap = await buildAccountSnapshot(c.userId);
      if (snap) send(c.ws, snap);
    }

    if (c.userId && c.topics.has("positions")) {
      const snap = await buildPositionsSnapshot(c.userId);
      send(c.ws, snap);
    }
  }

  wss.on("connection", (ws: WebSocket, req: any) => {
    const url = new URL(req.url || "/ws", "http://localhost");
    const token = url.searchParams.get("token") || url.searchParams.get("authToken");

    // Token auth first (mobile)
    let userId: number | null = resolveUserIdFromToken(token);

    // Session auth fallback (web)
    const resShim = {
      getHeader: () => undefined,
      setHeader: () => {},
    };

    sessionParser(req, resShim, () => {
      try {
        if (!userId && req?.session?.userId) userId = Number(req.session.userId);
      } catch { /* ignore */ }

      const c: ClientState = {
        ws,
        userId: userId ?? null,
        topics: new Set<Topic>(),
        quoteSymbols: new Set<string>(),
        connectedAt: nowMs(),
        lastSeenAt: nowMs(),
      };

      clients.add(c);

      send(ws, {
        t: "HELLO",
        userId: c.userId,
        sessionAuth: !!req?.session?.userId,
        tokenAuth: !!userId,
        serverTime: nowMs(),
      });

      ws.on("message", async (data: any) => {
        c.lastSeenAt = nowMs();
        const parsed = safeJsonParse(String(data || ""));
        if (!parsed) return;

        // Accept both new and legacy shapes
        const op = (parsed.op || parsed.type || "").toString().toUpperCase();

        if (op === "PING") {
          send(ws, { t: "PONG", ts: nowMs() });
          return;
        }

        if (op === "SUBSCRIBE") {
          // Do not allow user-bound topics if unauthenticated
          if (!c.userId) {
            // allow only global + quotes
            const requested = Array.isArray(parsed.topics) ? parsed.topics.map((x: any) => String(x).toLowerCase()) : [];
            const illegal = requested.some((t: string) => ["account", "positions", "orders", "audit"].includes(t));
            if (illegal) {
              send(ws, { t: "ERROR", code: "AUTH_REQUIRED", message: "Authenticate (session or token) to subscribe to user topics." });
              return;
            }
          }
          await handleSubscribe(c, parsed);
          return;
        }

        if (op === "UNSUBSCRIBE") {
          const topicsIn: string[] = Array.isArray(parsed.topics) ? parsed.topics : [];
          for (const raw of topicsIn) {
            const t = String(raw).toLowerCase() as Topic;
            c.topics.delete(t);
          }
          if (parsed.symbols === null) c.quoteSymbols.clear();
          return;
        }
      });

      ws.on("close", () => {
        clients.delete(c);
      });

      ws.on("error", () => {
        clients.delete(c);
      });
    });
  });

  function publishGlobal(event: any) {
    const evt = { ...event };
    for (const c of clients) {
      if (!c.topics.has("global")) continue;
      send(c.ws, evt);
    }
  }

  function publishToUser(uid: number, event: any) {
    const evt = { ...event };
    const topic = topicForEvent(evt);

    for (const c of clients) {
      if (c.userId !== uid) continue;

      // If we can infer a topic, only deliver when subscribed.
      if (topic && !c.topics.has(topic)) continue;

      send(c.ws, evt);
    }
  }

  function publishQuoteTick(symbol: string, tick: any) {
    const sym = symbol.toUpperCase();
    const evt = { t: "QUOTE_TICK", symbol: sym, ...tick, ts: tick?.ts ?? nowMs() };

    for (const c of clients) {
      if (!matchesQuoteSub(c, sym)) continue;
      send(c.ws, evt);
    }
  }

  async function pushAccountSnapshot(userId: number) {
    const snap = await buildAccountSnapshot(userId);
    if (!snap) return;
    publishToUser(userId, snap);
  }

  async function pushPositionsSnapshot(userId: number) {
    const snap = await buildPositionsSnapshot(userId);
    publishToUser(userId, snap);
  }

  function close() {
    try { wss.close(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
  }

  return {
    publishToUser,
    publishGlobal,
    publishQuoteTick,
    pushAccountSnapshot,
    pushPositionsSnapshot,
    close,
  };
}

Part 18 — Remove loopback HTTP self-calls and use a shared in-process quote truth source
This part removes the internal server→server REST call (axios.get("http://localhost:5000/api/quotes/latest")) from trade creation, and replaces it with a shared in-process quote access layer so your engine + REST routes share the same quote truth source (the quotes table), without any loopback HTTP.

1) Full file replacement: server/services/quoteService.ts
Copy/paste this whole file:

/**
 * Quote Service - Server-authoritative quote retrieval for trade execution
 * Replaces client-supplied prices with server-side quotes
 */

import Database from "better-sqlite3";
import { isMarketOpenForSymbol } from "./marketHours";

// Quote freshness: 5 minutes default to accommodate 1Forge refresh intervals
const STALE_AFTER_MS = Number(process.env.QUOTE_STALE_AFTER_MS ?? 300_000);

function normalizeSymbol(s: string): string {
  return s.replace("/", "").trim().toUpperCase();
}

function toNum(v: any): number | null {
  if (v === null || v === undefined) return null;
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
}

export type ExecutionQuote = {
  symbol: string;
  bid: number;
  ask: number;
  mid: number;
  spread: number;
  quoteTs: Date;
  source: string;
  isStale: boolean;
  marketOpen: boolean;
  execPrice: number;
};

export type QuoteFreshnessOptions = {
  /** Override the default staleness threshold (ms). */
  staleAfterMs?: number;
};

// Get a fresh database connection for each query to avoid locking issues
function getDb() {
  return new Database("./trading_app.db");
}

export function getLatestQuoteRow(symbol: string): any | null {
  const sym = normalizeSymbol(symbol);
  const sqlite = getDb();
  try {
    const row = sqlite
      .prepare(
        `SELECT symbol, price, bid, ask, is_stale, last_api_update, updated_at FROM quotes WHERE symbol = ?`
      )
      .get(sym) as any;
    return row || null;
  } finally {
    sqlite.close();
  }
}

export function getExecutionQuote(
  symbol: string,
  side: "BUY" | "SELL",
  action: "OPEN" | "CLOSE",
  opts?: QuoteFreshnessOptions
): ExecutionQuote {
  const sym = normalizeSymbol(symbol);
  const row = getLatestQuoteRow(sym);
  if (!row) throw new Error(`QUOTE_NOT_FOUND:${sym}`);

  const bid = toNum(row.bid);
  const ask = toNum(row.ask);
  const px = toNum(row.price);

  const usableBid = bid ?? px;
  const usableAsk = ask ?? px;
  if (usableBid === null || usableAsk === null) throw new Error(`QUOTE_INVALID:${sym}`);

  const mid = (usableBid + usableAsk) / 2;
  const spread = usableAsk - usableBid;

  // Handle both timestamp formats (seconds or milliseconds)
  const lastApiRaw = toNum(row.last_api_update) ?? toNum(row.updated_at);
  const lastApiMs =
    lastApiRaw === null ? null : lastApiRaw < 1e12 ? lastApiRaw * 1000 : lastApiRaw;

  const now = Date.now();
  const staleAfterMs = Number.isFinite(Number(opts?.staleAfterMs))
    ? Number(opts!.staleAfterMs)
    : STALE_AFTER_MS;

  const isStale =
    Number(row.is_stale ?? 0) === 1 || lastApiMs === null || now - lastApiMs > staleAfterMs;

  const marketOpen = isMarketOpenForSymbol(sym, new Date());

  // Institutional: BUY opens at ask, closes at bid. SELL opens at bid, closes at ask.
  const execPrice =
    action === "OPEN"
      ? side === "BUY"
        ? usableAsk
        : usableBid
      : side === "BUY"
      ? usableBid
      : usableAsk;

  const quoteTs = new Date(lastApiMs ?? now);

  return {
    symbol: sym,
    bid: usableBid,
    ask: usableAsk,
    mid,
    spread,
    quoteTs,
    source: process.env.QUOTE_SOURCE ?? (process.env.FORGE_KEY ? "1forge" : "quotes_db"),
    isStale,
    marketOpen,
    execPrice,
  };
}

// FX conversion (quoteCurrency -> USD) using available pairs in quotes table
export function getConversionRate(fromCcy: string, toCcy: string): number {
  const from = fromCcy.toUpperCase();
  const to = toCcy.toUpperCase();
  if (from === to) return 1;

  const direct = getLatestQuoteRow(from + to);
  if (direct) {
    const b = toNum(direct.bid) ?? toNum(direct.price);
    const a = toNum(direct.ask) ?? toNum(direct.price);
    if (b !== null && a !== null) return (a + b) / 2;
  }

  const inverse = getLatestQuoteRow(to + from);
  if (inverse) {
    const b = toNum(inverse.bid) ?? toNum(inverse.price);
    const a = toNum(inverse.ask) ?? toNum(inverse.price);
    if (b !== null && a !== null) return 1 / ((a + b) / 2);
  }

  // Bridge via USD
  if (from !== "USD" && to !== "USD") {
    return getConversionRate(from, "USD") * getConversionRate("USD", to);
  }

  // Fallback rates for common currencies if quote not found
  const fallbackRates: Record<string, number> = {
    JPY: 0.0067, // 1 JPY = ~0.0067 USD
    EUR: 1.09, // 1 EUR = ~1.09 USD
    GBP: 1.27, // 1 GBP = ~1.27 USD
    AUD: 0.65, // 1 AUD = ~0.65 USD
    USD: 1.0,
  };

  if (to === "USD" && fallbackRates[from]) return fallbackRates[from];
  if (from === "USD" && fallbackRates[to]) return 1 / fallbackRates[to];

  throw new Error(`FX_CONVERSION_MISSING:${from}->${to}`);
}

// IMPORTANT: Never self-call our own REST APIs from inside the server.
// Use in-process quote truth so the engine and routes share a single source.
// Match freshness logic to systemConfig.staleThresholdMs (same as risk middleware).
const sc = await db.query.systemConfig.findFirst({
  where: eq(systemConfig.id, 1),
});
const staleThresholdMs = Number(sc?.staleThresholdMs ?? 30000);
const blockOpenOnStaleQuotes = Boolean(sc?.blockOpenOnStaleQuotes ?? true);

let execQ: any;
try {
  execQ = getExecutionQuote(symbolConfig.symbol, data.type, "OPEN", {
    staleAfterMs: staleThresholdMs,
  });
} catch (e) {
  // No quote available: audit as received + reject deterministically.
  const errMsg = String((e as any)?.message ?? e);
  const rpRaw = req.body.limitPrice ?? req.body.stopPrice;
  const rpParsed = rpRaw != null ? parseFloat(String(rpRaw)) : null;
  const safeRequestedPrice =
    rpParsed !== null && Number.isFinite(rpParsed) ? rpParsed : null;

  try {
    await writeOrderIntentAudit({
      correlationId,
      eventCode: "ORDER_RECEIVED",
      ctx: auditCtx,
      userId: req.session.userId,
      symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
      side: data.type,
      orderType: orderType ?? "Market",
      qtyLots: orderSize,
      requestedPrice: safeRequestedPrice,
      limitPrice: req.body.limitPrice ? parseFloat(String(req.body.limitPrice)) : null,
      stopPrice: req.body.stopPrice ? parseFloat(String(req.body.stopPrice)) : null,
      takeProfit: req.body.takeProfit ? parseFloat(String(req.body.takeProfit)) : null,
      stopLoss: req.body.stopLoss ? parseFloat(String(req.body.stopLoss)) : null,
      quoteBid: null,
      quoteAsk: null,
      quoteMid: null,
      quoteTs: null,
      quoteIsStale: true,
      payload: { rawBody: req.body, receivedAtMs, quoteError: errMsg },
    });
  } catch {}

  try {
    await writeOrderIntentAudit({
      correlationId,
      eventCode: "DECISION",
      ctx: auditCtx,
      userId: req.session.userId,
      decision: "REJECT",
      symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
      side: data.type,
      orderType: orderType ?? "Market",
      qtyLots: orderSize,
      requestedPrice: safeRequestedPrice,
      quoteBid: null,
      quoteAsk: null,
      quoteMid: null,
      quoteTs: null,
      quoteIsStale: true,
      payload: {
        rejectReason: "QUOTE_NOT_AVAILABLE",
        quoteError: errMsg,
        latencyMs: Date.now() - receivedAtMs,
      },
    });
  } catch {}

  return res.status(404).json({
    message: "Symbol price not available",
    symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
  });
}

// Keep the existing `quote` shape used throughout this handler.
const quote = {
  symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
  price: execQ.mid,
  bid: execQ.bid,
  ask: execQ.ask,
  mid: execQ.mid,
  spread: execQ.spread,
  isStale: execQ.isStale,
  timestamp: execQ.quoteTs.getTime(),
  source: execQ.source,
};

// AUDIT: Write ORDER_RECEIVED event immediately after we have quote context
try {
  await writeOrderIntentAudit({
    correlationId,
    eventCode: "ORDER_RECEIVED",
    ctx: auditCtx,
    userId: req.session.userId,
    symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
    side: data.type,
    orderType: orderType ?? "Market",
    qtyLots: orderSize,
    requestedPrice: parseFloat(String(req.body.limitPrice ?? req.body.stopPrice ?? quote.price)),
    limitPrice: req.body.limitPrice ? parseFloat(String(req.body.limitPrice)) : null,
    stopPrice: req.body.stopPrice ? parseFloat(String(req.body.stopPrice)) : null,
    takeProfit: req.body.takeProfit ? parseFloat(String(req.body.takeProfit)) : null,
    stopLoss: req.body.stopLoss ? parseFloat(String(req.body.stopLoss)) : null,
    quoteBid: quote.bid ? parseFloat(String(quote.bid)) : null,
    quoteAsk: quote.ask ? parseFloat(String(quote.ask)) : null,
    quoteMid: quote.mid
      ? parseFloat(String(quote.mid))
      : quote.price
      ? parseFloat(String(quote.price))
      : null,
    quoteTs: quote.timestamp ? new Date(quote.timestamp) : null,
    quoteIsStale: quote.isStale ?? false,
    payload: { rawBody: req.body, receivedAtMs },
  });
} catch (auditErr) {
  console.error("Error writing ORDER_RECEIVED audit:", auditErr);
}

// OPTION 1: Block trade open on stale quote (only if systemConfig enables it)
if (blockOpenOnStaleQuotes && quote.isStale === true) {
  // AUDIT: Write DECISION REJECT for stale quote
  try {
    await writeOrderIntentAudit({
      correlationId,
      eventCode: "DECISION",
      ctx: auditCtx,
      userId: req.session.userId,
      decision: "REJECT",
      symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
      side: data.type,
      orderType: orderType ?? "Market",
      qtyLots: orderSize,
      requestedPrice: parseFloat(String(req.body.limitPrice ?? req.body.stopPrice ?? quote.price)),
      quoteBid: quote.bid ? parseFloat(String(quote.bid)) : null,
      quoteAsk: quote.ask ? parseFloat(String(quote.ask)) : null,
      quoteMid: quote.mid
        ? parseFloat(String(quote.mid))
        : quote.price
        ? parseFloat(String(quote.price))
        : null,
      quoteTs: quote.timestamp ? new Date(quote.timestamp) : null,
      quoteIsStale: true,
      riskLimit: {},
      riskObserved: {},
      payload: {
        rejectReason: "STALE_QUOTE",
        staleThresholdMs,
        latencyMs: Date.now() - receivedAtMs,
      },
    });
  } catch (auditErr) {
    console.error("Error writing DECISION REJECT audit:", auditErr);
  }

  return res.status(503).json({
    message: "Quote is stale. Cannot open trade until fresh quotes are available.",
    symbol: symbolConfig.symbol,
    isStale: true,
    staleThresholdMs,
  });
}
Resulting behavior (what you gain immediately)
No more loopback HTTP (axios.get("http://localhost:5000/...")) inside server execution paths.

Trade placement reads quotes via in-process DB access (getExecutionQuote()), which is the correct “platform truth” boundary for native/mobile hardening.

Quote freshness is aligned with:

systemConfig.staleThresholdMs

systemConfig.blockOpenOnStaleQuotes

If quotes are missing, you still get ORDER_RECEIVED + DECISION(REJECT) audit entries (deterministic, no silent failures).

If you confirm Part 18 is merged, the next logical step is to apply the same “no loopback + in-process truth” rule to any other server-internal HTTP calls that may exist in later branches (e.g., admin analytics endpoints, audit exports, etc.), and then tighten quote truth further by optionally adding a small in-memory read-through cache keyed by symbol (still backed by the quotes table as canonical).
